--- gridfall/NOTES.md ---
# Gridfall
Touch: drag a piece from the 3-slot hand onto the 8x8 board (piece rides above your finger, ghost shows the snap + which lines would pop). Keys: arrows move, 1/2/3 pick a slot, Tab cycles, Space drops, R restarts.
Loop: place pieces, complete any full row or column to clear it; consecutive clearing placements build a STREAK multiplier, a full wipe pays +300. Run ends when nothing in the hand fits.
Modes: MARATHON (endless) and DAILY (date-seeded board + piece stream, first finish of the day is the logged score). Bests persist per mode; last 10 runs show on the game-over card.
Better than the leader: the NEXT hand is always visible, and hands are validated before dealing so you never get a dead hand.
No build step, no network, no cookies — open index.html.
--- scrapper-squad/NOTES.md ---
Controls: left stick/WASD move; right stick/arrows aim and release/fire; SUPER/Space triggers a charged super.
Loop: win GEM HOARD by holding 10 gems for 15 seconds, or crack the enemy safe in HEIST before 2:30.
Every win adds trophies; trophy-road unlocks are permanent and all power is flat.
--- fallline/NOTES.md ---
Controls: left stick/WASD move; FIRE/Space auto-aims nearest threat; E/INTERACT loots; R reloads.
Loop: drop, loot, survive four shrinking storm phases, and be last alive in a four-minute run.
--- ridgeline-rumble/NOTES.md ---
Move: left stick or WASD. Abilities: right buttons or J/K/L; basic hits auto-fire.
Pick a free hero, clear waves, last-hit for gold, and tap shop cards for upgrades.
Push the lane and break the enemy far tower before the 4:00 clock expires.
Win streaks raise bot heat; hero mastery and wins/losses persist on this device.
--- frosthold/NOTES.md ---
# Frosthold

Controls: tap a slot then BUILD, drag camp badges to HUNT/CHOP/MEND or GUARD, use furnace −/+, keys 1–6 select buildings, Space builds, H/C/M/G assign.
Loop: build instantly during calm, tune coal through each whiteout, repel raider probes, and keep at least one survivor alive through cycle 10.
--- towerline-duel/NOTES.md ---
Drag a card onto a lane; tap a lane to deploy the selected card.
Keyboard: 1–4 picks a hand card, ←/→ picks a lane, Space/Enter deploys.
Units march, clash, and hit the opponent core; spells land where dropped.
Win the current rung to unlock its three cards; losing lets you retry.
Deck Forge makes an eight-card deck; progress is saved on this device.
--- starweft/NOTES.md ---
Tap an enemy to target; tap a hero to select; chips are basic / skill / ultimate.
Tap Ember, Tide, or Bloom to inspect that element; arrows select, 1-3 act, Enter continues.
Build SP with basics, spend SP on skills, charge ultimates, and break every weakness.
--- skyshard-vale/NOTES.md ---
# Skyshard Vale
Move: left stick or WASD. Attack: ATTACK or J; Skill: SKILL or K; swap with party chips or 1–3.
Explore meadow, lake, ruin, and peak; defeat shrine guardians to earn Dash, Lift, and Glide.
Wet + Spark = Chain Shock; Frost + Strike = Shatter; Ember + Wet = Steam Heal. Chests persist.
Defeat returns you to your last shrine; defeat the high shard boss to clear the valley.
--- bastionworks/NOTES.md ---
Tap kits to build, tap structures to upgrade, drag to move; B/L/D switch base, ladder, log.
Tap an open ladder rival, then tap the raid rim to deploy; 1–5 select troops, arrows move, Space deploys.
Everything builds instantly from earned ✦ gold and ◆ mist; clear all 12 rivals to earn the ladder crown.
Raid outcomes, base layout, loot, trophies, and the between-session defense log persist on this device.
--- fizzlift/NOTES.md ---
# Fizzlift

Controls: swipe (or tap two neighbours) to swap; arrows move the cursor, Enter selects then arrows swap; R restart, M mute, Esc deselect.
Loop: below the glowing fizz line pieces FLOAT up, above it they FALL down — matches cascade in both directions at once.
Goal: float every bottle cap to the fizz surface and crack all valve seals inside the move budget; broken seals raise the fizz line permanently, and the line also waves mid-level.
20 levels with stars plus Endless Fizz (each surfaced cap pays back 2 moves). No lives, no gates, instant retry; stars/best saved locally.

    38	  function resize() {
    39	    var r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();

    41	    var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    42	    var sc = Math.min(dpr, 960 / Math.max(W, H));
    43	    var bw = Math.max(1, Math.round(W * sc)), bh = Math.max(1, Math.round(H * sc));

    59	  function checkOrient() {

    71	  function start(mode) {
    72	    U.clearTimers();

    75	    S.mode = (mode === 'daily') ? 'daily' : 'marathon';

    82	      G.seedBoard(S.board, S.rng);
    83	    } else {
    84	      S.dailyKey = '';
    85	      S.rng = U.makeRng((Date.now() ^ (Math.random() * 0xffffffff)) >>> 0);
    86	    }
    87	    S.hand = G.dealHand(S.board, S.rng);
    88	    S.next = G.rollHand(S.rng);

    91	    $('tabD').className = S.mode === 'daily' ? 'tab on' : 'tab';

    93	  }

    95	  function syncHud() {
    96	    elScore.textContent = String(S.score);
    97	    elBest.textContent = String(Math.max(S.best, S.score));
    98	    elStreak.textContent = String(S.streak);

   101	  function handEmpty() { return !S.hand[0] && !S.hand[1] && !S.hand[2]; }
   102	
   103	  function tryPlace(piece, slot, cx, cy) {
   104	    if (S.over || S.paused) return false;
   105	    if (!piece || !G.canPlace(S.board, piece, cx, cy)) return false;
   106	    var cells = G.place(S.board, piece, cx, cy);
   107	    S.hand[slot] = null;
   108	    S.placed++;
   109	    for (var i = 0; i < cells.length; i++) spawnPuff(cells[i][0], cells[i][1], G.COLORS[piece.col], 3);
   110	    var cl = G.clearLines(S.board);
   111	    var perfect = cl.count > 0 && G.isEmpty(S.board);
   112	    if (cl.count > 0) {
   113	      S.streak++;
   114	      S.lines += cl.count;
   115	      for (i = 0; i < cl.cells.length && FX.flash.length < MAXFLASH; i++) {
   116	        FX.flash.push({ x: cl.cells[i][0], y: cl.cells[i][1], t: 0.42, col: G.COLORS[(cl.cells[i][2] || 1) - 1] || '#fff' });
   117	        spawnPuff(cl.cells[i][0], cl.cells[i][1], G.COLORS[(cl.cells[i][2] || 1) - 1] || '#fff', 6);
   118	      }
   119	      FX.shake = Math.min(1, 0.35 + cl.count * 0.18);
   120	      FX.boardPulse = 1;
   121	      var names = ['', 'LINE CLEAR', 'DOUBLE', 'TRIPLE', 'QUAD', 'PENTA', 'MEGA'];
   122	      pop(names[Math.min(cl.count, 6)] + (S.streak > 1 ? '  STREAK x' + S.streak : ''), '#ffe066');
   123	      if (perfect) pop('BOARD WIPE  +300', '#7ed37e');
   124	      Snd.clear(cl.count, S.streak);
   125	      if (perfect) U.later(function () { Snd.perfect(); }, 180);
   126	    } else {
   127	      S.streak = 0;
   128	      Snd.drop();
   129	    }
   130	    S.score += G.scoreFor(piece.n, cl, S.streak, perfect);
   131	    if (handEmpty()) {
   132	      var nx = S.next;
   133	      if (G.handDead(S.board, nx)) nx = G.dealHand(S.board, S.rng);
   134	      S.hand = nx;
   135	      S.next = G.rollHand(S.rng);
   136	    }
   137	    syncHud();
   138	    if (G.handDead(S.board, S.hand)) endRun();
   139	    return true;
   140	  }
   141	
   142	  function endRun() {
   143	    if (S.over) return;
   144	    S.over = true;
   145	    resetInput();
   146	    FX.shake = 0.6;
   147	    Snd.over();
   148	    var newBest = S.score > S.best;
   149	    if (newBest) { S.best = S.score; U.setNum(bestKey(S.mode), S.score); }
   150	    var official = true;
   151	    if (S.mode === 'daily') {
   152	      var rec = U.getJSON('daily.result', null);
   153	      var have = rec && typeof rec === 'object' && rec.d === S.dailyKey && isFinite(parseFloat(rec.s));
   154	      if (have) { official = false; }
   155	      else U.setJSON('daily.result', { d: S.dailyKey, s: S.score, l: S.lines });
   156	    }
   157	    var hist = U.pushHistory({ m: S.mode, s: S.score, l: S.lines, d: U.todayKey() });
   158	    U.later(function () { showCard(newBest, official, hist); }, 650);
   159	  }

   173	    } else if (S.mode === 'daily') badge = '<span class="nb">DAILY LOGGED</span>';
   174	    $('oBadge').innerHTML = badge;
   175	    var rows = '';

   185	  /* ================= fx ================= */
   186	  function spawnPuff(cx, cy, col, n) {
   187	    var x = L.bx + (cx + 0.5) * L.c, y = L.by + (cy + 0.5) * L.c;
   188	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   189	      if (FX.parts.length >= MAXP) break;
   190	      var a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, sp = 40 + Math.random() * 160;
   191	      FX.parts.push({
   192	        x: x, y: y, vx: Math.cos(a) * sp, vy: Math.sin(a) * sp - 40,
   193	        life: 0.45 + Math.random() * 0.4, max: 0.85, col: col, s: L.c * (0.1 + Math.random() * 0.16)
   194	      });
   195	    }
   196	    while (FX.parts.length > MAXP) FX.parts.shift();
   197	  }
   198	  function pop(text, col) {
   199	    FX.pops.push({ t: 1.1, text: text, col: col || '#fff' });
   200	    while (FX.pops.length > MAXPOP) FX.pops.shift();
   201	  }
   202	
   203	  function update(dt) {
   204	    var i, p;
   205	    for (i = FX.parts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   206	      p = FX.parts[i];
   207	      p.life -= dt;
   208	      if (p.life <= 0) { FX.parts.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   209	      p.vy += 900 * dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt;
   210	    }
   211	    for (i = FX.flash.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { FX.flash[i].t -= dt; if (FX.flash[i].t <= 0) FX.flash.splice(i, 1); }
   212	    for (i = FX.pops.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { FX.pops[i].t -= dt; if (FX.pops[i].t <= 0) FX.pops.splice(i, 1); }
   213	    if (FX.shake > 0) FX.shake = Math.max(0, FX.shake - dt * 2.2);
   214	    if (FX.boardPulse > 0) FX.boardPulse = Math.max(0, FX.boardPulse - dt * 2.5);
   215	  }
   216	
   217	  /* ================= drawing ================= */
   218	  function rr(x, y, w, h, r) {
   219	    r = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
   220	    ctx.beginPath();
   221	    ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
   222	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
   223	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
   224	    ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
   225	    ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
   226	    ctx.closePath();
   227	  }
   228	  function block(x, y, s, col, alpha) {
   229	    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha === undefined ? 1 : alpha;
   230	    var g = s * 0.07;
   231	    ctx.fillStyle = col;
   232	    rr(x + g, y + g, s - g * 2, s - g * 2, s * 0.2); ctx.fill();
   233	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.22)';
   234	    rr(x + g * 2, y + g * 2, s - g * 4, (s - g * 4) * 0.34, s * 0.14); ctx.fill();
   235	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   236	  }
   237	  function drawPiece(piece, x, y, cell, alpha) {
   238	    var c = piece.cells;
   239	    for (var i = 0; i < c.length; i++) block(x + c[i][0] * cell, y + c[i][1] * cell, cell, G.COLORS[piece.col], alpha);
   240	  }
   241	
   242	  function previewClears(piece, ox, oy) {
   243	    /* returns map of cells that would clear, for the highlight */
   244	    scratch.set(S.board);
   245	    var c = piece.cells, i, x, y;
   246	    for (i = 0; i < c.length; i++) scratch[(oy + c[i][1]) * N + (ox + c[i][0])] = 9;
   247	    var out = null;
   248	    for (y = 0; y < N; y++) {
   249	      var full = true;
   250	      for (x = 0; x < N; x++) if (!scratch[y * N + x]) { full = false; break; }
   251	      if (full) { out = out || {}; for (x = 0; x < N; x++) out[y * N + x] = 1; }
   252	    }
   253	    for (x = 0; x < N; x++) {
   254	      var f2 = true;
   255	      for (y = 0; y < N; y++) if (!scratch[y * N + x]) { f2 = false; break; }
   256	      if (f2) { out = out || {}; for (y = 0; y < N; y++) out[y * N + x] = 1; }
   257	    }
   258	    return out;
   259	  }
   260	
   261	  function render() {
   262	    var W = L.w, H = L.h, c = L.c, i, j;
   263	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0e1220';
   264	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   265	    ctx.save();
   266	    if (FX.shake > 0) {
   267	      var m = FX.shake * c * 0.16;
   268	      ctx.translate((Math.random() * 2 - 1) * m, (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * m);
   269	    }
   270	
   271	    /* board frame */
   272	    var pulse = FX.boardPulse;
   273	    ctx.fillStyle = '#151b2e';
   274	    rr(L.bx - c * 0.12, L.by - c * 0.12, L.bs + c * 0.24, L.bs + c * 0.24, c * 0.3); ctx.fill();
   275	    if (pulse > 0) { ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,224,102,' + (pulse * 0.7).toFixed(3) + ')'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.stroke(); }
   276	
   277	    /* ghost target cells */
   278	    var ghostSet = null, ghostOK = false, gp = null, gx = 0, gy = 0;
   279	    if (drag.on && drag.piece) { gp = drag.piece; gx = drag.gc; gy = drag.gr; ghostOK = drag.ok; }
   280	    else if (kb.on && S.hand[kb.slot] && !S.over) {
   281	      gp = S.hand[kb.slot];
   282	      gx = U.clamp(kb.c, 0, N - gp.w); gy = U.clamp(kb.r, 0, N - gp.h);
   283	      ghostOK = G.canPlace(S.board, gp, gx, gy);
   284	    }
   285	    if (gp && ghostOK) ghostSet = previewClears(gp, gx, gy);
   286	
   287	    /* cells */
   288	    for (var y = 0; y < N; y++) for (var x = 0; x < N; x++) {
   289	      var px = L.bx + x * c, py = L.by + y * c, v = S.board[y * N + x];
   290	      var hot = ghostSet && ghostSet[y * N + x];
   291	      if (!v) {
   292	        ctx.fillStyle = hot ? 'rgba(255,224,102,.16)' : ((x + y) & 1 ? '#1b2238' : '#1e2740');
   293	        rr(px + c * 0.07, py + c * 0.07, c * 0.86, c * 0.86, c * 0.2); ctx.fill();
   294	      } else {
   295	        block(px, py, c, G.COLORS[v - 1] || '#888', 1);
   296	        if (hot) { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.3)'; rr(px + c * .07, py + c * .07, c * .86, c * .86, c * .2); ctx.fill(); }
   297	      }
   298	    }
   299	    /* ghost outline */
   300	    if (gp) {
   301	      var cs = gp.cells;
   302	      for (i = 0; i < cs.length; i++) {
   303	        var ex = L.bx + (gx + cs[i][0]) * c, ey = L.by + (gy + cs[i][1]) * c;
   304	        if (ghostOK) { block(ex, ey, c, G.COLORS[gp.col], 0.42); }
   305	        else {
   306	          ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5; ctx.strokeStyle = '#ef6b6b'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   307	          rr(ex + c * .1, ey + c * .1, c * .8, c * .8, c * .18); ctx.stroke(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   308	        }
   309	      }
   310	    }
   311	    /* clear flashes */
   312	    for (i = 0; i < FX.flash.length; i++) {
   313	      var f = FX.flash[i], a = f.t / 0.42;
   314	      ctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.85;
   315	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
   316	      var s2 = c * (1 + (1 - a) * 0.5), off = (s2 - c) / 2;
   317	      rr(L.bx + f.x * c - off, L.by + f.y * c - off, s2, s2, c * 0.2); ctx.fill();
   318	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   319	    }
   320	    /* particles */
   321	    for (i = 0; i < FX.parts.length; i++) {
   322	      var p = FX.parts[i];
   323	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p.life / p.max));
   324	      ctx.fillStyle = p.col;
   325	      ctx.fillRect(p.x - p.s / 2, p.y - p.s / 2, p.s, p.s);
   326	    }
   327	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   328	
   329	    /* hand slots */
   330	    for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
   331	      var sx = L.pad + i * L.sw, sy = L.hy, sw = L.sw - 6, sh = L.hh;
   332	      var pc = S.hand[i];
   333	      var isDragged = drag.on && drag.slot === i;
   334	      var sel = kb.on && kb.slot === i && pc;
   335	      ctx.fillStyle = sel ? '#22304f' : '#141a2c';
   336	      rr(sx + 3, sy, sw, sh, c * 0.22); ctx.fill();
   337	      if (sel) { ctx.strokeStyle = '#4d7bea'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke(); }
   338	      if (pc && !isDragged) {
   339	        var hc = Math.min(sw * 0.8 / pc.w, sh * 0.74 / pc.h, c * 0.7);
   340	        var ox = sx + 3 + (sw - pc.w * hc) / 2, oy = sy + (sh - pc.h * hc) / 2;
   341	        drawPiece(pc, ox, oy, hc, 1);
   342	      }
   343	    }
   344	
   345	    /* next hand preview */
   346	    ctx.fillStyle = '#7f8fb8';
   347	    ctx.font = '700 ' + Math.round(c * 0.24) + 'px -apple-system,system-ui,sans-serif';
   348	    ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   349	    ctx.fillText('NEXT', L.pad + 2, L.ny + L.nh / 2);
   350	    var lw = Math.max(34, c * 0.95), avail = (W - 2 * L.pad - lw) / 3;
   351	    for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
   352	      var np = S.next[i]; if (!np) continue;
   353	      var nc = Math.min(avail * 0.62 / np.w, L.nh * 0.8 / np.h, c * 0.3);
   354	      var nx = L.pad + lw + i * avail + (avail - np.w * nc) / 2;
   355	      var ny = L.ny + (L.nh - np.h * nc) / 2;
   356	      drawPiece(np, nx, ny, nc, 0.55);
   357	    }
   358	
   359	    /* dragged piece, lifted above the finger */
   360	    if (drag.on && drag.piece) {
   361	      var dp = drag.piece;
   362	      var px2 = drag.x - dp.w * c / 2, py2 = drag.y - dp.h * c - c * 0.55;
   363	      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.28; ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
   364	      rr(px2 + 4, py2 + 6, dp.w * c, dp.h * c, c * 0.2); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   365	      drawPiece(dp, px2, py2, c, 1);
   366	    }
   367	
   368	    /* combo popups */
   369	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   370	    for (i = 0; i < FX.pops.length; i++) {
   371	      var q = FX.pops[i], t = q.t / 1.1;
   372	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, t * 1.6);
   373	      var yy = L.by + L.bs * 0.42 - (1 - t) * c * 1.6 + i * c * 0.85;
   374	      ctx.font = '800 ' + Math.round(c * (0.44 + (1 - t) * 0.06)) + 'px -apple-system,system-ui,sans-serif';
   375	      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(3, c * 0.1); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(8,10,20,.9)';
   376	      ctx.strokeText(q.text, L.w / 2, yy);
   377	      ctx.fillStyle = q.col;
   378	      ctx.fillText(q.text, L.w / 2, yy);
   379	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   380	    }
   381	    ctx.restore();
   382	  }
   383	
   384	  /* ================= input ================= */
   385	  function pos(e) {
   386	    var r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
   387	    return { x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top };
   388	  }
   389	  function slotAt(x, y) {
   390	    if (y < L.hy || y > L.hy + L.hh) return -1;
   391	    var i = Math.floor((x - L.pad) / L.sw);
   392	    return (i >= 0 && i < 3) ? i : -1;
   393	  }
   394	  function updGhost() {
   395	    var p = drag.piece;
   396	    if (!p) return;
   397	    var px = drag.x - p.w * L.c / 2, py = drag.y - p.h * L.c - L.c * 0.55;
   398	    drag.gc = Math.round((px - L.bx) / L.c);
   399	    drag.gr = Math.round((py - L.by) / L.c);
   400	    drag.gc = U.clamp(drag.gc, 0, N - p.w);
   401	    drag.gr = U.clamp(drag.gr, 0, N - p.h);
   402	    drag.ok = G.canPlace(S.board, p, drag.gc, drag.gr);
   403	  }
   404	  function onDown(e) {
   405	    if (!S.started || S.paused || S.over) return;
   406	    if (drag.on) return;
   407	    var q = pos(e), si = slotAt(q.x, q.y);
   408	    if (si < 0 || !S.hand[si]) return;
   409	    drag.on = true; drag.pid = (e.pointerId === undefined ? -2 : e.pointerId);
   410	    drag.slot = si; drag.piece = S.hand[si];
   411	    drag.x = q.x; drag.y = q.y; drag.moved = false;
   412	    kb.on = false;
   413	    updGhost();
   414	    Snd.pick();
   415	    if (cv.setPointerCapture && e.pointerId !== undefined) { try { cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (er) {} }
   416	    e.preventDefault();
   417	  }
   418	  function onMove(e) {
   419	    if (!drag.on) return;
   420	    var id = (e.pointerId === undefined ? -2 : e.pointerId);
   421	    if (id !== drag.pid) return;
   422	    var q = pos(e);
   423	    drag.x = q.x; drag.y = q.y; drag.moved = true;
   424	    updGhost();
   425	    e.preventDefault();
   426	  }
   427	  function onUp(e) {
   428	    if (!drag.on) return;
   429	    var id = (e.pointerId === undefined ? -2 : e.pointerId);
   430	    if (id !== drag.pid) return;
   431	    var piece = drag.piece, slot = drag.slot, gc = drag.gc, gr = drag.gr, ok = drag.ok;
   432	    drag.on = false; drag.pid = -1; drag.piece = null; drag.slot = -1; drag.moved = false;
   433	    if (ok) tryPlace(piece, slot, gc, gr); else Snd.bad();
   434	    if (e && e.preventDefault) e.preventDefault();
   435	  }
   436	  function onCancel(e) {
   437	    if (!drag.on) return;
   438	    var id = (e && e.pointerId !== undefined) ? e.pointerId : drag.pid;
   439	    if (id !== drag.pid) return;
   440	    drag.on = false; drag.pid = -1; drag.piece = null; drag.slot = -1; drag.moved = false;
   441	  }
   442	  function releaseAll() {
   443	    drag.on = false; drag.pid = -1; drag.piece = null; drag.slot = -1; drag.moved = false;
   444	    kb.keys = Object.create(null);
   445	  }
   446	
   447	  if (window.PointerEvent) {
   448	    cv.addEventListener('pointerdown', onDown, { passive: false });
   449	    window.addEventListener('pointermove', onMove, { passive: false });
   450	    window.addEventListener('pointerup', onUp, { passive: false });
   451	    window.addEventListener('pointercancel', onCancel, { passive: false });
   452	  } else {
   453	    cv.addEventListener('touchstart', function (e) { var t = e.changedTouches[0]; onDown({ clientX: t.clientX, clientY: t.clientY, pointerId: t.identifier, preventDefault: function () { e.preventDefault(); } }); }, { passive: false });
   454	    window.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) { var t = e.changedTouches[0]; onMove({ clientX: t.clientX, clientY: t.clientY, pointerId: t.identifier, preventDefault: function () { e.preventDefault(); } }); }, { passive: false });
   455	    window.addEventListener('touchend', function (e) { var t = e.changedTouches[0]; onUp({ clientX: t.clientX, clientY: t.clientY, pointerId: t.identifier, preventDefault: function () { e.preventDefault(); } }); }, { passive: false });
   456	    window.addEventListener('touchcancel', function (e) { onCancel({ pointerId: e.changedTouches[0].identifier }); }, { passive: false });
   457	    cv.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) { onDown({ clientX: e.clientX, clientY: e.clientY, pointerId: -2, preventDefault: function () { e.preventDefault(); } }); });
   458	    window.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) { onMove({ clientX: e.clientX, clientY: e.clientY, pointerId: -2, preventDefault: function () {} }); });
   459	    window.addEventListener('mouseup', function (e) { onUp({ clientX: e.clientX, clientY: e.clientY, pointerId: -2, preventDefault: function () {} }); });
   460	  }
   461	  cv.addEventListener('touchstart', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   462	  cv.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   463	  cv.addEventListener('contextmenu', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
   464	
   465	  /* keyboard */
   466	  function nextSlot(dir) {
   467	    for (var k = 1; k <= 3; k++) {
   468	      var i = ((kb.slot + dir * k) % 3 + 3) % 3;
   469	      if (S.hand[i]) { kb.slot = i; return; }
   470	    }
   471	  }
   472	  window.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
   473	    var k = e.key;
   474	    if (k === ' ' || k.indexOf('Arrow') === 0) e.preventDefault();
   475	    kb.keys[k] = 1;
   476	    if (!S.started) { if (k === ' ' || k === 'Enter') begin(); return; }
   477	    if (k === 'r' || k === 'R') { Snd.ui(); start(S.mode); return; }
   478	    if (S.over || S.paused) { if (k === ' ' || k === 'Enter') start(S.mode); return; }
   479	    var p = S.hand[kb.slot];
   480	    if (!p) nextSlot(1), p = S.hand[kb.slot];
   481	    if (k === 'ArrowLeft') { kb.on = true; kb.c--; }
   482	    else if (k === 'ArrowRight') { kb.on = true; kb.c++; }
   483	    else if (k === 'ArrowUp') { kb.on = true; kb.r--; }
   484	    else if (k === 'ArrowDown') { kb.on = true; kb.r++; }
   485	    else if (k === '1' || k === '2' || k === '3') { kb.on = true; var i = parseInt(k, 10) - 1; if (S.hand[i]) { kb.slot = i; Snd.pick(); } }
   486	    else if (k === 'Tab') { e.preventDefault(); kb.on = true; nextSlot(1); Snd.pick(); }
   487	    else if (k === ' ' || k === 'Enter') {
   488	      kb.on = true;
   489	      p = S.hand[kb.slot];
   490	      if (p) {
   491	        var cx = U.clamp(kb.c, 0, N - p.w), cy = U.clamp(kb.r, 0, N - p.h);
   492	        if (!tryPlace(p, kb.slot, cx, cy)) Snd.bad();
   493	        else if (!S.hand[kb.slot]) nextSlot(1);
   494	      }
   495	    }
   496	    if (p) { kb.c = U.clamp(kb.c, 0, N - p.w); kb.r = U.clamp(kb.r, 0, N - p.h); }
   497	    else { kb.c = U.clamp(kb.c, 0, N - 1); kb.r = U.clamp(kb.r, 0, N - 1); }
   498	  });
   499	  window.addEventListener('keyup', function (e) { delete kb.keys[e.key]; });
   500	  window.addEventListener('blur', releaseAll);
   501	  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { if (document.hidden) releaseAll(); });
   502	
   503	  /* buttons */
   504	  function bind(id, fn) {
   505	    var el = $(id);
   506	    el.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); Snd.unlock(); Snd.ui(); fn(); });
   507	  }
   508	  bind('tabM', function () { if (S.mode !== 'marathon' || S.over) start('marathon'); });
   509	  bind('tabD', function () { if (S.mode !== 'daily' || S.over) start('daily'); });
   510	  bind('btnAgain', function () { start(S.mode); });
   511	  bind('btnSwap', function () { start(S.mode === 'daily' ? 'marathon' : 'daily'); });
   512	  bind('btnStart', function () { begin(); });
   513	
   514	  function begin() {
   515	    Snd.unlock();
   516	    ovStart.className = 'ov hide';
   517	    resize();
   518	    start('marathon');
   519	  }
   520	
   521	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
   522	  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', function () { U.later(resize, 120); });
   523	
   524	  /* ================= loop ================= */
   525	  var last = 0;
   526	  function frame(ts) {
   527	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   528	    var dt = (ts - last) / 1000;
   529	    last = ts;
   530	    if (!isFinite(dt) || dt < 0) dt = 0;
   531	    dt = Math.min(dt, 0.05);
   532	    if (!S.paused) update(dt);
   533	    render();
   534	  }
   535	
   536	  resize();
   537	  S.best = Math.max(0, Math.round(U.getNum(bestKey('marathon'), 0)));
   538	  syncHud();
   539	  requestAnimationFrame(function (t) { last = t; requestAnimationFrame(frame); });
   540	})();
--- gridfall/game.js ---
     1	/* Gridfall - game.js : board model, piece set, dealing, scoring. No rendering here. */
     2	'use strict';
     3	var G = (function () {
     4	
     5	  var N = 8;                 // board is 8x8
     6	  var COLORS = ['#4fc3f7', '#ffb74d', '#7ed37e', '#ef6b6b', '#b47ae0', '#ffe066', '#4dd0e1'];
     7	
     8	  /* ---- piece library: base shapes expanded through unique rotations ---- */
     9	  function norm(cells) {
    10	    var mx = 99, my = 99, i;
    11	    for (i = 0; i < cells.length; i++) { if (cells[i][0] < mx) mx = cells[i][0]; if (cells[i][1] < my) my = cells[i][1]; }
    12	    var out = [];
    13	    for (i = 0; i < cells.length; i++) out.push([cells[i][0] - mx, cells[i][1] - my]);
    14	    out.sort(function (a, b) { return (a[1] - b[1]) || (a[0] - b[0]); });
    15	    return out;
    16	  }
    17	  function rot(cells) {
    18	    var my = 0;
    19	    for (var i = 0; i < cells.length; i++) if (cells[i][1] > my) my = cells[i][1];
    20	    var out = [];
    21	    for (i = 0; i < cells.length; i++) out.push([my - cells[i][1], cells[i][0]]);
    22	    return norm(out);
    23	  }
    24	  function key(cells) { var s = ''; for (var i = 0; i < cells.length; i++) s += cells[i][0] + ',' + cells[i][1] + ';'; return s; }
    25	
    26	  var BASES = [
    27	    { c: 0, w: 5, s: [[0, 0]] },                                                   // single
    28	    { c: 0, w: 8, s: [[0, 0], [1, 0]] },                                           // domino
    29	    { c: 1, w: 8, s: [[0, 0], [1, 0], [2, 0]] },                                   // tri line
    30	    { c: 1, w: 6, s: [[0, 0], [1, 0], [2, 0], [3, 0]] },                           // quad line
    31	    { c: 2, w: 3, s: [[0, 0], [1, 0], [2, 0], [3, 0], [4, 0]] },                   // penta line
    32	    { c: 3, w: 8, s: [[0, 0], [1, 0], [0, 1], [1, 1]] },                           // 2x2
    33	    { c: 4, w: 2, s: [[0, 0], [1, 0], [2, 0], [0, 1], [1, 1], [2, 1], [0, 2], [1, 2], [2, 2]] }, // 3x3
    34	    { c: 5, w: 9, s: [[0, 0], [0, 1], [1, 1]] },                                   // corner (3)
    35	    { c: 6, w: 5, s: [[0, 0], [1, 0], [2, 0], [1, 1]] },                           // T
    36	    { c: 2, w: 4, s: [[1, 0], [2, 0], [0, 1], [1, 1]] },                           // S
    37	    { c: 3, w: 4, s: [[0, 0], [1, 0], [1, 1], [2, 1]] },                           // Z
    38	    { c: 4, w: 5, s: [[0, 0], [0, 1], [0, 2], [1, 2]] },                           // L
    39	    { c: 6, w: 5, s: [[1, 0], [1, 1], [1, 2], [0, 2]] },                           // J
    40	    { c: 5, w: 4, s: [[0, 0], [0, 1], [0, 2], [1, 2], [2, 2]] }                    // big corner (5)
    41	  ];
    42	
    43	  var PIECES = [], BAG = [];
    44	  (function build() {
    45	    for (var b = 0; b < BASES.length; b++) {
    46	      var cur = norm(BASES[b].s), seen = {}, r;
    47	      for (r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
    48	        var k = key(cur);
    49	        if (!seen[k]) {
    50	          seen[k] = 1;
    51	          var w = 0, h = 0;
    52	          for (var i = 0; i < cur.length; i++) { if (cur[i][0] + 1 > w) w = cur[i][0] + 1; if (cur[i][1] + 1 > h) h = cur[i][1] + 1; }
    53	          PIECES.push({ id: PIECES.length, cells: cur, w: w, h: h, n: cur.length, col: BASES[b].c, wt: BASES[b].w });
    54	        }
    55	        cur = rot(cur);
    56	      }
    57	    }
    58	    for (var p = 0; p < PIECES.length; p++) for (var q = 0; q < PIECES[p].wt; q++) BAG.push(PIECES[p].id);
    59	  })();
    60	
    61	  /* ---- board helpers ---- */
    62	  function newBoard() { return new Uint8Array(N * N); }
    63	
    64	  function canPlace(bd, piece, ox, oy) {
    65	    if (ox < 0 || oy < 0 || ox + piece.w > N || oy + piece.h > N) return false;
    66	    var c = piece.cells;
    67	    for (var i = 0; i < c.length; i++) {
    68	      if (bd[(oy + c[i][1]) * N + (ox + c[i][0])]) return false;
    69	    }
    70	    return true;
    71	  }
    72	  function anySpot(bd, piece) {
    73	    for (var y = 0; y <= N - piece.h; y++)
    74	      for (var x = 0; x <= N - piece.w; x++)
    75	        if (canPlace(bd, piece, x, y)) return true;
    76	    return false;
    77	  }
    78	  function place(bd, piece, ox, oy) {
    79	    var c = piece.cells, cells = [];
    80	    for (var i = 0; i < c.length; i++) {
    81	      var cx = ox + c[i][0], cy = oy + c[i][1];
    82	      bd[cy * N + cx] = piece.col + 1;
    83	      cells.push([cx, cy]);
    84	    }
    85	    return cells;
    86	  }
    87	  /* returns {rows:[], cols:[], cells:[[x,y,color]...]} and mutates board */
    88	  function clearLines(bd) {
    89	    var rows = [], cols = [], x, y, full;
    90	    for (y = 0; y < N; y++) {
    91	      full = true;
    92	      for (x = 0; x < N; x++) if (!bd[y * N + x]) { full = false; break; }
    93	      if (full) rows.push(y);
    94	    }
    95	    for (x = 0; x < N; x++) {
    96	      full = true;
    97	      for (y = 0; y < N; y++) if (!bd[y * N + x]) { full = false; break; }
    98	      if (full) cols.push(x);
    99	    }
   100	    var cells = [], mark = {};
   101	    function add(cx, cy) {
   102	      var k = cy * N + cx;
   103	      if (mark[k]) return;
   104	      mark[k] = 1;
   105	      cells.push([cx, cy, bd[k]]);
   106	    }
   107	    for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) for (x = 0; x < N; x++) add(x, rows[i]);
   108	    for (i = 0; i < cols.length; i++) for (y = 0; y < N; y++) add(cols[i], y);
   109	    for (i = 0; i < cells.length; i++) bd[cells[i][1] * N + cells[i][0]] = 0;
   110	    return { rows: rows, cols: cols, cells: cells, count: rows.length + cols.length };
   111	  }
   112	  function isEmpty(bd) { for (var i = 0; i < bd.length; i++) if (bd[i]) return false; return true; }
   113	  function filled(bd) { var n = 0; for (var i = 0; i < bd.length; i++) if (bd[i]) n++; return n; }
   114	
   115	  /* ---- dealing: a hand NEVER arrives dead ---- */
   116	  function rollHand(rng) {
   117	    var h = [];
   118	    for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) h.push(PIECES[BAG[Math.floor(rng() * BAG.length) % BAG.length]]);
   119	    return h;
   120	  }
   121	  function playableCount(bd, hand) {
   122	    var n = 0;
   123	    for (var i = 0; i < hand.length; i++) if (hand[i] && anySpot(bd, hand[i])) n++;
   124	    return n;
   125	  }
   126	  /* prefer a hand where all three fit somewhere; never return one where none fit */
   127	  function dealHand(bd, rng) {
   128	    var best = null, bestScore = -1;
   129	    for (var t = 0; t < 60; t++) {
   130	      var h = rollHand(rng);
   131	      var s = playableCount(bd, h);
   132	      if (s > bestScore) { bestScore = s; best = h; }
   133	      if (s === 3) return h;
   134	      if (t >= 24 && bestScore >= 1) return best;
   135	    }
   136	    return best;
   137	  }
   138	  function handDead(bd, hand) {
   139	    for (var i = 0; i < hand.length; i++) if (hand[i] && anySpot(bd, hand[i])) return false;
   140	    return true;
   141	  }
   142	
   143	  /* ---- daily starting board: seeded scatter, never a pre-made full line ---- */
   144	  function seedBoard(bd, rng) {
   145	    var want = 8 + Math.floor(rng() * 5), tries = 0;
   146	    var colr = Math.floor(rng() * COLORS.length);
   147	    while (want > 0 && tries < 400) {
   148	      tries++;
   149	      var x = Math.floor(rng() * N), y = Math.floor(rng() * N), k = y * N + x;
   150	      if (bd[k]) continue;
   151	      bd[k] = ((colr + x + y) % COLORS.length) + 1;
   152	      var cl = clearLines(bd);
   153	      if (cl.count > 0) { bd[k] = 0; continue; }
   154	      want--;
   155	    }
   156	  }
   157	
   158	  /* ---- scoring ---- */
   159	  function scoreFor(nCells, cl, streak, perfect) {
   160	    var pts = nCells;
   161	    if (cl.count > 0) {
   162	      var base = 10 * cl.count * cl.count + 10;
   163	      var mult = 1 + 0.25 * Math.max(0, streak - 1);
   164	      pts += Math.round(base * mult);
   165	      if (perfect) pts += 300;
   166	    }
   167	    return pts;
   168	  }
   169	
   170	  return {
   171	    N: N, COLORS: COLORS, PIECES: PIECES,
   172	    newBoard: newBoard, canPlace: canPlace, anySpot: anySpot, place: place,
   173	    clearLines: clearLines, isEmpty: isEmpty, filled: filled,
   174	    dealHand: dealHand, rollHand: rollHand, handDead: handDead, playableCount: playableCount,
   175	    seedBoard: seedBoard, scoreFor: scoreFor
   176	  };
   177	})();
--- gridfall/util.js ---
     1	/* Gridfall - util.js : rng, safe storage, timers, math */
     2	'use strict';
     3	var U = (function () {
     4	
     5	  function clamp(v, a, b) { return v < a ? a : (v > b ? b : v); }
     6	  function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
     7	
     8	  /* ---- deterministic RNG (mulberry32) ---- */
     9	  function makeRng(seed) {
    10	    var s = (seed >>> 0) || 1;
    11	    function r() {
    12	      s = (s + 0x6D2B79F5) >>> 0;
    13	      var t = s;
    14	      t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);
    15	      t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    16	      return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    17	    }
    18	    r.int = function (n) { return Math.floor(r() * n) % (n || 1); };
    19	    r.pick = function (arr) { return arr[Math.floor(r() * arr.length) % arr.length]; };
    20	    return r;
    21	  }
    22	  function hashStr(str) {
    23	    var h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
    24	    for (var i = 0; i < str.length; i++) { h ^= str.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619) >>> 0; }
    25	    return h >>> 0;
    26	  }
    27	  function todayKey(d) {
    28	    d = d || new Date();
    29	    var m = d.getMonth() + 1, day = d.getDate();
    30	    return d.getFullYear() + '-' + (m < 10 ? '0' : '') + m + '-' + (day < 10 ? '0' : '') + day;
    31	  }
    32	
    33	  /* ---- storage: every access guarded, every parse type-validated ---- */
    34	  var PRE = 'gridfall.';
    35	  function raw(key) {
    36	    try {
    37	      if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined' || !localStorage) return null;
    38	      var v = localStorage.getItem(PRE + key);
    39	      return (typeof v === 'string') ? v : null;
    40	    } catch (e) { return null; }
    41	  }
    42	  function setRaw(key, val) {
    43	    try {
    44	      if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined' || !localStorage) return false;
    45	      localStorage.setItem(PRE + key, String(val));
    46	      return true;
    47	    } catch (e) { return false; }
    48	  }
    49	  function getNum(key, dflt) {
    50	    var v = raw(key);
    51	    if (v === null) return dflt;
    52	    var n = parseFloat(v);
    53	    if (typeof n !== 'number' || !isFinite(n)) return dflt;
    54	    return n;
    55	  }
    56	  function setNum(key, n) {
    57	    if (typeof n !== 'number' || !isFinite(n)) return;
    58	    setRaw(key, Math.round(n));
    59	  }
    60	  function getJSON(key, dflt) {
    61	    var v = raw(key);
    62	    if (v === null) return dflt;
    63	    var o = null;
    64	    try { o = JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { return dflt; }
    65	    if (o === null || o === undefined) return dflt;
    66	    return o;
    67	  }
    68	  function setJSON(key, o) {
    69	    var s = null;
    70	    try { s = JSON.stringify(o); } catch (e) { return; }
    71	    if (typeof s === 'string') setRaw(key, s);
    72	  }
    73	  /* history: array of {m:mode, s:score, l:lines, d:datestr} - always pruned */
    74	  var HIST_MAX = 10;
    75	  function getHistory() {
    76	    var a = getJSON('history', null);
    77	    if (!a || Object.prototype.toString.call(a) !== '[object Array]') return [];
    78	    var out = [];
    79	    for (var i = 0; i < a.length && out.length < HIST_MAX; i++) {
    80	      var r = a[i];
    81	      if (!r || typeof r !== 'object') continue;
    82	      var s = parseFloat(r.s);
    83	      if (!isFinite(s)) continue;
    84	      var l = parseFloat(r.l); if (!isFinite(l)) l = 0;
    85	      out.push({
    86	        m: (r.m === 'daily') ? 'daily' : 'marathon',
    87	        s: Math.max(0, Math.round(s)),
    88	        l: Math.max(0, Math.round(l)),
    89	        d: (typeof r.d === 'string' && r.d.length < 16) ? r.d : ''
    90	      });
    91	    }
    92	    return out;
    93	  }
    94	  function pushHistory(rec) {
    95	    var a = getHistory();
    96	    a.unshift(rec);
    97	    while (a.length > HIST_MAX) a.pop();
    98	    setJSON('history', a);
    99	    return a;
   100	  }
   101	
   102	  /* ---- managed timers: cancellable in one call (restart safety) ---- */
   103	  var timers = [];
   104	  function later(fn, ms) {
   105	    var id = setTimeout(function () {
   106	      var i = timers.indexOf(id); if (i >= 0) timers.splice(i, 1);
   107	      fn();
   108	    }, ms);
   109	    timers.push(id);
   110	    if (timers.length > 64) { clearTimeout(timers.shift()); }
   111	    return id;
   112	  }
   113	  function clearTimers() {
   114	    for (var i = 0; i < timers.length; i++) clearTimeout(timers[i]);
   115	    timers.length = 0;
   116	  }
   117	
   118	  return {
   119	    clamp: clamp, lerp: lerp, makeRng: makeRng, hashStr: hashStr, todayKey: todayKey,
   120	    getNum: getNum, setNum: setNum, getJSON: getJSON, setJSON: setJSON,
   121	    getHistory: getHistory, pushHistory: pushHistory,
   122	    later: later, clearTimers: clearTimers
   123	  };
   124	})();

    20	    { name: 'Orbit', role: 'boomerang', tag: 'LOOPBACK', color: '#b59cff', hp: 104, speed: 210, rate: .58, damage: 17, shots: 1, bullet: 430, super: 'TRIPLE ARC' }
    21	  ];
    22	  const ROAD = [0, 2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 14];
    23	  const COVERS = [
    24	    { x: 205, y: 270, w: 120, h: 36 }, { x: 575, y: 270, w: 120, h: 36 },
    25	    { x: 205, y: 1094, w: 120, h: 36 }, { x: 575, y: 1094, w: 120, h: 36 },
    26	    { x: 335, y: 450, w: 58, h: 130 }, { x: 507, y: 450, w: 58, h: 130 },
    27	    { x: 335, y: 820, w: 58, h: 130 }, { x: 507, y: 820, w: 58, h: 130 }
    28	  ];
    29	
    30	  let W = 390, H = 700, scale = 1, lastFrame = 0, orientationBlocked = false;
    31	  let camera = { x: 0, y: 0 };
    32	  let audio = null;
    33	  let game = null;
    34	  let save = loadSave();
    35	  let landscapeKnown = false;
    36	
    37	  const keys = new Set();
    38	  const actions = [];
    39	  const sticks = { left: { id: null, x: 0, y: 0 }, right: { id: null, x: 0, y: 0 } };
    40	  const buttons = { super: null, menu: null };
    41	  const timers = new Set();
    42	
    43	  function finite(value, fallback, min, max) {
    44	    const n = Number(value);
    45	    return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.max(min, Math.min(max, n)) : fallback;
    46	  }
    47	
    48	  function loadSave() {
    49	    const fallback = { trophies: 0, wins: 0, losses: 0, bestGems: 0, bestHeist: 0, selectedKit: 0, mode: 'gem' };
    50	    try {
    51	      const raw = localStorage.getItem('scrapper-squad-save');
    52	      const parsed = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    53	      if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed)) return fallback;
    54	      return {
    55	        trophies: Math.floor(finite(parsed.trophies, 0, 0, 10000)),
    56	        wins: Math.floor(finite(parsed.wins, 0, 0, 1000000)),
    57	        losses: Math.floor(finite(parsed.losses, 0, 0, 1000000)),
    58	        bestGems: Math.floor(finite(parsed.bestGems, 0, 0, 99)),
    59	        bestHeist: Math.floor(finite(parsed.bestHeist, 0, 0, 100)),
    60	        selectedKit: Math.floor(finite(parsed.selectedKit, 0, 0, KITS.length - 1)),
    61	        mode: parsed.mode === 'heist' ? 'heist' : 'gem'
    62	      };
    63	    } catch (err) { return fallback; }
    64	  }
    65	
    66	  function saveProgress() {
    67	    try {
    68	      localStorage.setItem('scrapper-squad-save', JSON.stringify(save));
    69	    } catch (err) { /* local storage is optional */ }
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  function unlocked(index) { return save.trophies >= ROAD[index]; }
    73	  function clamp(n, a, b) { return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n)); }
    74	  function dist(a, b) { return Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y); }
    75	  function norm(x, y) {
    76	    const l = Math.hypot(x, y);
    77	    return l > .001 ? { x: x / l, y: y / l } : { x: 1, y: 0 };
    78	  }
    79	  function angle(v) { return Math.atan2(v.y, v.x); }
    80	  function addBounded(list, item, limit) {
    81	    list.push(item);
    82	    if (list.length > limit) list.splice(0, list.length - limit);
    83	  }
    84	  function randomRange(a, b) { return a + Math.random() * (b - a); }
    85	
    86	  function schedule(fn, delay) {
    87	    const id = setTimeout(() => { timers.delete(id); fn(); }, delay);
    88	    timers.add(id);
    89	    return id;
    90	  }
    91	  function cancelTimers() {
    92	    timers.forEach(id => clearTimeout(id));
    93	    timers.clear();
    94	  }
    95	
    96	  function resetInput() {
    97	    sticks.left.id = sticks.right.id = null;
    98	    sticks.left.x = sticks.left.y = sticks.right.x = sticks.right.y = 0;
    99	    buttons.super = buttons.menu = null;
   100	    keys.clear();
   101	    actions.length = 0;
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  function unlockAudio() {
   105	    try {
   106	      if (!audio) audio = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
   107	      if (audio.state === 'suspended') audio.resume();
   108	    } catch (err) { audio = null; }
   109	  }
   110	  function tone(freq, duration, type, volume) {
   111	    if (!audio) return;
   112	    try {
   113	      const now = audio.currentTime;
   114	      const osc = audio.createOscillator();
   115	      const gain = audio.createGain();
   116	      osc.type = type || 'sine'; osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now);
   117	      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(volume || .025, now);
   118	      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001, now + duration);
   119	      osc.connect(gain).connect(audio.destination); osc.start(now); osc.stop(now + duration);
   120	    } catch (err) { /* audio is a bonus */ }
   121	  }
   122	
   123	  function resize() {
   124	    W = Math.max(1, window.innerWidth);
   125	    H = Math.max(1, window.innerHeight);
   126	    const dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
   127	    scale = Math.min(dpr, 960 / Math.max(W, H));
   128	    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(W * scale));
   129	    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(H * scale));
   130	    canvas.style.width = W + 'px'; canvas.style.height = H + 'px';
   131	    ctx.setTransform(scale, 0, 0, scale, 0, 0);
   132	    updateOrientation();
   133	  }
   134	
   135	  function updateOrientation() {
   136	    const blocked = window.innerWidth > window.innerHeight;
   137	    if (blocked !== landscapeKnown) {
   138	      landscapeKnown = blocked;
   139	      resetInput();
   140	    }
   141	    orientationBlocked = blocked;
   142	    rotateOverlay.hidden = !blocked;
   143	  }
   144	
   145	  function pushAction(action) {
   146	    if (actions.length >= LIMITS.actions) actions.shift();
   147	    actions.push(action);
   148	  }
   149	
   150	  function newMatch(mode) {
   151	    cancelTimers();
   152	    resetInput();
   153	    save.mode = mode === 'heist' ? 'heist' : 'gem';
   154	    saveProgress();
   155	    game = {
   156	      mode: save.mode, status: 'playing', time: 0, shake: 0, flash: 0, gemClock: 1.7,
   157	      holdTeam: -1, holdTimer: 15, actors: [], projectiles: [], gems: [], particles: [], texts: [], mines: [], turrets: [],
   158	      safes: { 0: 100, 1: 100 }, result: null, resultReason: '', playerAim: { x: 1, y: 0 }, hint: save.mode === 'gem' ? 'Mine drip incoming — scoop, scrap, hold.' : 'Press the enemy safe while your squad covers yours.'
   159	    };
   160	    game.actors.push(actor(0, save.selectedKit, 130, 700, true));
   161	    game.actors.push(actor(0, (save.selectedKit + 2) % KITS.length, 174, 770, false));
   162	    game.actors.push(actor(0, (save.selectedKit + 5) % KITS.length, 174, 630, false));
   163	    game.actors.push(actor(1, (save.selectedKit + 1) % KITS.length, 770, 700, false));
   164	    game.actors.push(actor(1, (save.selectedKit + 3) % KITS.length, 726, 630, false));
   165	    game.actors.push(actor(1, (save.selectedKit + 6) % KITS.length, 726, 770, false));
   166	    if (game.mode === 'gem') {
   167	      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) dropGem(450 + randomRange(-22, 22), 700 + randomRange(-22, 22));
   168	    }
   169	    unlockAudio();
   170	    tone(240, .12, 'square', .04);
   171	  }
   172	
   173	  function actor(team, kitIndex, x, y, human) {
   174	    const kit = KITS[kitIndex];
   175	    return { id: team + '-' + kitIndex + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), team, kit: kitIndex, x, y, spawnX: x, spawnY: y, human: !!human, r: 22,
   176	      hp: kit.hp, maxHp: kit.hp, speed: kit.speed, cooldown: 0, super: 0, gems: 0, alive: true, respawn: 0, aim: { x: team ? -1 : 1, y: 0 },
   177	      aiClock: randomRange(0, .4), barrier: 0, dash: 0, label: human ? 'YOU' : (team ? 'RIVAL' : 'ALLY') };
   178	  }
   179	
   180	  function dropGem(x, y) {
   181	    if (!game || game.gems.length >= LIMITS.gems) return;
   182	    addBounded(game.gems, { x: clamp(x, 28, WORLD.w - 28), y: clamp(y, 100, WORLD.h - 30), pulse: Math.random() * TAU }, LIMITS.gems);
   183	  }
   184	
   185	  function burst(x, y, color, count, speed) {
   186	    if (!game) return;
   187	    const room = Math.max(0, LIMITS.particles - game.particles.length);
   188	    const amount = Math.min(count, room);
   189	    for (let i = 0; i < amount; i++) {
   190	      const a = Math.random() * TAU, v = randomRange(speed * .35, speed);
   191	      game.particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * v, vy: Math.sin(a) * v, life: randomRange(.25, .7), max: .7, size: randomRange(2, 6), color });
   192	    }
   193	  }
   194	  function textPop(x, y, text, color) {
   195	    if (!game) return;
   196	    addBounded(game.texts, { x, y, text, color: color || COLORS.paper, life: 1 }, LIMITS.texts);
   197	  }
   198	
   199	  function living(team) { return game.actors.filter(a => a.team === team && a.alive); }
   200	  function teamGems(team) { return game.actors.reduce((sum, a) => sum + (a.team === team ? a.gems : 0), 0); }
   201	  function nearest(from, team, includeSafe) {
   202	    let best = null, bestD = Infinity;
   203	    for (const a of game.actors) if (a.alive && a.team !== team) { const d = dist(from, a); if (d < bestD) { best = a; bestD = d; } }
   204	    if (includeSafe) {
   205	      const safe = { x: team ? 80 : 820, y: 700 };
   206	      const d = dist(from, safe);
   207	      if (d < bestD) best = { ...safe, safe: true };
   208	    }
   209	    return best;
   210	  }
   211	
   212	  function getPlayerMove() {
   213	    let x = sticks.left.x, y = sticks.left.y;
   214	    if (Math.hypot(x, y) < .08) { x = (keys.has('d') ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has('a') ? 1 : 0); y = (keys.has('s') ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has('w') ? 1 : 0); }
   215	    return norm(x, y);
   216	  }
   217	  function getPlayerAim() {
   218	    let x = sticks.right.x, y = sticks.right.y;
   219	    if (Math.hypot(x, y) < .08) { x = (keys.has('ArrowRight') ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has('ArrowLeft') ? 1 : 0); y = (keys.has('ArrowDown') ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has('ArrowUp') ? 1 : 0); }
   220	    return Math.hypot(x, y) > .08 ? norm(x, y) : game.playerAim;
   221	  }
   222	
   223	  function update(dt) {
   224	    if (!game || game.status !== 'playing') return;
   225	    game.time += dt;
   226	    game.shake = Math.max(0, game.shake - dt * 4);
   227	    game.flash = Math.max(0, game.flash - dt * 2.6);
   228	    if (game.time >= MAX_MATCH) return finishMatch(decideByScore(), 'TIME CALLED');
   229	    const p = game.actors[0];
   230	    if (p.alive) {
   231	      const move = getPlayerMove();
   232	      moveActor(p, move.x * p.speed * dt, move.y * p.speed * dt);
   233	      const aim = getPlayerAim();
   234	      game.playerAim = aim; p.aim = aim;
   235	      if (sticks.right.id === null && (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('ArrowDown'))) fire(p, aim);
   236	      while (actions.length) {
   237	        const action = actions.shift();
   238	        if (action.type === 'fire' && p.alive) fire(p, action.dir);
   239	        if (action.type === 'super' && p.alive) useSuper(p);
   240	      }
   241	    } else if (actions.length) actions.length = 0;
   242	    for (const a of game.actors) {
   243	      a.cooldown = Math.max(0, a.cooldown - dt); a.barrier = Math.max(0, a.barrier - dt);
   244	      if (!a.alive) { a.respawn -= dt; if (a.respawn <= 0) respawn(a); continue; }
   245	      a.super = clamp(a.super + dt * 1.3, 0, 100);
   246	      if (!a.human) botUpdate(a, dt);
   247	    }
   248	    updateProjectiles(dt);
   249	    updateMines(dt);
   250	    updateTurrets(dt);
   251	    collectGems();
   252	    if (game.mode === 'gem') updateGemMode(dt);
   253	    else updateHeistMode(dt);
   254	    updateParticles(dt);
   255	    camera.x = clamp(p.x - W * .5, 0, Math.max(0, WORLD.w - W));
   256	    camera.y = clamp(p.y - H * .48, 0, Math.max(0, WORLD.h - H));
   257	  }
   258	
   259	  function moveActor(a, dx, dy) {
   260	    a.x = clamp(a.x + dx, 32, WORLD.w - 32); a.y = clamp(a.y + dy, 100, WORLD.h - 26);
   261	  }
   262	
   263	  function botUpdate(a, dt) {
   264	    a.aiClock -= dt;
   265	    if (a.aiClock > 0) return;
   266	    a.aiClock = .16 + Math.random() * .15;
   267	    const kit = KITS[a.kit], targetEnemy = nearest(a, a.team, false);
   268	    let goal = targetEnemy || { x: a.team ? 820 : 80, y: 700 };
   269	    if (game.mode === 'gem') {
   270	      const held = teamGems(a.team);
   271	      if (held < 7 || a.gems === 0) goal = { x: 450 + Math.sin(game.time * .7) * 45, y: 700 + Math.cos(game.time * .7) * 45 };
   272	      if (a.gems >= 4) goal = { x: a.team ? 770 : 130, y: 700 };
   273	      if (kit.role === 'sniper') goal = targetEnemy ? { x: targetEnemy.x + (a.team ? 125 : -125), y: targetEnemy.y } : goal;
   274	      if (kit.role === 'healer') {
   275	        const hurt = living(a.team).sort((u, v) => u.hp / u.maxHp - v.hp / v.maxHp)[0];
   276	        if (hurt && hurt.hp < hurt.maxHp * .82) goal = hurt;
   277	      }
   278	    } else {
   279	      goal = { x: a.team ? 80 : 820, y: 700 };
   280	      if (kit.role === 'engineer') goal = { x: a.team ? 330 : 570, y: 700 };
   281	      if (kit.role === 'sniper' && targetEnemy) goal = { x: targetEnemy.x + (a.team ? 160 : -160), y: targetEnemy.y };
   282	      if (kit.role === 'healer') {
   283	        const hurt = living(a.team).sort((u, v) => u.hp / u.maxHp - v.hp / v.maxHp)[0];
   284	        if (hurt && hurt.hp < hurt.maxHp * .85) goal = hurt;
   285	      }
   286	    }
   287	    const d = Math.hypot(goal.x - a.x, goal.y - a.y);
   288	    const dir = norm(goal.x - a.x, goal.y - a.y);
   289	    const keepAway = kit.role === 'sniper' && targetEnemy && d < 250;
   290	    moveActor(a, (keepAway ? -dir.x : dir.x) * a.speed * .48 * .16, (keepAway ? -dir.y : dir.y) * a.speed * .48 * .16);
   291	    if (targetEnemy) { a.aim = norm(targetEnemy.x - a.x, targetEnemy.y - a.y); if (d < (kit.role === 'sniper' ? 690 : 460)) fire(a, a.aim); }
   292	    if (kit.role === 'healer') {
   293	      const hurt = living(a.team).find(u => u !== a && u.hp < u.maxHp * .88);
   294	      if (hurt) { a.aim = norm(hurt.x - a.x, hurt.y - a.y); fire(a, a.aim); }
   295	    }
   296	    if (a.super >= 100 && ((game.mode === 'heist' && d < 260) || a.hp < a.maxHp * .4 || game.mode === 'gem' && a.gems >= 4)) useSuper(a);
   297	  }
   298	
   299	  function fire(a, dir) {
   300	    if (!a.alive || a.cooldown > 0) return;
   301	    const kit = KITS[a.kit], spread = kit.role === 'shotgunner' ? .2 : kit.role === 'tank' ? .12 : 0;
   302	    a.cooldown = kit.rate;
   303	    for (let i = 0; i < kit.shots; i++) {
   304	      const offset = kit.shots === 1 ? 0 : (i - (kit.shots - 1) / 2) * spread;
   305	      const a0 = angle(dir) + offset;
   306	      const bomb = kit.role === 'bomber';
   307	      const boomer = kit.role === 'boomerang';
   308	      addBounded(game.projectiles, { x: a.x + Math.cos(a0) * 26, y: a.y + Math.sin(a0) * 26, vx: Math.cos(a0) * kit.bullet, vy: Math.sin(a0) * kit.bullet,
   309	        life: bomb ? 1.55 : boomer ? 1.1 : 1.05, team: a.team, owner: a, damage: kit.damage, radius: bomb ? 12 : 7, bomb, boomer, returning: false, heal: kit.role === 'healer' }, LIMITS.projectiles);
   310	    }
   311	    burst(a.x + dir.x * 28, a.y + dir.y * 28, kit.color, kit.role === 'shotgunner' ? 4 : 2, 45);
   312	    tone(kit.role === 'sniper' ? 520 : 190, .045, 'square', .018);
   313	  }
   314	
   315	  function useSuper(a) {
   316	    if (!a.alive || a.super < 100) return;
   317	    const kit = KITS[a.kit]; a.super = 0; game.flash = .8; game.shake = .55;
   318	    textPop(a.x, a.y - 34, kit.super, kit.color); burst(a.x, a.y, kit.color, 22, 190); tone(kit.role === 'sniper' ? 880 : 420, .18, 'sawtooth', .045);
   319	    if (kit.role === 'shotgunner') areaDamage(a, 130, 42);
   320	    if (kit.role === 'sniper') spawnSpecial(a, a.aim, 78, 980, { pierce: true, color: kit.color, life: 1.3 });
   321	    if (kit.role === 'healer') for (const ally of living(a.team)) { ally.hp = Math.min(ally.maxHp, ally.hp + 42); burst(ally.x, ally.y, COLORS.green, 8, 70); }
   322	    if (kit.role === 'tank') { a.barrier = 5; areaDamage(a, 115, 32); }
   323	    if (kit.role === 'bomber') for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) addBounded(game.mines, { x: a.x + randomRange(-90, 90), y: a.y + randomRange(-90, 90), team: a.team, life: 10 }, LIMITS.mines);
   324	    if (kit.role === 'dasher') { const d = norm(a.aim.x, a.aim.y); for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { moveActor(a, d.x * 34, d.y * 34); areaDamage(a, 38, 13); burst(a.x, a.y, kit.color, 3, 60); } }
   325	    if (kit.role === 'engineer') addBounded(game.turrets, { x: a.x, y: a.y, team: a.team, life: 18, cooldown: 0 }, LIMITS.turrets);
   326	    if (kit.role === 'boomerang') for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++) { const aa = angle(a.aim) + i * .3; spawnSpecial(a, { x: Math.cos(aa), y: Math.sin(aa) }, 28, 470, { boomer: true, color: kit.color, life: 1.5 }); }
   327	  }
   328	
   329	  function spawnSpecial(a, dir, damage, speed, extra) {
   330	    const aa = angle(dir);
   331	    addBounded(game.projectiles, { x: a.x + Math.cos(aa) * 25, y: a.y + Math.sin(aa) * 25, vx: Math.cos(aa) * speed, vy: Math.sin(aa) * speed,
   332	      life: extra.life || 1, team: a.team, owner: a, damage, radius: 9, bomb: false, boomer: !!extra.boomer, returning: false, pierce: !!extra.pierce, color: extra.color || COLORS.gold, heal: false }, LIMITS.projectiles);
   333	  }
   334	
   335	  function areaDamage(source, radius, damage) {
   336	    for (const a of game.actors) if (a.alive && a.team !== source.team && dist(a, source) < radius) hit(a, damage, source);
   337	  }
   338	
   339	  function updateProjectiles(dt) {
   340	    for (let i = game.projectiles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   341	      const p = game.projectiles[i]; p.life -= dt;
   342	      if (p.boomer && p.life < .45) { p.returning = true; p.vx *= -.94; p.vy *= -.94; }
   343	      p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt;
   344	      let remove = p.life <= 0 || p.x < 0 || p.x > WORLD.w || p.y < 70 || p.y > WORLD.h;
   345	      if (!remove) {
   346	        for (const a of game.actors) {
   347	          if (!a.alive || a.team === p.team || p.owner === a) continue;
   348	          if (dist(p, a) < p.radius + a.r) { hit(a, p.damage, p.owner); if (p.bomb) explosion(p.x, p.y, p.owner); if (!p.pierce) remove = true; break; }
   349	        }
   350	        if (p.heal && !remove) {
   351	          for (const a of game.actors) if (a.alive && a.team === p.team && dist(p, a) < p.radius + a.r) { a.hp = Math.min(a.maxHp, a.hp + 22); textPop(a.x, a.y - 30, '+22', COLORS.green); remove = true; break; }
   352	        }
   353	        if (game.mode === 'heist' && !remove) {
   354	          const safeTeam = p.team ? 0 : 1, safe = { x: safeTeam ? 820 : 80, y: 700 };
   355	          if (Math.hypot(p.x - safe.x, p.y - safe.y) < 56) { game.safes[safeTeam] = Math.max(0, game.safes[safeTeam] - p.damage * .34); burst(p.x, p.y, p.color || COLORS.gold, 4, 55); if (!p.pierce) remove = true; }
   356	        }
   357	      }
   358	      if (remove) game.projectiles.splice(i, 1);
   359	    }
   360	  }
   361	
   362	  function explosion(x, y, source) {
   363	    burst(x, y, COLORS.red, 18, 130); game.shake = Math.max(game.shake, .24);
   364	    for (const a of game.actors) if (a.alive && a.team !== source.team && Math.hypot(a.x - x, a.y - y) < 70) hit(a, 24, source);
   365	  }
   366	
   367	  function hit(target, damage, source) {
   368	    if (!target || !target.alive) return;
   369	    const actual = target.barrier > 0 ? damage * .35 : damage;
   370	    target.hp -= actual; target.super = clamp(target.super + damage * .7, 0, 100);
   371	    burst(target.x, target.y, target.team ? COLORS.red : COLORS.blue, 3, 45);
   372	    if (target.hp <= 0) down(target, source);
   373	  }
   374	  function down(target) {
   375	    target.alive = false; target.respawn = 3; target.hp = 0;
   376	    for (let i = 0; i < target.gems; i++) dropGem(target.x + randomRange(-18, 18), target.y + randomRange(-18, 18));
   377	    target.gems = 0; burst(target.x, target.y, target.team ? COLORS.red : COLORS.blue, 24, 180); game.shake = Math.max(game.shake, .4); tone(90, .16, 'sawtooth', .04);
   378	  }
   379	  function respawn(a) {
   380	    a.alive = true; a.hp = a.maxHp; a.respawn = 0; a.x = a.team ? 770 : 130; a.y = 700 + randomRange(-70, 70); a.super = Math.max(a.super, 20); burst(a.x, a.y, a.team ? COLORS.red : COLORS.blue, 12, 90);
   381	  }
   382	
   383	  function updateMines(dt) {
   384	    for (let i = game.mines.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   385	      const m = game.mines[i]; m.life -= dt;
   386	      let trigger = m.life <= 0;
   387	      for (const a of game.actors) if (a.alive && a.team !== m.team && Math.hypot(a.x - m.x, a.y - m.y) < 34) trigger = true;
   388	      if (trigger) { const owner = { team: m.team, x: m.x, y: m.y }; explosion(m.x, m.y, owner); game.mines.splice(i, 1); }
   389	    }
   390	  }
   391	  function updateTurrets(dt) {
   392	    for (let i = game.turrets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   393	      const t = game.turrets[i]; t.life -= dt; t.cooldown -= dt;
   394	      if (t.life <= 0) { game.turrets.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   395	      const target = nearest(t, t.team, false);
   396	      if (target && t.cooldown <= 0 && dist(t, target) < 500) { t.cooldown = .78; const d = norm(target.x - t.x, target.y - t.y); spawnTurretShot(t, d); }
   397	    }
   398	  }
   399	  function spawnTurretShot(t, d) {
   400	    addBounded(game.projectiles, { x: t.x, y: t.y, vx: d.x * 480, vy: d.y * 480, life: 1.1, team: t.team, owner: t, damage: 9, radius: 6, color: COLORS.blue, bomb: false, boomer: false, returning: false, pierce: false, heal: false }, LIMITS.projectiles);
   401	  }
   402	
   403	  function collectGems() {
   404	    for (let gi = game.gems.length - 1; gi >= 0; gi--) {
   405	      const gem = game.gems[gi];
   406	      for (const a of game.actors) if (a.alive && dist(a, gem) < 30 && a.gems < 10) {
   407	        a.gems++; game.gems.splice(gi, 1); a.super = clamp(a.super + 5, 0, 100); textPop(a.x, a.y - 32, '+1 GEM', COLORS.gold); burst(gem.x, gem.y, COLORS.gold, 8, 85); tone(660, .08, 'triangle', .025); break;
   408	      }
   409	    }
   410	  }
   411	  function updateGemMode(dt) {
   412	    game.gemClock -= dt;
   413	    if (game.gemClock <= 0) { game.gemClock = 3.4; dropGem(450 + randomRange(-35, 35), 700 + randomRange(-35, 35)); burst(450, 700, COLORS.gold, 10, 70); }
   414	    const blue = teamGems(0), red = teamGems(1);
   415	    let lead = -1;
   416	    if (blue >= 10) lead = 0; if (red >= 10 && red > blue) lead = 1;
   417	    if (lead < 0) { game.holdTeam = -1; game.holdTimer = 15; }
   418	    else if (game.holdTeam !== lead) { game.holdTeam = lead; game.holdTimer = 15; game.flash = .25; }
   419	    else { game.holdTimer -= dt; if (game.holdTimer <= 0) finishMatch(lead === 0 ? 'win' : 'loss', 'GEM LOCK'); }
   420	  }
   421	  function updateHeistMode() {
   422	    if (game.safes[1] <= 0) finishMatch('win', 'SAFE CRACKED');
   423	    else if (game.safes[0] <= 0) finishMatch('loss', 'BASE BREACHED');
   424	  }
   425	  function decideByScore() {
   426	    if (game.mode === 'heist') return game.safes[1] > game.safes[0] ? 'win' : game.safes[1] < game.safes[0] ? 'loss' : 'draw';
   427	    const b = teamGems(0), r = teamGems(1); return b > r ? 'win' : r > b ? 'loss' : 'draw';
   428	  }
   429	  function finishMatch(result, reason) {
   430	    if (!game || game.status !== 'playing') return;
   431	    game.status = 'result'; game.result = result; game.resultReason = reason; resetInput(); cancelTimers();
   432	    if (result === 'win') { save.wins++; save.trophies = Math.min(10000, save.trophies + 2); }
   433	    if (result === 'loss') save.losses++;
   434	    if (game.mode === 'gem') save.bestGems = Math.max(save.bestGems, teamGems(0));
   435	    else save.bestHeist = Math.max(save.bestHeist, Math.round(100 - game.safes[1]));
   436	    saveProgress(); game.flash = result === 'win' ? 1 : .4; game.shake = .5; tone(result === 'win' ? 780 : 110, .3, 'triangle', .05);
   437	  }
   438	
   439	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   440	    for (let i = game.particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const p = game.particles[i]; p.life -= dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= .97; p.vy *= .97; if (p.life <= 0) game.particles.splice(i, 1); }
   441	    for (let i = game.texts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const t = game.texts[i]; t.life -= dt; t.y -= 25 * dt; if (t.life <= 0) game.texts.splice(i, 1); }
   442	  }
   443	
   444	  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r, fill, stroke) {
   445	    const rr = Math.min(r, Math.abs(w) / 2, Math.abs(h) / 2);
   446	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + rr, y); ctx.lineTo(x + w - rr, y); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + rr, rr); ctx.lineTo(x + w, y + h - rr); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - rr, y + h, rr); ctx.lineTo(x + rr, y + h); ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - rr, rr); ctx.lineTo(x, y + rr); ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + rr, y, rr); ctx.closePath(); if (fill) { ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill(); } if (stroke) { ctx.strokeStyle = stroke; ctx.stroke(); }
   447	  }
   448	  function txt(text, x, y, size, color, align, weight) {
   449	    ctx.font = (weight || 700) + ' ' + size + 'px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = color || COLORS.paper; ctx.textAlign = align || 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.fillText(text, x, y);
   450	  }
   451	  function draw() {
   452	    ctx.setTransform(scale, 0, 0, scale, 0, 0);
   453	    ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.ink; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   454	    if (!game || game.status === 'menu') drawMenu(); else { drawArena(); if (game.status === 'result') drawResult(); }
   455	    if (orientationBlocked) { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(4,10,17,.5)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
   456	  }
   457	
   458	  function drawMenu() {
   459	    const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, W, H); grad.addColorStop(0, '#10283a'); grad.addColorStop(1, '#07111c'); ctx.fillStyle = grad; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   460	    ctx.globalAlpha = .18; for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) { ctx.fillStyle = i % 2 ? COLORS.blue : COLORS.purple; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc((i * 97) % W, 100 + (i * 137) % H, 1 + (i % 4), 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); } ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   461	    txt('SCRAPPER', W / 2, 57, 31, COLORS.paper, 'center', 950); txt('SQUAD', W / 2, 89, 31, COLORS.blue, 'center', 950);
   462	    txt('3v3 FIELD TEST  /  FREE BY DESIGN', W / 2, 117, 10, COLORS.muted, 'center', 800);
   463	    txt('CHOOSE THE RUN', 20, 151, 11, COLORS.muted, 'left', 800);
   464	    const modeW = (W - 52) / 2;
   465	    modeCard(20, 165, modeW, 54, 'GEM HOARD', '10 gems + 15s lock', save.mode === 'gem');
   466	    modeCard(32 + modeW, 165, modeW, 54, 'HEIST', 'crack their safe', save.mode === 'heist');
   467	    txt('TROPHY ROAD  /  WIN +2', 20, 242, 11, COLORS.muted, 'left', 800);
   468	    drawRoad(20, 283);
   469	    txt('KIT BAY  /  FLAT POWER', 20, 319, 11, COLORS.muted, 'left', 800);
   470	    const cardW = (W - 52) / 4;
   471	    for (let i = 0; i < KITS.length; i++) {
   472	      const x = 10 + (i % 4) * (cardW + 4), y = 334 + Math.floor(i / 4) * 65;
   473	      kitCard(x, y, cardW, 56, i, save.selectedKit === i);
   474	    }
   475	    txt('BEST  ' + save.bestGems + ' gems  ·  ' + save.bestHeist + '% safe damage', W / 2, 484, 11, COLORS.muted, 'center', 700);
   476	    roundRect(20, H - 92, W - 40, 58, 18, COLORS.blue); txt('TAP TO ARM AUDIO  /  BEGIN RUN', W / 2, H - 63, 14, COLORS.ink, 'center', 950);
   477	    txt('One hint line. Eight free kits. Make the call.', W / 2, H - 16, 11, COLORS.muted, 'center', 650);
   478	  }
   479	  function modeCard(x, y, w, h, title, sub, active) { roundRect(x, y, w, h, 15, active ? '#1c5b78' : '#10202e', active ? COLORS.blue : '#263b4a'); txt(title, x + 12, y + 20, 12, active ? COLORS.paper : COLORS.muted, 'left', 900); txt(sub, x + 12, y + 39, 10, active ? '#c9f5ff' : '#71879b', 'left', 650); }
   480	  function kitCard(x, y, w, h, i, active) {
   481	    const k = KITS[i], open = unlocked(i); roundRect(x, y, w, h, 12, active ? '#1b5367' : '#0e1c29', active ? k.color : '#223443');
   482	    ctx.fillStyle = open ? k.color : '#324453'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x + 18, y + 28, 11, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   483	    txt(open ? k.name : 'LOCK', x + 34, y + 19, 10, open ? COLORS.paper : '#73879a', 'left', 900); txt(open ? k.role.toUpperCase() : ROAD[i] + 'T', x + 34, y + 37, 8, open ? k.color : '#607487', 'left', 800);
   484	  }
   485	  function drawRoad(x, y) {
   486	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#2b495b'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + 9, y, x + W - 29, y); ctx.stroke();
   487	    const gap = (W - 40) / 7;
   488	    for (let i = 0; i < ROAD.length; i++) { const px = x + 9 + i * gap, open = unlocked(i); ctx.fillStyle = open ? COLORS.gold : '#1d3544'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, y, 11, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); if (open) txt(i === 0 ? '★' : String(i), px, y, 9, COLORS.ink, 'center', 950); else txt(String(ROAD[i]), px, y, 7, COLORS.muted, 'center', 800); txt(i === 0 ? 'RAT' : KITS[i].name.slice(0, 4).toUpperCase(), px, y + 23, 7, open ? COLORS.paper : '#657c90', 'center', 800); }
   489	  }
   490	
   491	  function drawArena() {
   492	    const shakeX = game.shake > 0 ? randomRange(-game.shake * 5, game.shake * 5) : 0, shakeY = game.shake > 0 ? randomRange(-game.shake * 5, game.shake * 5) : 0;
   493	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(shakeX, shakeY); ctx.save(); ctx.translate(-camera.x, -camera.y);
   494	    const bg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, WORLD.h); bg.addColorStop(0, '#0d2737'); bg.addColorStop(1, '#091822'); ctx.fillStyle = bg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, WORLD.w, WORLD.h);
   495	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(126,186,208,.08)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   496	    for (let x = 0; x <= WORLD.w; x += 50) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 0); ctx.lineTo(x, WORLD.h); ctx.stroke(); }
   497	    for (let y = 0; y <= WORLD.h; y += 50) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(WORLD.w, y); ctx.stroke(); }
   498	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,214,107,.07)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(450, 700, 145 + Math.sin(game.time * 2) * 6, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,214,107,.33)'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.stroke();
   499	    for (const c of COVERS) { roundRect(c.x + 7, c.y + 8, c.w, c.h, 10, 'rgba(0,0,0,.25)'); roundRect(c.x, c.y, c.w, c.h, 10, '#1b3844', '#315868'); }
   500	    drawSafe(80, 700, 0, game.safes[0]); drawSafe(820, 700, 1, game.safes[1]);
   501	    for (const g of game.gems) { const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(game.time * 4 + g.pulse) * .12; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(g.x, g.y); ctx.rotate(Math.PI / 4); ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.gold; ctx.shadowColor = COLORS.gold; ctx.shadowBlur = 14; ctx.fillRect(-8 * pulse, -8 * pulse, 16 * pulse, 16 * pulse); ctx.restore(); }
   502	    for (const m of game.mines) { ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.red; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(m.x, m.y, 13, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffd2db'; ctx.stroke(); }
   503	    for (const t of game.turrets) { ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.blue2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(t.x, t.y, 18, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.blue; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.stroke(); ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(t.x, t.y); ctx.lineTo(t.x + 23, t.y); ctx.stroke(); }
   504	    for (const p of game.projectiles) { ctx.fillStyle = p.color || (p.team ? COLORS.red : COLORS.blue); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.radius, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
   505	    for (const a of game.actors) drawActor(a);
   506	    for (const p of game.particles) { ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(p.life / p.max, 0, 1); ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.fillRect(p.x - p.size / 2, p.y - p.size / 2, p.size, p.size); } ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   507	    for (const t of game.texts) { ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(t.life, 0, 1); txt(t.text, t.x, t.y, 10, t.color, 'center', 900); } ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   508	    ctx.restore(); ctx.restore();
   509	    drawHud();
   510	  }
   511	  function drawSafe(x, y, team, health) {
   512	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.fillStyle = team ? 'rgba(255,107,131,.16)' : 'rgba(93,215,255,.16)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 70, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); roundRect(-32, -37, 64, 74, 12, team ? '#54263a' : '#174056', team ? COLORS.red : COLORS.blue); roundRect(-22, -27, 44, 54, 8, '#0a1722'); ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.gold; ctx.fillRect(-5, -9, 10, 18); ctx.fillStyle = team ? COLORS.red : COLORS.blue; ctx.fillRect(-32, -50, 64 * clamp(health / 100, 0, 1), 5); ctx.restore();
   513	  }
   514	  function drawActor(a) {
   515	    if (!a.alive) { ctx.globalAlpha = .34; ctx.strokeStyle = a.team ? COLORS.red : COLORS.blue; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(a.x, a.y, 18, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; return; }
   516	    const k = KITS[a.kit]; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(a.x, a.y); ctx.rotate(angle(a.aim));
   517	    ctx.fillStyle = a.team ? COLORS.red2 : COLORS.blue2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, a.r + 3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = k.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, a.r - 3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#101c29'; ctx.fillRect(3, -5, 19, 10); ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.paper; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(7, -6, 3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   518	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.55)'; ctx.fillRect(a.x - 24, a.y - 35, 48, 4); ctx.fillStyle = a.team ? COLORS.red : COLORS.blue; ctx.fillRect(a.x - 24, a.y - 35, 48 * clamp(a.hp / a.maxHp, 0, 1), 4);
   519	    if (a.gems) txt(String(a.gems), a.x, a.y + 37, 11, COLORS.gold, 'center', 950);
   520	    if (a.human) { ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.paper; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(a.x, a.y, 29, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); }
   521	  }
   522	  function drawHud() {
   523	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(6,15,25,.88)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, 76);
   524	    txt(game.mode === 'gem' ? 'GEM HOARD' : 'HEIST', 14, 18, 11, COLORS.gold, 'left', 900);
   525	    const remain = Math.max(0, MAX_MATCH - game.time); txt(formatTime(remain), W / 2, 19, 18, COLORS.paper, 'center', 950);
   526	    txt('BLUE ' + teamGems(0), 14, 49, 12, COLORS.blue, 'left', 900); txt('RED ' + teamGems(1), W - 14, 49, 12, COLORS.red, 'right', 900);
   527	    if (game.mode === 'heist') { ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.blue; ctx.fillRect(W / 2 - 48, 35, 40 * clamp(game.safes[0] / 100, 0, 1), 5); ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.red; ctx.fillRect(W / 2 + 8, 35, 40 * clamp(game.safes[1] / 100, 0, 1), 5); }
   528	    const hint = game.holdTeam >= 0 ? (game.holdTeam === 0 ? 'BLUE LOCK  ' : 'RED LOCK  ') + game.holdTimer.toFixed(1) + 's' : game.hint;
   529	    txt(hint, W / 2, 68, 10, game.holdTeam >= 0 ? COLORS.gold : COLORS.muted, 'center', 750);
   530	    drawSticks(); drawSuper();
   531	    if (game.flash > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + clamp(game.flash * .13, 0, .13) + ')'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
   532	  }
   533	  function drawSticks() {
   534	    const baseY = H - 91, leftX = 74, rightX = W - 74;
   535	    stick(leftX, baseY, sticks.left, COLORS.blue); stick(rightX, baseY, sticks.right, COLORS.purple);
   536	  }
   537	  function stick(x, y, s, color) { ctx.globalAlpha = .38; ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 57, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = .5; ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x + s.x * 33, y + s.y * 33, 22, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.ink; ctx.globalAlpha = .35; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x + s.x * 33, y + s.y * 33, 10, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }
   538	  function drawSuper() {
   539	    const p = game.actors[0], x = W / 2, y = H - 91, ready = p && p.super >= 100; ctx.fillStyle = ready ? COLORS.gold : '#263f51'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 31, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = ready ? '#fff0a7' : '#496476'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.stroke(); txt(ready ? 'SUPER' : Math.floor(p.super) + '%', x, y, ready ? 9 : 10, ready ? COLORS.ink : COLORS.paper, 'center', 950); }
   540	  function drawResult() {
   541	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(4,10,17,.84)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 76, W, H - 76);
   542	    const win = game.result === 'win'; txt(win ? 'FIELD WON' : game.result === 'draw' ? 'FIELD EVEN' : 'FIELD LOST', W / 2, 150, 28, win ? COLORS.green : game.result === 'draw' ? COLORS.gold : COLORS.red, 'center', 950);
   543	    txt(game.resultReason, W / 2, 180, 12, COLORS.paper, 'center', 800);
   544	    txt(win ? '+2 TROPHIES' : game.result === 'draw' ? 'NO TROPHY CHANGE' : 'SCRAP THE PLAN', W / 2, 216, 15, COLORS.gold, 'center', 900);
   545	    txt('TROPHIES  ' + save.trophies + '   ·   WINS  ' + save.wins, W / 2, 247, 11, COLORS.muted, 'center', 750);
   546	    txt(game.mode === 'gem' ? 'BEST GEM HAUL  ' + save.bestGems : 'BEST SAFE DAMAGE  ' + save.bestHeist + '%', W / 2, 270, 11, COLORS.muted, 'center', 750);
   547	    txt('TROPHY ROAD', W / 2, 322, 11, COLORS.muted, 'center', 800); drawRoad(20, 356);
   548	    roundRect(20, H - 92, W - 40, 58, 18, win ? COLORS.green : COLORS.blue); txt('RUN AGAIN', W / 2, H - 63, 16, COLORS.ink, 'center', 950);
   549	    txt('Tap a kit or mode after the next drop.', W / 2, H - 16, 10, COLORS.muted, 'center', 700);
   550	  }
   551	  function formatTime(t) { const m = Math.floor(t / 60), s = Math.floor(t % 60); return m + ':' + String(s).padStart(2, '0'); }
   552	
   553	  function canvasPoint(e) { const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); return { x: (e.clientX - r.left) * W / r.width, y: (e.clientY - r.top) * H / r.height }; }
   554	  function setStick(name, p) { const baseX = name === 'left' ? 74 : W - 74, baseY = H - 91, dx = p.x - baseX, dy = p.y - baseY, l = Math.hypot(dx, dy), max = 57, m = Math.min(1, l / max); if (!l) { sticks[name].x = sticks[name].y = 0; return; } sticks[name].x = dx / l * m; sticks[name].y = dy / l * m; }
   555	  function inCircle(p, x, y, r) { return Math.hypot(p.x - x, p.y - y) <= r; }
   556	  function handleMenuTap(p) {
   557	    const modeW = (W - 52) / 2;
   558	    if (p.y >= 160 && p.y <= 229) { save.mode = p.x < W / 2 ? 'gem' : 'heist'; saveProgress(); tone(310, .05, 'sine', .02); return; }
   559	    if (p.y >= 330 && p.y <= 465) { const cardW = (W - 52) / 4, col = clamp(Math.floor((p.x - 10) / (cardW + 4)), 0, 3), row = p.y < 397 ? 0 : 1, i = row * 4 + col; if (unlocked(i)) { save.selectedKit = i; saveProgress(); tone(420, .05, 'sine', .02); } return; }
   560	    if (p.y >= H - 110) { unlockAudio(); newMatch(save.mode); }
   561	  }
   562	  function onPointerDown(e) {
   563	    e.preventDefault(); if (orientationBlocked) return; try { canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) { /* capture is optional */ } const p = canvasPoint(e);
   564	    if (!game || game.status === 'menu') { buttons.menu = e.pointerId; handleMenuTap(p); return; }
   565	    if (game.status === 'result') { if (p.y >= H - 115) { buttons.menu = e.pointerId; newMatch(game.mode); } return; }
