    56	      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    57	      if (typeof raw !== 'string' || !raw) return fallback;
    58	      const value = JSON.parse(raw);
    59	      if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return fallback;
    60	      for (const key of ['wins', 'kills', 'best']) {
    61	        if (typeof value[key] !== 'number') return fallback;
    62	        const n = value[key];
    63	        if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 999999 || !Number.isInteger(n)) return fallback;
    64	      }
    65	      return { wins: value.wins, kills: value.kills, best: value.best };
    66	    } catch (_) { return fallback; }
    67	  }
    68	  function saveStats(stats) {
    69	    try {
    70	      const clean = { wins: Number.isInteger(stats.wins) && stats.wins >= 0 ? stats.wins : 0, kills: Number.isInteger(stats.kills) && stats.kills >= 0 ? stats.kills : 0, best: Number.isInteger(stats.best) && stats.best >= 0 ? stats.best : 0 };
    71	      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(clean));
    72	    } catch (_) {}
    73	  }
    74	  function capPush(list, item, max) { list.push(item); if (list.length > max) list.splice(0, list.length - max); }
    75	  function clamp(n, min, max) { return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, n)); }
    76	  function dist(a, b) { return Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y); }
    77	  function angleTo(a, b) { return Math.atan2(b.y - a.y, b.x - a.x); }
    78	  function copyWeapon(kind) { const d = weaponDefs[kind]; return { kind, name: d.name, ammo: d.mag, reserve: d.reserve, cooldown: 0, reload: 0 }; }
    79	
    80	  class RNG {
    81	    constructor(seed) { this.seed = seed >>> 0; }
    82	    next() { this.seed = (this.seed * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0; return this.seed / 4294967296; }
    83	    range(a, b) { return a + (b - a) * this.next(); }
    84	    int(a, b) { return Math.floor(this.range(a, b + 1)); }
    85	    pick(list) { return list[Math.floor(this.next() * list.length)]; }
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  class Fallline {
    89	    constructor() {
    90	      this.stats = loadStats(); this.seed = 0xF4111E; this.rng = new RNG(this.seed);
    91	      this.viewW = 390; this.viewH = 700; this.scale = 1; this.camera = { x: 900, y: 900 };
    92	      this.state = 'active'; this.paused = false; this.lastFrame = performance.now(); this.matchTime = 0; this.alive = 24;
    93	      this.bots = []; this.loot = []; this.bullets = []; this.particles = []; this.feed = []; this.buildings = []; this.replayT = 0; this.deathShot = null; this.timers = new Set();
    94	      this.player = null; this.shake = 0; this.flash = 0; this.prevPhase = 0;
    95	      this.bind(); this.resize(); this.resetMatch(); requestAnimationFrame(t => this.frame(t));
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    bind() {
    99	      window.addEventListener('resize', () => this.resize());
   100	      window.addEventListener('orientationchange', () => this.resize());
   101	      window.addEventListener('blur', () => this.resetInput());
   102	      window.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
   103	        unlockAudio();
   104	        if (['w', 'a', 's', 'd', 'W', 'A', 'S', 'D', ' ', 'e', 'E', 'r', 'R', 'Enter'].includes(e.key)) e.preventDefault();
   105	        this.keysDown(e.key, true);
   106	      }, { passive: false });
   107	      window.addEventListener('keyup', e => this.keysDown(e.key, false));
   108	      this.bindPointer($('stick'), 'stick'); this.bindPointer($('fire'), 'fire'); this.bindPointer($('interact'), 'interact');
   109	      $('audioButton').addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { e.preventDefault(); unlockAudio(); });
   110	      $('requeue').addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { e.preventDefault(); unlockAudio(); this.resetMatch(); });
   111	      $('requeue').addEventListener('click', () => { unlockAudio(); this.resetMatch(); });
   112	      $('audioOverlay').addEventListener('pointerdown', () => unlockAudio(), { passive: true });
   113	      canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', () => unlockAudio(), { passive: true });
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    bindPointer(el, kind) {
   117	      el.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
   118	        e.preventDefault(); unlockAudio();
   119	        if (input[kind].id !== null) return;
   120	        if (input.pointers.size >= 8) return;
   121	        input[kind].id = e.pointerId; input.pointers.set(e.pointerId, kind);
   122	        try { el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {}
   123	        if (kind === 'fire') { input.fire.held = true; el.classList.add('held'); }
   124	        if (kind === 'interact') input.queued.interact = true;
   125	        if (kind === 'stick') this.updateStick(e);
   126	      }, { passive: false });
   127	      el.addEventListener('pointermove', e => { if (input[kind].id === e.pointerId && kind === 'stick') { e.preventDefault(); this.updateStick(e); } }, { passive: false });
   128	      const release = e => { if (input[kind].id !== e.pointerId) return; e.preventDefault(); this.releasePointer(kind, e.pointerId, el); };
   129	      el.addEventListener('pointerup', release, { passive: false }); el.addEventListener('pointercancel', release, { passive: false });
   130	    }
   131	
   132	    releasePointer(kind, id, el) {
   133	      input.pointers.delete(id); input[kind].id = null;
   134	      if (kind === 'fire') { input.fire.held = false; el.classList.remove('held'); }
   135	      if (kind === 'stick') { input.stick.x = 0; input.stick.y = 0; $('stickKnob').style.transform = 'translate(0, 0)'; }
   136	    }
   137	    updateStick(e) {
   138	      const r = $('stick').getBoundingClientRect(); const max = 31;
   139	      let x = e.clientX - (r.left + r.width / 2), y = e.clientY - (r.top + r.height / 2); const len = Math.hypot(x, y);
   140	      if (len > max) { x = x / len * max; y = y / len * max; }
   141	      input.stick.x = clamp(x / max, -1, 1); input.stick.y = clamp(y / max, -1, 1); $('stickKnob').style.transform = `translate(${x}px, ${y}px)`;
   142	    }
   143	    keysDown(key, down) {
   144	      if (down) input.keys.add(key.toLowerCase()); else input.keys.delete(key.toLowerCase());
   145	      if (down && key.toLowerCase() === 'e') input.queued.interact = true;
   146	      if (down && key.toLowerCase() === 'r') this.startReload(this.player, true);
   147	      if (down && key === 'Enter' && this.state !== 'active' && !this.isLandscape()) this.resetMatch();
   148	    }
   149	    resetInput() {
   150	      for (const [id, kind] of input.pointers) {
   151	        const el = kind === 'stick' ? $('stick') : kind === 'fire' ? $('fire') : $('interact');
   152	        try { el.releasePointerCapture(id); } catch (_) {}
   153	      }
   154	      input.keys.clear(); input.queued.interact = false; input.pointers.clear();
   155	      input.stick.id = null; input.stick.x = 0; input.stick.y = 0; input.fire.id = null; input.fire.held = false; input.interact.id = null;
   156	      $('stickKnob').style.transform = 'translate(0, 0)'; $('fire').classList.remove('held');
   157	    }
   158	    setTimer(fn, ms) { const id = setTimeout(() => { this.timers.delete(id); fn(); }, ms); this.timers.add(id); return id; }
   159	    clearTimers() { for (const id of this.timers) clearTimeout(id); this.timers.clear(); }
   160	
   161	    resize() {
   162	      this.viewW = Math.max(1, window.innerWidth); this.viewH = Math.max(1, window.innerHeight);
   163	      const longAxis = Math.max(this.viewW, this.viewH); this.scale = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2, 960 / longAxis);
   164	      canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(this.viewW * this.scale)); canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(this.viewH * this.scale));
   165	      canvas.style.width = `${this.viewW}px`; canvas.style.height = `${this.viewH}px`; ctx.setTransform(this.scale, 0, 0, this.scale, 0, 0);
   166	      const landscape = this.isLandscape(); this.paused = landscape;
   167	      $('rotateOverlay').setAttribute('aria-hidden', landscape ? 'false' : 'true');
   168	      if (landscape) this.resetInput();
   169	    }
   170	    isLandscape() { return this.viewW > this.viewH && this.viewW > 500; }
   171	
   172	    resetMatch() {
   173	      this.clearTimers(); this.resetInput(); this.rng = new RNG(this.seed); this.state = 'active'; this.matchTime = 0; this.prevPhase = 0; this.replayT = 0; this.deathShot = null; this.shake = 0; this.flash = 0; this.feed.length = 0; this.bullets.length = 0; this.particles.length = 0;
   174	      $('resultOverlay').setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); this.makeMap(); this.spawnActors(); this.updateHud();
   175	    }
   176	    makeMap() {
   177	      this.buildings.length = 0; this.loot.length = 0;
   178	      const layouts = [[180,180,170,110],[490,165,220,130],[850,130,150,220],[1240,190,230,120],[1500,410,120,220],[1210,520,210,160],[1500,850,170,130],[1210,1010,250,150],[1450,1260,180,220],[1090,1430,230,120],[650,1430,190,150],[180,1260,230,130],[120,850,150,210],[420,570,230,130],[730,790,170,170],[910,460,130,110]];
   179	      for (const [x, y, w, h] of layouts) this.buildings.push({ x, y, w, h, tone: this.rng.int(0, 2) });
   180	      for (let i = 0; i < 78; i++) {
   181	        let p = this.findOpen(70); if (!p) continue;
   182	        const roll = this.rng.next(); let type = roll < .44 ? 'weapon' : roll < .72 ? 'ammo' : roll < .88 ? 'armor' : 'med';
   183	        let kind = null; if (type === 'weapon') kind = this.rng.pick(['pistol', 'smg', 'rifle', 'shotgun']);
   184	        capPush(this.loot, { x: p.x, y: p.y, type, kind, taken: false, bob: this.rng.range(0, TAU) }, MAX_LOOT);
   185	      }
   186	    }
   187	    findOpen(margin) {
   188	      for (let tries = 0; tries < 20; tries++) { const p = { x: this.rng.range(margin, WORLD - margin), y: this.rng.range(margin, WORLD - margin) }; if (!this.blocked(p.x, p.y, 18)) return p; }
   189	      return null;
   190	    }
   191	    spawnActors() {
   192	      this.player = { x: 900, y: 900, r: 14, hp: 100, shield: 0, alive: true, weapon: copyWeapon('pistol'), medkits: 0, kills: 0, color: '#d7ff5c', name: 'YOU', lastAim: -Math.PI / 2, hitFlash: 0 };
   193	      if (this.blocked(this.player.x, this.player.y, 16)) { const p = this.findOpen(60); if (p) Object.assign(this.player, p); }
   194	      this.bots.length = 0;
   195	      for (let i = 0; i < 23; i++) {
   196	        let p = this.findActorSpawn(); if (!p) p = { x: 900 + (i % 5) * 35, y: 900 + Math.floor(i / 5) * 35 };
   197	        const personality = personalities[i % personalities.length]; const kind = this.rng.pick(['pistol', 'pistol', 'smg', 'rifle', 'shotgun']);
   198	        this.bots.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, r: 13, hp: 100, shield: this.rng.next() < .3 ? 25 : 0, alive: true, weapon: copyWeapon(kind), medkits: this.rng.next() < .25 ? 1 : 0, name: botNames[i], color: botColors[i % botColors.length], personality, target: null, wander: null, think: this.rng.range(0, 1), kills: 0, hitFlash: 0, dir: this.rng.range(0, TAU) });
   199	      }
   200	      this.alive = 24; this.camera.x = this.player.x; this.camera.y = this.player.y; this.addFeed('DROP COMPLETE — 23 RIVALS IN THE FALLLINE', 'system');
   201	    }
   202	    findActorSpawn() {
   203	      for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const p = this.findOpen(90); if (p && dist(p, this.player) > 180 && this.bots.every(b => dist(p, b) > 65)) return p; }
   204	      return this.findOpen(90) || { x: 200, y: 200 };
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    frame(now) {
   208	      const raw = Math.max(0, (now - this.lastFrame) / 1000); this.lastFrame = now; const dt = Math.min(raw, .05);
   209	      if (!this.isLandscape()) {
   210	        if (this.state === 'active') this.update(dt); else if (this.state === 'death') this.replayT += dt;
   211	      }
   212	      this.render(); requestAnimationFrame(t => this.frame(t));
   213	    }
   214	    update(dt) {
   215	      if (!this.player.alive) return;
   216	      this.matchTime = Math.min(MATCH_LENGTH, this.matchTime + dt); this.shake = Math.max(0, this.shake - dt * 2.8); this.flash = Math.max(0, this.flash - dt); this.player.hitFlash = Math.max(0, this.player.hitFlash - dt);
   217	      for (const b of this.bots) b.hitFlash = Math.max(0, b.hitFlash - dt);
   218	      const phase = this.stormPhase(); if (phase !== this.prevPhase && this.matchTime > 0) { this.prevPhase = phase; this.addFeed(`STORM PHASE ${phase}/4 — MOVE WITH THE RING`, 'storm'); beep(120, .12, 'sawtooth', .025); }
   219	      this.updatePlayer(dt); for (const bot of this.bots) if (bot.alive) this.updateBot(bot, dt); this.updateBullets(dt); this.updateStorm(dt); this.updateParticles(dt);
   220	      this.camera.x += (this.player.x - this.camera.x) * Math.min(1, dt * 8); this.camera.y += (this.player.y - this.camera.y) * Math.min(1, dt * 8);
   221	      this.updateHud();
   222	      if (this.matchTime >= MATCH_LENGTH && this.player.alive) this.win();
   223	      if (this.alive <= 1 && this.player.alive) this.win();
   224	    }
   225	    movementInput() {
   226	      let x = input.stick.x, y = input.stick.y; if (!x && !y) { x = (input.keys.has('d') ? 1 : 0) - (input.keys.has('a') ? 1 : 0); y = (input.keys.has('s') ? 1 : 0) - (input.keys.has('w') ? 1 : 0); }
   227	      const len = Math.hypot(x, y); return len > 1 ? { x: x / len, y: y / len } : { x, y };
   228	    }
   229	    updatePlayer(dt) {
   230	      const p = this.player; p.weapon.cooldown = Math.max(0, p.weapon.cooldown - dt); const m = this.movementInput(); this.moveEntity(p, m.x * 210 * dt, m.y * 210 * dt); if (m.x || m.y) p.lastAim = Math.atan2(m.y, m.x);
   231	      this.startReload(p); if (input.fire.held || input.keys.has(' ')) this.shoot(p, true); if (input.queued.interact) { input.queued.interact = false; this.interact(p); }
   232	      if (p.weapon.reload > 0) { p.weapon.reload -= dt; if (p.weapon.reload <= 0) this.finishReload(p); }
   233	    }
   234	    updateBot(bot, dt) {
   235	      bot.think -= dt; bot.weapon.cooldown = Math.max(0, bot.weapon.cooldown - dt);
   236	      if (bot.weapon.reload > 0) { bot.weapon.reload -= dt; if (bot.weapon.reload <= 0) this.finishReload(bot); }
   237	      if (bot.think <= 0) { bot.think = bot.personality === 'hunter' ? .25 : .55 + this.rng.range(0, .4); bot.target = this.findNearestTarget(bot); bot.wander = this.botDestination(bot); }
   238	      const target = bot.target && bot.target.alive ? bot.target : null; const targetDist = target ? dist(bot, target) : 9999; let dx = 0, dy = 0;
   239	      if (target && targetDist < 760 && this.hasLine(bot, target)) {
   240	        const a = angleTo(bot, target); const want = bot.personality === 'camper' ? (targetDist < 230 ? a + Math.PI : 0) : bot.personality === 'hunter' ? a : targetDist < 250 ? a + Math.PI : a + Math.PI / 2;
   241	        if (bot.personality === 'camper' && targetDist > 170) { dx = Math.cos(bot.dir); dy = Math.sin(bot.dir); } else { dx = Math.cos(want); dy = Math.sin(want); }
   242	        if (targetDist <= weaponDefs[bot.weapon.kind].range * 1.05) this.shoot(bot, false, target);
   243	      } else if (bot.wander) { const a = angleTo(bot, bot.wander); dx = Math.cos(a); dy = Math.sin(a); }
   244	      if (bot.personality === 'edge-runner') { const r = this.safeRadius(); const d = Math.hypot(bot.x - WORLD / 2, bot.y - WORLD / 2); if (d < r - 80) { const a = Math.atan2(bot.y - WORLD / 2, bot.x - WORLD / 2); dx = Math.cos(a); dy = Math.sin(a); } }
   245	      if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > .1) this.moveEntity(bot, dx * (bot.personality === 'hunter' ? 138 : 108) * dt, dy * (bot.personality === 'hunter' ? 138 : 108) * dt);
   246	      this.botLoot(bot); this.startReload(bot);
   247	    }
   248	    botDestination(bot) {
   249	      if (bot.personality === 'looter') { const loot = this.nearestLoot(bot); if (loot) return loot; }
   250	      if (bot.personality === 'edge-runner') { const a = this.rng.range(0, TAU), r = Math.max(90, this.safeRadius() - 50); return { x: WORLD / 2 + Math.cos(a) * r, y: WORLD / 2 + Math.sin(a) * r }; }
   251	      if (bot.personality === 'camper') return { x: clamp(bot.x + this.rng.range(-80, 80), 50, WORLD - 50), y: clamp(bot.y + this.rng.range(-80, 80), 50, WORLD - 50) };
   252	      return { x: this.rng.range(80, WORLD - 80), y: this.rng.range(80, WORLD - 80) };
   253	    }
   254	    findNearestTarget(me) { let best = null, bestD = Infinity; const candidates = [this.player, ...this.bots]; for (const e of candidates) if (e !== me && e.alive) { const d = dist(me, e); if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; best = e; } } return best; }
   255	    nearestLoot(e) { let best = null, bestD = 330; for (const item of this.loot) if (!item.taken) { const d = dist(e, item); if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; best = item; } } return best; }
   256	    botLoot(bot) { const item = this.nearestLoot(bot); if (!item || dist(bot, item) > 31) return; item.taken = true; if (item.type === 'weapon' && item.kind && weaponDefs[item.kind].tier >= weaponDefs[bot.weapon.kind].tier) bot.weapon = copyWeapon(item.kind); else if (item.type === 'ammo') bot.weapon.reserve = Math.min(180, bot.weapon.reserve + 24); else if (item.type === 'armor') bot.shield = Math.min(50, bot.shield + 25); else if (item.type === 'med') bot.medkits = Math.min(2, bot.medkits + 1); }
   257	
   258	    shoot(shooter, isPlayer, target) {
   259	      if (!shooter.alive || shooter.weapon.reload > 0 || shooter.weapon.cooldown > 0) return;
   260	      const d = weaponDefs[shooter.weapon.kind]; if (shooter.weapon.ammo <= 0) { this.startReload(shooter); return; }
   261	      shooter.weapon.ammo--; shooter.weapon.cooldown = d.rate; const aim = target && target.alive ? angleTo(shooter, target) : shooter.lastAim || 0; shooter.lastAim = aim;
   262	      for (let i = 0; i < d.pellets; i++) {
   263	        const spread = (this.rng.next() - .5) * d.spread; const a = aim + spread; const bullet = { x: shooter.x + Math.cos(a) * 18, y: shooter.y + Math.sin(a) * 18, px: shooter.x, py: shooter.y, vx: Math.cos(a) * d.speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * d.speed, ttl: d.range / d.speed, damage: d.damage, owner: shooter, color: d.color, from: { x: shooter.x, y: shooter.y } }; capPush(this.bullets, bullet, MAX_BULLETS); }
   264	      this.burst(shooter.x + Math.cos(aim) * 17, shooter.y + Math.sin(aim) * 17, d.color, 3); if (isPlayer) { this.shake = Math.min(1, this.shake + .12); beep(d.short === 'SHOTGUN' ? 92 : 180, .045, 'square', .035); }
   265	    }
   266	    startReload(e, force) { if (!e || !e.alive || e.weapon.reload > 0 || e.weapon.reserve <= 0 || (!force && e.weapon.ammo > 0) || e.weapon.ammo >= weaponDefs[e.weapon.kind].mag) return; e.weapon.reload = .76; }
   267	    finishReload(e) { if (!e || !e.weapon) return; const d = weaponDefs[e.weapon.kind]; const take = Math.min(d.mag - e.weapon.ammo, e.weapon.reserve); e.weapon.ammo += take; e.weapon.reserve -= take; e.weapon.reload = 0; if (e === this.player) beep(440, .05, 'triangle', .02); }
   268	    updateBullets(dt) {
   269	      for (let i = this.bullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   270	        const b = this.bullets[i]; b.px = b.x; b.py = b.y; b.x += b.vx * dt; b.y += b.vy * dt; b.ttl -= dt;
   271	        if (b.ttl <= 0 || b.x < 0 || b.y < 0 || b.x > WORLD || b.y > WORLD || this.blocked(b.x, b.y, 1)) { this.burst(b.x, b.y, '#bdcec2', 2); this.bullets.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   272	        let hit = null; for (const e of [this.player, ...this.bots]) if (e !== b.owner && e.alive && this.segmentDistance(b.px, b.py, b.x, b.y, e.x, e.y) < e.r + 3) { hit = e; break; }
   273	        if (hit) { this.damage(hit, b.damage, b.owner, b); this.burst(hit.x, hit.y, b.color, 7); this.bullets.splice(i, 1); }
   274	      }
   275	    }
   276	    segmentDistance(x1, y1, x2, y2, px, py) { const dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1, len = dx * dx + dy * dy; const t = len ? clamp(((px - x1) * dx + (py - y1) * dy) / len, 0, 1) : 0; return Math.hypot(px - (x1 + t * dx), py - (y1 + t * dy)); }
   277	    damage(target, amount, owner, bullet) {
   278	      if (!target.alive) return; let left = amount; if (target.shield > 0) { const absorbed = Math.min(target.shield, left); target.shield -= absorbed; left -= absorbed; } target.hp -= left; target.hitFlash = .14;
   279	      if (target === this.player) { this.flash = .12; this.shake = Math.min(1, this.shake + .22); beep(75, .07, 'sawtooth', .03); }
   280	      if (target.hp <= 0) this.eliminate(target, owner, bullet);
   281	    }
   282	    eliminate(victim, killer, bullet) {
   283	      if (!victim.alive) return; victim.alive = false; this.alive = Math.max(0, this.alive - 1); if (killer) { killer.kills = (killer.kills || 0) + 1; if (killer === this.player) { this.stats.kills++; saveStats(this.stats); } }
   284	      const killerName = killer ? killer.name : 'THE STORM'; this.addFeed(`${killerName} › ${victim.name}`, victim === this.player ? 'danger' : killer === this.player ? 'player' : 'bot'); this.burst(victim.x, victim.y, victim.color || '#fff', 22);
   285	      if (victim === this.player) { this.deathShot = bullet ? { from: bullet.from, to: { x: victim.x, y: victim.y }, color: bullet.color || '#ff8066', killer: killerName } : null; this.state = 'death'; this.replayT = 0; this.showResult(false); beep(48, .3, 'sawtooth', .04); }
   286	      else if (this.alive <= 1 && this.player.alive) this.win();
   287	    }
   288	    updateStorm(dt) {
   289	      const r = this.safeRadius(), center = { x: WORLD / 2, y: WORLD / 2 };
   290	      for (const e of [this.player, ...this.bots]) if (e.alive && dist(e, center) > r) { this.damage(e, 7 * dt, null, null); }
   291	    }
   292	    safeRadius() { const t = this.matchTime; for (let i = 0; i < stormStarts.length - 1; i++) if (t < stormStarts[i + 1]) { const a = (t - stormStarts[i]) / (stormStarts[i + 1] - stormStarts[i]); return stormRadii[i] + (stormRadii[i + 1] - stormRadii[i]) * clamp(a, 0, 1); } return stormRadii[4]; }
   293	    stormPhase() { let phase = 1; for (let i = 1; i < stormStarts.length - 1; i++) if (this.matchTime >= stormStarts[i]) phase = i + 1; return clamp(phase, 1, 4); }
   294	
   295	    interact(p) {
   296	      let best = null, bestD = 62; for (const item of this.loot) if (!item.taken) { const d = dist(p, item); if (d < bestD) { best = item; bestD = d; } }
   297	      if (!best) return; best.taken = true;
   298	      if (best.type === 'weapon' && best.kind) { const old = p.weapon.kind; p.weapon = copyWeapon(best.kind); this.addFeed(`FOUND ${weaponDefs[best.kind].name}`, 'loot'); if (old !== best.kind) this.flash = .07; }
   299	      if (best.type === 'ammo') { p.weapon.reserve = Math.min(240, p.weapon.reserve + 36); this.addFeed('AMMO +36', 'loot'); }
   300	      if (best.type === 'armor') { p.shield = Math.min(75, p.shield + 25); this.addFeed('ARMOR PLATE +25', 'loot'); }
   301	      if (best.type === 'med') { p.medkits = Math.min(3, p.medkits + 1); this.addFeed('MEDKIT SECURED', 'loot'); }
   302	      this.burst(best.x, best.y, '#d7ff5c', 10); beep(580, .08, 'triangle', .024);
   303	    }
   304	    updateParticles(dt) { for (let i = this.particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const p = this.particles[i]; p.life -= dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= .94; p.vy *= .94; if (p.life <= 0) this.particles.splice(i, 1); } }
   305	    burst(x, y, color, count) { for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) { const a = this.rng.range(0, TAU), s = this.rng.range(22, 110); capPush(this.particles, { x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s, life: this.rng.range(.18, .52), max: .52, color, size: this.rng.range(1, 3.5) }, MAX_PARTICLES); } }
   306	    addFeed(text, type) { capPush(this.feed, { text, type: type || 'bot', age: 0 }, MAX_FEED); }
   307	    win() { if (this.state !== 'active' || !this.player.alive) return; this.state = 'win'; this.stats.wins++; this.stats.best = Math.max(this.stats.best, this.player.kills); saveStats(this.stats); this.showResult(true); this.burst(this.player.x, this.player.y, '#d7ff5c', 45); beep(660, .12, 'triangle', .04); this.setTimer(() => beep(880, .18, 'triangle', .04), 130); }
   308	
   309	    updateHud() {
   310	      const p = this.player; if (!p) return; $('aliveReadout').textContent = `${this.alive} ALIVE`; $('healthReadout').textContent = `${Math.max(0, Math.ceil(p.hp))}`; $('shieldReadout').textContent = `${Math.max(0, Math.ceil(p.shield))}`;
   311	      $('healthBar').style.width = `${clamp(p.hp, 0, 100)}%`; $('shieldBar').style.width = `${clamp(p.shield, 0, 75) / .75}%`; $('weaponReadout').textContent = p.weapon.reload > 0 ? 'RELOADING…' : weaponDefs[p.weapon.kind].name.toUpperCase(); $('ammoReadout').textContent = p.weapon.ammo; $('reserveReadout').textContent = `/ ${p.weapon.reserve}`; $('medReadout').textContent = `MEDKITS ${p.medkits}`;
   312	      const phase = this.stormPhase(), next = phase < 4 ? stormStarts[phase] : MATCH_LENGTH; const remain = Math.max(0, next - this.matchTime); $('stormReadout').textContent = `STORM PHASE ${phase}/4`; $('stormTimer').textContent = phase < 4 ? `COLLAPSES ${this.clock(remain)}` : 'FINAL RING'; $('matchTimer').textContent = this.clock(Math.max(0, MATCH_LENGTH - this.matchTime));
   313	      const loot = this.nearestLoot(p), prompt = loot && dist(p, loot) < 62; $('interactPrompt').style.opacity = prompt ? '1' : '0'; $('interactPrompt').textContent = prompt ? `E  ${loot.type === 'weapon' ? weaponDefs[loot.kind].short : loot.type.toUpperCase()}` : 'INTERACT';
   314	      const target = this.findNearestTarget(p), reticle = $('reticle'); if (target && dist(p, target) < 840) { const s = this.toScreen(target.x, target.y); reticle.style.left = `${s.x}px`; reticle.style.top = `${s.y}px`; reticle.style.opacity = '1'; } else reticle.style.opacity = '0';
   315	      this.feed.forEach(f => f.age += .016); $('feed').innerHTML = this.feed.map(f => `<div class="${f.type === 'bot' ? 'bot' : ''}">${this.escape(f.text)}</div>`).join('');
   316	    }
   317	    showResult(won) {
   318	      const placement = won ? 1 : Math.min(24, this.alive + 1); $('resultOverlay').setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false'); $('resultEyebrow').textContent = won ? 'LAST SIGNAL STANDING' : 'LINE LOST'; $('resultTitle').textContent = won ? 'YOU HELD THE FALLLINE' : 'RUN ENDED'; $('resultCopy').textContent = won ? 'The ring closed. Every rival is down.' : `Eliminated by ${this.deathShot ? this.deathShot.killer : 'the storm'}. No spectating — queue another drop.`; $('placementReadout').textContent = `#${placement}`; $('killsReadout').textContent = this.player.kills; $('timeReadout').textContent = this.clock(this.matchTime); $('careerReadout').textContent = `${this.stats.wins} WINS · ${this.stats.kills} KILLS`; }
   319	    clock(seconds) { const s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(seconds)); return `${String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`; }
   320	    escape(value) { return String(value).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c])); }
   321	
   322	    moveEntity(e, dx, dy) {
   323	      const nx = clamp(e.x + dx, e.r + 10, WORLD - e.r - 10); if (!this.blocked(nx, e.y, e.r)) e.x = nx;
   324	      const ny = clamp(e.y + dy, e.r + 10, WORLD - e.r - 10); if (!this.blocked(e.x, ny, e.r)) e.y = ny;
   325	    }
   326	    blocked(x, y, r) { for (const b of this.buildings) if (x > b.x - r && x < b.x + b.w + r && y > b.y - r && y < b.y + b.h + r) return true; return false; }
   327	    hasLine(a, b) { const d = dist(a, b), steps = Math.ceil(d / 32); for (let i = 1; i < steps; i++) { const t = i / steps; if (this.blocked(a.x + (b.x - a.x) * t, a.y + (b.y - a.y) * t, 2)) return false; } return true; }
   328	    toScreen(x, y) { return { x: x - this.camera.x + this.viewW / 2, y: y - this.camera.y + this.viewH / 2 }; }
   329	    drawWorld() {
   330	      const shakeX = Math.sin(this.matchTime * 91.1) * this.shake * 4, shakeY = Math.cos(this.matchTime * 77.3) * this.shake * 4; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(shakeX, shakeY);
   331	      ctx.fillStyle = '#253b31'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.viewW, this.viewH);
   332	      const left = this.camera.x - this.viewW / 2 - 60, top = this.camera.y - this.viewH / 2 - 60;
   333	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#8fbe7540'; ctx.lineWidth = 1; for (let x = Math.floor(left / 90) * 90; x < left + this.viewW + 120; x += 90) { const s = this.toScreen(x, 0); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(s.x, 0); ctx.lineTo(s.x, this.viewH); ctx.stroke(); } for (let y = Math.floor(top / 90) * 90; y < top + this.viewH + 120; y += 90) { const s = this.toScreen(0, y); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, s.y); ctx.lineTo(this.viewW, s.y); ctx.stroke(); }
   334	      for (const b of this.buildings) this.drawBuilding(b); this.drawLandmarks(); for (const item of this.loot) if (!item.taken) this.drawLoot(item); for (const b of this.bots) if (b.alive) this.drawActor(b, false); if (this.player.alive) this.drawActor(this.player, true); for (const b of this.bullets) this.drawBullet(b); for (const p of this.particles) this.drawParticle(p);
   335	      this.drawStorm(); ctx.restore();
   336	    }
   337	    drawBuilding(b) { const s = this.toScreen(b.x, b.y); ctx.fillStyle = ['#1a2b2b', '#203030', '#172727'][b.tone]; ctx.fillRect(s.x, s.y, b.w, b.h); ctx.strokeStyle = '#a9d0aa55'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.strokeRect(s.x + 2, s.y + 2, b.w - 4, b.h - 4); ctx.fillStyle = '#c4e5af18'; for (let x = s.x + 20; x < s.x + b.w - 10; x += 34) for (let y = s.y + 19; y < s.y + b.h - 8; y += 34) ctx.fillRect(x, y, 12, 7); ctx.fillStyle = '#07131366'; ctx.fillRect(s.x + 7, s.y + b.h / 2 - 5, 15, 10); }
   338	    drawLandmarks() { const points = [[900,260,'NORTH RIDGE'],[300,1020,'PINE FIELD'],[1030,1180,'DRY CREEK'],[1530,1050,'ECHO YARD']]; for (const [x,y,label] of points) { const s = this.toScreen(x,y); ctx.fillStyle = '#b8e28d25'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x,s.y,46,0,TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle='#b8d2a777'; ctx.font='9px Arial'; ctx.fillText(label,s.x-32,s.y+65); } }
   339	    drawLoot(item) { const s = this.toScreen(item.x, item.y), bob = Math.sin(this.matchTime * 3 + item.bob) * 3; const color = item.type === 'weapon' ? weaponDefs[item.kind].color : item.type === 'armor' ? '#69f2ce' : item.type === 'med' ? '#ff86b1' : '#ffc86b'; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(s.x, s.y + bob); ctx.shadowColor = color; ctx.shadowBlur = 12; ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 8, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = '#11201b'; ctx.font = 'bold 8px Arial'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.fillText(item.type === 'weapon' ? weaponDefs[item.kind].short[0] : item.type === 'armor' ? 'A' : item.type === 'med' ? '+' : '•', 0, 1); ctx.restore(); }
   340	    drawActor(e, player) { const s = this.toScreen(e.x, e.y); ctx.save(); ctx.translate(s.x,s.y); ctx.shadowColor = e.hitFlash > 0 ? '#fff' : e.color; ctx.shadowBlur = player ? 14 : 6; ctx.fillStyle = e.hitFlash > 0 ? '#fff' : e.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, e.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.strokeStyle = player ? '#f6ffbc' : '#0c1716'; ctx.lineWidth = player ? 3 : 2; ctx.stroke(); const a = e.lastAim || e.dir || 0; ctx.strokeStyle = '#e8f3e6'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*7, Math.sin(a)*7); ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*18, Math.sin(a)*18); ctx.stroke(); if (e.shield > 0) { ctx.strokeStyle='#69f2ceaa'; ctx.lineWidth=2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,0,e.r+4,-Math.PI*.8,Math.PI*.25); ctx.stroke(); } if (!player) { ctx.fillStyle='#06100f'; ctx.fillRect(-14,-23,28,3); ctx.fillStyle='#ff8066'; ctx.fillRect(-14,-23,28*clamp(e.hp/100,0,1),3); } ctx.restore(); }
   341	    drawBullet(b) { const a = Math.atan2(b.vy,b.vx), p = this.toScreen(b.x,b.y), q = this.toScreen(b.px,b.py); ctx.strokeStyle=b.color; ctx.globalAlpha=.9; ctx.lineWidth=2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(q.x,q.y);ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.stroke();ctx.globalAlpha=1; }
   342	    drawParticle(p) { const s=this.toScreen(p.x,p.y); ctx.globalAlpha=clamp(p.life/p.max,0,1);ctx.fillStyle=p.color;ctx.fillRect(s.x-p.size/2,s.y-p.size/2,p.size,p.size);ctx.globalAlpha=1; }
   343	    drawStorm() { const center=this.toScreen(WORLD/2,WORLD/2), r=this.safeRadius(); ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle='#5965c52e'; ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(0,0,this.viewW,this.viewH);ctx.arc(center.x,center.y,r,0,TAU,true);ctx.fill('evenodd'); ctx.strokeStyle='#92a5ffcc';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.setLineDash([12,8]);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(center.x,center.y,r,0,TAU);ctx.stroke();ctx.setLineDash([]);ctx.restore(); }
   344	    drawKillCam() { if (!this.deathShot || this.replayT > 2.6) return; const a=this.toScreen(this.deathShot.from.x,this.deathShot.from.y), b=this.toScreen(this.deathShot.to.x,this.deathShot.to.y), t=clamp(this.replayT/1.15,0,1); ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle=this.deathShot.color;ctx.shadowColor=this.deathShot.color;ctx.shadowBlur=12;ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.globalAlpha=.95;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(a.x,a.y);ctx.lineTo(a.x+(b.x-a.x)*t,a.y+(b.y-a.y)*t);ctx.stroke();ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(a.x+(b.x-a.x)*t,a.y+(b.y-a.y)*t,5,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.restore(); }
   345	    render() { ctx.setTransform(this.scale,0,0,this.scale,0,0); this.drawWorld(); if (this.state === 'death') this.drawKillCam(); if (this.flash > 0) { ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(255,100,70,${this.flash*1.5})`;ctx.fillRect(0,0,this.viewW,this.viewH); } }
   346	  }
   347	
   348	  new Fallline();
   349	})();

    39	  function validNum(v, fallback, min, max) {
    40	    return typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? clamp(v, min, max) : fallback;

    42	  function loadProfile() {
    43	    var fresh = { wins: 0, losses: 0, best: 0, mastery: {} };
    44	    try {
    45	      var raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    46	      if (!raw) return fresh;
    47	      var parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    48	      if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed)) return fresh;

    57	  }
    58	  function saveProfile() {
    59	    try {
    60	      var safeMastery = {};
    61	      heroes.forEach(function (hero) { safeMastery[hero.name] = Math.floor(validNum(profile.mastery[hero.name], 0, 0, 999999)); });
    62	      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
    63	        wins: Math.floor(validNum(profile.wins, 0, 0, 999999)),
    64	        losses: Math.floor(validNum(profile.losses, 0, 0, 999999)),
    65	        best: validNum(profile.best, 0, 0, 240), mastery: safeMastery
    66	      }));
    67	    } catch (e) { /* Storage is optional. */ }
    68	  }
    69	  function resize() {
    70	    W = Math.max(280, window.innerWidth || 390);
    71	    H = Math.max(420, window.innerHeight || 700);
    72	    dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    73	    var longAxis = Math.max(W, H);
    74	    if (longAxis * dpr > 960) dpr = 960 / longAxis;
    75	    canvas.width = Math.floor(W * dpr); canvas.height = Math.floor(H * dpr);
    76	    canvas.style.width = W + 'px'; canvas.style.height = H + 'px';
    77	    laneY = clamp(H * .49, 270, 405);
    78	  }
    79	  function unlockAudio() {
    80	    if (audio) { if (audio.state === 'suspended') audio.resume(); return; }
    81	    try { audio = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); } catch (e) { audio = null; }
    82	  }
    83	  function beep(kind) {
    84	    if (!audio) return;
    85	    try {
    86	      var now = audio.currentTime, o = audio.createOscillator(), g = audio.createGain();
    87	      var tones = { hit: [160, .045], cast: [420, .11], dash: [760, .1], win: [640, .28], lose: [90, .3], buy: [300, .09] };
    88	      var tone = tones[kind] || tones.hit;
    89	      o.type = kind === 'lose' ? 'sawtooth' : 'triangle'; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(tone[0], now);
    90	      o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(45, tone[0] * (kind === 'win' ? 1.7 : .62)), now + tone[1]);
    91	      g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001, now); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.055, now + .012); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001, now + tone[1]);
    92	      o.connect(g); g.connect(audio.destination); o.start(now); o.stop(now + tone[1] + .02);
    93	    } catch (e) { /* Audio is optional. */ }
    94	  }
    95	  function clearWorld() { entities.length = 0; particles.length = 0; floaters.length = 0; towers.length = 0; }
    96	  function resetInput() {
    97	    pointerMap.clear(); abilityPointer[0] = abilityPointer[1] = abilityPointer[2] = null;
    98	    stickId = null; stick.x = 0; stick.y = 0; keys.clear(); abilityQueue.length = 0;
    99	  }
   100	  function addEntity(e) {
   101	    if (entities.length >= MAX_ENTITIES) {
   102	      var removable = entities.findIndex(function (x) { return x.type === 'minion' && x.hp <= 0; });
   103	      if (removable >= 0) entities.splice(removable, 1); else return null;
   104	    }
   105	    e.id = idSeq++; e.alive = true; entities.push(e); return e;
   106	  }
   107	  function makeHero(team, x, y, hero, role, isPlayer) {
   108	    var difficulty = 1 + Math.min(.34, profile.wins * .055);
   109	    var hp = hero.hp * (isPlayer ? 1 : team === 'red' ? difficulty : 1);
   110	    return addEntity({ type: 'hero', team: team, x: x, y: y, hp: hp, maxHp: hp, radius: 16, hero: hero,
   111	      role: role, isPlayer: !!isPlayer, damage: hero.damage * (isPlayer ? 1 : team === 'red' ? difficulty : 1),
   112	      range: hero.range, speed: hero.speed, attackCd: .2 + Math.random() * .25, abilityCd: 0,
   113	      stun: 0, slow: 0, shield: 0, respawn: 0, face: team === 'blue' ? 1 : -1 });
   114	  }
   115	  function makeMinion(team, x, y, ranged) {
   116	    var difficulty = 1 + Math.min(.25, profile.wins * .04);
   117	    return addEntity({ type: 'minion', team: team, x: x, y: y + (Math.random() - .5) * 30, hp: (ranged ? 42 : 56) * (team === 'red' ? difficulty : 1),
   118	      maxHp: (ranged ? 42 : 56) * (team === 'red' ? difficulty : 1), radius: ranged ? 9 : 11, ranged: !!ranged,
   119	      damage: (ranged ? 7 : 9) * (team === 'red' ? difficulty : 1), range: ranged ? 95 : 34, speed: ranged ? 33 : 43,
   120	      attackCd: Math.random() * .7, stun: 0, slow: 0, face: team === 'blue' ? 1 : -1 });
   121	  }
   122	  function setupMatch() {
   123	    clearWorld(); resetInput(); elapsed = 0; nextWave = 0; waveNo = 0; result = ''; shake = 0; flash = 0;
   124	    var hero = heroes[selected];
   125	    var player = makeHero('blue', 104, laneY + 34, hero, 'player', true); player.items = { health: false, speed: false, power: false }; player.gold = 120;
   126	    makeHero('blue', 74, laneY - 34, heroes[0], 'guard', false);
   127	    makeHero('blue', 118, laneY - 69, heroes[4], 'support', false);
   128	    makeHero('red', 286, laneY + 32, heroes[5], 'bruiser', false);
   129	    makeHero('red', 320, laneY - 28, heroes[1], 'flank', false);
   130	    makeHero('red', 352, laneY + 58, heroes[2], 'caster', false);
   131	    towers = [
   132	      { team: 'blue', x: 42, y: laneY - 2, hp: 390, maxHp: 390, alive: true, far: false },
   133	      { team: 'blue', x: 92, y: laneY - 2, hp: 450, maxHp: 450, alive: true, far: true },
   134	      { team: 'red', x: W - 92, y: laneY - 2, hp: 450, maxHp: 450, alive: true, far: false },
   135	      { team: 'red', x: W - 42, y: laneY - 2, hp: 390, maxHp: 390, alive: true, far: true }
   136	    ];
   137	    mode = 'playing'; spawnWave(); nextWave = 20; beep('cast');
   138	  }
   139	  function spawnWave() {
   140	    if (entities.filter(function (e) { return e.type === 'minion'; }).length > 30) return;
   141	    waveNo++;
   142	    for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) { makeMinion('blue', 122 - i * 16, laneY + (i - 1) * 22, i === 2); makeMinion('red', W - 122 + i * 16, laneY + (i - 1) * 22, i === 2); }
   143	    addFloater(W / 2, laneY - 86, 'WAVE ' + waveNo, '#d7f2d4');
   144	  }
   145	  function playerEntity() { return entities.find(function (e) { return e.isPlayer; }); }
   146	  function teamAlive(team, kind) { return entities.filter(function (e) { return e.alive && e.team === team && (!kind || e.type === kind); }); }
   147	  function nearestEnemy(source, maxRange, filter) {
   148	    var best = null, bestD = maxRange == null ? Infinity : maxRange;
   149	    entities.forEach(function (e) {
   150	      if (!e.alive || e.team === source.team || e === source || (filter && !filter(e))) return;
   151	      var d = dist(source, e) - source.radius - e.radius;
   152	      if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; best = e; }
   153	    });
   154	    return best;
   155	  }
   156	  function nearestTower(source, maxRange) {
   157	    var best = null, bestD = maxRange == null ? Infinity : maxRange;
   158	    towers.forEach(function (t) {
   159	      if (!t.alive || t.team === source.team) return;
   160	      var d = Math.abs(t.x - source.x) - 16;
   161	      if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; best = t; }
   162	    });
   163	    return best;
   164	  }
   165	  function moveToward(e, tx, ty, amount, dt) {
   166	    var dx = tx - e.x, dy = ty - e.y, m = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   167	    var slow = e.slow > 0 ? .58 : 1;
   168	    e.x += dx / m * amount * slow * dt; e.y += dy / m * amount * slow * dt;
   169	    e.x = clamp(e.x, 25, W - 25); e.y = clamp(e.y, laneY - 78, laneY + 78);
   170	    e.face = dx >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
   171	  }
   172	  function hurt(target, amount, source, text) {
   173	    if (!target || !target.alive) return;
   174	    if (target.shield > 0) { var blocked = Math.min(target.shield, amount); target.shield -= blocked; amount -= blocked; }
   175	    if (amount <= 0) { addFloater(target.x, target.y - 24, 'BLOCK', '#baf6ed'); return; }
   176	    target.hp -= amount; target.lastHitBy = source || null; addParticles(target.x, target.y, target.team === 'blue' ? '#ffcf80' : '#ff8f9d', 6, 55);
   177	    if (text) addFloater(target.x, target.y - 25, text, '#fff1ae');
   178	    shake = Math.min(10, shake + 1.6); flash = Math.min(.18, flash + .05); beep('hit');
   179	    if (target.hp <= 0) killEntity(target, source);
   180	  }
   181	  function killEntity(e, source) {
   182	    if (!e.alive) return;
   183	    e.alive = false; addParticles(e.x, e.y, e.team === 'blue' ? '#65d8c3' : '#ff6f86', 18, 120); shake = Math.min(14, shake + 4);
   184	    if (source && source.isPlayer && e.type === 'minion') { source.gold = Math.min(9999, source.gold + 22); addFloater(source.x, source.y - 34, '+22 GOLD', '#ffd56a'); }
   185	    if (e.type === 'hero') {
   186	      if (e.isPlayer) { e.respawn = 5; e.alive = true; e.hp = 1; addFloater(e.x, e.y - 28, 'DOWN 5s', '#ffb0a8'); }
   187	      else if (source && source.isPlayer) { source.gold = Math.min(9999, source.gold + 65); addFloater(source.x, source.y - 34, '+65 GOLD', '#ffd56a'); }
   188	    }
   189	  }
   190	  function doBasicAttack(e) {
   191	    if (!e.alive || e.respawn > 0 || e.attackCd > 0 || e.stun > 0) return;
   192	    var target = nearestEnemy(e, e.range, function (x) { return x.type === 'hero' || x.type === 'minion'; });
   193	    if (target) { hurt(target, e.damage, e, '-' + Math.round(e.damage)); addParticles(target.x, target.y, e.team === 'blue' ? '#b8fff0' : '#ffb0b8', 4, 35); e.attackCd = e.isPlayer ? e.hero.cool : .82; return; }
   194	    var tower = nearestTower(e, e.range + 20);
   195	    if (tower) { tower.hp -= e.damage; tower.lastHitBy = e; e.attackCd = e.isPlayer ? e.hero.cool : .92; addParticles(tower.x, tower.y - 20, '#f3cc78', 4, 28); shake = Math.min(8, shake + 1); if (tower.hp <= 0) { tower.alive = false; addFloater(tower.x, tower.y - 36, 'TOWER DOWN', '#ffe09b'); addParticles(tower.x, tower.y - 20, '#ffd36c', 32, 150); } }
   196	  }
   197	  function useAbility(player, n) {
   198	    if (!player || !player.alive || player.respawn > 0 || player.abilityCd > 0 || player.stun > 0) return;
   199	    var kit = player.hero.kit, target, list, i, d;
   200	    if (kit === 'hook') {
   201	      target = nearestEnemy(player, 235, function (x) { return x.type === 'hero' || x.type === 'minion'; });
   202	      if (!target) return;
   203	      target.x = lerp(target.x, player.x + player.face * 26, .36); target.y = lerp(target.y, player.y, .42); hurt(target, 22, player, 'HOOK'); target.stun = 1.15;
   204	      addParticles(target.x, target.y, '#9af0be', 16, 90); player.abilityCd = 8; beep('cast');
   205	    } else if (kit === 'dash') {
   206	      target = nearestEnemy(player, 215, function (x) { return x.type === 'hero' || x.type === 'minion'; });
   207	      if (!target) return;
   208	      var oldx = player.x; player.x = clamp(target.x - player.face * 34, 25, W - 25); player.y = target.y; hurt(target, 32, player, 'BITE');
   209	      addParticles(oldx, player.y, '#ff9fd3', 20, 150); player.abilityCd = 6.5; beep('dash');
   210	    } else if (kit === 'burst') {
   211	      target = nearestEnemy(player, 225, function (x) { return x.type === 'hero' || x.type === 'minion'; });
   212	      if (!target) return;
   213	      entities.forEach(function (e) { if (e.alive && e.team !== player.team && Math.hypot(e.x - target.x, e.y - target.y) < 62) hurt(e, 27, player, 'BURST'); });
   214	      addParticles(target.x, target.y, '#ffb36e', 32, 125); player.abilityCd = 7; beep('cast');
   215	    } else if (kit === 'shot') {
   216	      target = nearestEnemy(player, 390, function (x) { return x.type === 'hero' || x.type === 'minion'; });
   217	      if (!target) return;
   218	      hurt(target, 36, player, 'ECHO'); addParticles(target.x, target.y, '#fff09e', 22, 180); player.abilityCd = 5.8; beep('cast');
   219	    } else if (kit === 'shield') {
   220	      list = teamAlive(player.team, 'hero').filter(function (x) { return x.alive && x.respawn <= 0; });
   221	      target = list.sort(function (a, b) { return (a.hp + a.shield) / a.maxHp - (b.hp + b.shield) / b.maxHp; })[0] || player;
   222	      target.shield = Math.min(80, target.shield + 46); target.hp = Math.min(target.maxHp, target.hp + 12); addFloater(target.x, target.y - 28, 'SHIELDED', '#b5fff1');
   223	      addParticles(target.x, target.y, '#a3eff2', 22, 100); player.abilityCd = 7.4; beep('cast');
   224	    } else if (kit === 'spin') {
   225	      for (i = entities.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { var foe = entities[i]; if (foe.alive && foe.team !== player.team && Math.hypot(foe.x - player.x, foe.y - player.y) < 78) { hurt(foe, 25, player, 'SPIN'); foe.slow = 2.2; } }
   226	      addParticles(player.x, player.y, '#c7a5ff', 34, 130); player.abilityCd = 6.8; beep('dash');
   227	    }
   228	  }
   229	  function botThink(e, dt) {
   230	    if (!e.alive || e.respawn > 0 || e.stun > 0) return;
   231	    var own = teamAlive(e.team), foes = teamAlive(e.team === 'blue' ? 'red' : 'blue');
   232	    var low = e.hp / e.maxHp < .27, nearFoe = nearestEnemy(e, 165, function (x) { return x.type === 'hero' || x.type === 'minion'; });
   233	    var ownPower = own.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + x.hp / x.maxHp; }, 0), foePower = foes.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + x.hp / x.maxHp; }, 0);
   234	    var home = e.team === 'blue' ? 65 : W - 65;
   235	    if (low) { moveToward(e, home, laneY + (e.role === 'support' ? -42 : 38), e.speed * 1.1, dt); return; }
   236	    if (e.role === 'support') {
   237	      var ally = own.filter(function (x) { return x !== e && x.type === 'hero' && x.hp / x.maxHp < .68; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.hp / a.maxHp - b.hp / b.maxHp; })[0];
   238	      if (ally && dist(e, ally) > 80) moveToward(e, ally.x, ally.y, e.speed, dt);
   239	      else if (e.abilityCd <= 0) useBotShield(e, ally || e);
   240	    } else if (nearFoe) {
   241	      if (e.role === 'flank' && e.abilityCd <= 0 && ownPower > foePower * .8) useBotDash(e, nearFoe);
   242	      else if (e.role === 'caster' && e.abilityCd <= 0) useBotBurst(e, nearFoe);
   243	      else if (dist(e, nearFoe) > e.range * .72) moveToward(e, nearFoe.x, nearFoe.y, e.speed, dt);
   244	    } else {
   245	      var tower = nearestTower(e, e.range + 30);
   246	      var canDive = ownPower > foePower * 1.16 && e.hp / e.maxHp > .63;
   247	      if (tower && (!tower.far || canDive)) { if (Math.abs(tower.x - e.x) > e.range) moveToward(e, tower.x, laneY, e.speed, dt); }
   248	      else moveToward(e, e.team === 'blue' ? W - 70 : 70, laneY + (e.role === 'guard' ? 30 : -15), e.speed, dt);
   249	    }
   250	    doBasicAttack(e);
   251	  }
   252	  function useBotDash(e, t) { e.x = clamp(t.x - e.face * 31, 25, W - 25); hurt(t, 19, e, 'DASH'); e.abilityCd = 7; addParticles(e.x, e.y, '#ff9fd3', 10, 75); }
   253	  function useBotBurst(e, t) { entities.forEach(function (x) { if (x.alive && x.team !== e.team && Math.hypot(x.x - t.x, x.y - t.y) < 48) hurt(x, 16, e, 'POP'); }); e.abilityCd = 8; addParticles(t.x, t.y, '#ffb36e', 14, 75); }
   254	  function useBotShield(e, t) { t.shield = Math.min(70, t.shield + 30); e.abilityCd = 8; addParticles(t.x, t.y, '#a3eff2', 10, 55); }
   255	  function update(dt) {
   256	    if (mode !== 'playing') return;
   257	    elapsed += dt; if (elapsed >= 240) { finish(false); return; }
   258	    if (elapsed >= nextWave) { spawnWave(); nextWave = elapsed + 20; }
   259	    var player = playerEntity();
   260	    if (player) {
   261	      if (player.respawn > 0) { player.respawn -= dt; if (player.respawn <= 0) { player.hp = player.maxHp; player.x = 104; player.y = laneY + 34; player.shield = 0; addFloater(player.x, player.y - 25, 'BACK IN', '#baf6ed'); } }
   262	      else if (player.stun <= 0) {
   263	        var vx = stick.x, vy = stick.y;
   264	        if (keys.has('a')) vx -= 1; if (keys.has('d')) vx += 1; if (keys.has('w')) vy -= 1; if (keys.has('s')) vy += 1;
   265	        var mag = Math.hypot(vx, vy); if (mag > 1) { vx /= mag; vy /= mag; }
   266	        var speed = player.speed * (player.items.speed ? 1.2 : 1); player.x = clamp(player.x + vx * speed * dt, 25, W - 25); player.y = clamp(player.y + vy * speed * dt, laneY - 78, laneY + 78); if (vx) player.face = vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
   267	        while (abilityQueue.length) useAbility(player, abilityQueue.shift()); doBasicAttack(player);
   268	      }
   269	    }
   270	    entities.forEach(function (e) {
   271	      if (!e.alive) return;
   272	      e.attackCd = Math.max(0, e.attackCd - dt); e.abilityCd = Math.max(0, e.abilityCd - dt); e.stun = Math.max(0, e.stun - dt); e.slow = Math.max(0, e.slow - dt); e.shield = Math.max(0, e.shield - dt * 7);
   273	      if (e.type === 'minion') updateMinion(e, dt); else if (e.type === 'hero' && !e.isPlayer) botThink(e, dt);
   274	    });
   275	    entities = entities.filter(function (e) { return e.alive || (e.type === 'hero' && e.isPlayer); });
   276	    if (!towers[3].alive) { finish(true); return; }
   277	    if (!towers[1].alive) { finish(false); return; }
   278	    updateParticles(dt); updateFloaters(dt); shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 22); flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt);
   279	  }
   280	  function updateMinion(e, dt) {
   281	    if (e.stun > 0) return;
   282	    var target = nearestEnemy(e, e.range + 2, function (x) { return x.type === 'hero' || x.type === 'minion'; });
   283	    if (target) { if (dist(e, target) > e.range + 4) moveToward(e, target.x, target.y, e.speed, dt); doBasicAttack(e); return; }
   284	    var tower = nearestTower(e, e.range + 14);
   285	    if (tower) { if (Math.abs(tower.x - e.x) > e.range + 3) moveToward(e, tower.x, laneY, e.speed, dt); doBasicAttack(e); }
   286	    else moveToward(e, e.team === 'blue' ? W - 25 : 25, laneY, e.speed, dt);
   287	  }
   288	  function finish(win) {
   289	    if (mode !== 'playing') return;
   290	    mode = win ? 'win' : 'loss'; result = win ? 'RIDGE HELD' : 'LANE LOST';
   291	    if (win) { profile.wins++; profile.mastery[heroes[selected].name] = (profile.mastery[heroes[selected].name] || 0) + 1; if (!profile.best || elapsed < profile.best) profile.best = elapsed; beep('win'); }
   292	    else { profile.losses++; beep('lose'); }
   293	    saveProfile(); resetInput(); flash = .28; render();
   294	  }
   295	  function buy(index) {
   296	    var p = playerEntity(), item = shop[index]; if (!p || p.items[item.key] || p.gold < item.cost) return;
   297	    p.gold -= item.cost; p.items[item.key] = true; if (item.key === 'health') { p.maxHp += 55; p.hp += 55; } if (item.key === 'power') p.damage += 8; addFloater(p.x, p.y - 35, item.name, item.color); addParticles(p.x, p.y, item.color, 15, 85); beep('buy');
   298	  }
   299	  function addParticles(x, y, color, count, speed) {
   300	    count = Math.min(count, MAX_PARTICLES - particles.length); if (count <= 0) return;
   301	    for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) { var a = Math.random() * TAU, s = speed * (.35 + Math.random() * .65); particles.push({ x: x, y: y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s, life: .32 + Math.random() * .52, max: .85, color: color, size: 1.5 + Math.random() * 3.5 }); }
   302	  }
   303	  function addFloater(x, y, text, color) {
   304	    if (floaters.length >= MAX_FLOATERS) floaters.shift(); floaters.push({ x: x, y: y, text: String(text).slice(0, 24), color: color, life: 1.15 });
   305	  }
   306	  function updateParticles(dt) { particles = particles.filter(function (p) { p.life -= dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= .985; p.vy = p.vy * .985 + 20 * dt; return p.life > 0; }); }
   307	  function updateFloaters(dt) { floaters = floaters.filter(function (f) { f.life -= dt; f.y -= 17 * dt; return f.life > 0; }); }
   308	  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r, fill, stroke) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect ? ctx.roundRect(x, y, w, h, r) : (ctx.rect(x, y, w, h)); if (fill) { ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill(); } if (stroke) { ctx.strokeStyle = stroke; ctx.stroke(); } }
   309	  function text(str, x, y, size, color, align, weight) { ctx.font = (weight || 700) + ' ' + size + 'px ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace'; ctx.fillStyle = color || '#fff'; ctx.textAlign = align || 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.fillText(str, x, y); }
   310	  function render() {
   311	    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0); ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
   312	    drawBackdrop();
   313	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate((Math.random() - .5) * shake, (Math.random() - .5) * shake); drawArena(); if (mode === 'playing' || mode === 'win' || mode === 'loss') drawHud(); if (mode === 'playing' || mode === 'win' || mode === 'loss') drawEntities(); drawParticles(); drawFloaters(); if (mode === 'playing') drawControls(); ctx.restore();
   314	    if (mode === 'select') drawSelect(); else if (mode === 'win' || mode === 'loss') drawResult();
   315	    if (window.innerWidth > window.innerHeight) drawRotate();

   235	  function beginRaid() {
   236	    const threat = Math.round(18 + game.cycle * 8 + Math.random() * 8);
   237	    game.raid = { timer: 6.5, threat, resolved: false, success: false, defense: calcDefense() };
   238	    addFloat('RAIDER PROBE INBOUND', VW / 2, 112, COLORS.hot, 2.2);
   239	    beep('danger');
   240	  }
   241	
   242	  function calcDefense() {
   243	    const guards = countJob('guard');
   244	    const wall = game.buildings.wall ? 36 + game.cycle * 2 : 0;
   245	    const healthyGuards = aliveSurvivors().filter((s) => s.job === 'guard' && s.hp > 55).length;
   246	    return Math.round(guards * 8 + healthyGuards * 3 + wall);
   247	  }
   248	
   249	  function resolveRaid() {
   250	    if (!game.raid || game.raid.resolved) return;
   251	    const defense = calcDefense();
   252	    const threat = game.raid.threat;
   253	    game.raid.defense = defense;
   254	    game.raid.resolved = true;
   255	    if (defense >= threat) {
   256	      game.raid.success = true;
   257	      resource('wood', 8 + game.cycle * 2);
   258	      resource('coal', 5 + game.cycle);
   259	      addFloat('PROBE TURNED BACK', VW / 2, 132, COLORS.green, 2.1);
   260	      burst(300, 230, COLORS.green, 22);
   261	      beep('good');
   262	    } else {
   263	      const losses = Math.min(2, Math.max(1, Math.ceil((threat - defense) / 22)));
   264	      const victims = aliveSurvivors().sort((a, b) => (a.job === 'guard' ? -1 : 1) - (b.job === 'guard' ? -1 : 1)).slice(0, losses);
   265	      victims.forEach((survivor) => {
   266	        survivor.hp -= 18 + game.cycle * 2;
   267	        survivor.sick = Math.max(survivor.sick, 3);
   268	        survivor.cold += 1;
   269	        if (survivor.hp <= 0) { survivor.hp = 0; survivor.alive = false; survivor.job = 'dead'; }
   270	      });
   271	      game.shake = 1.2;
   272	      game.flash = .45;
   273	      addFloat(`${losses} HIT${losses > 1 ? 'S' : ''} TAKEN`, VW / 2, 132, COLORS.hot, 2.1);
   274	      burst(300, 230, COLORS.hot, 24);
   275	      beep('danger');
   276	      if (!aliveSurvivors().length) endGame('loss');
   277	    }
   278	  }
   279	
   280	  function updateSimulation(dt) {
   281	    if (!game.started || game.ended || landscape || document.hidden) return;
   282	    dt = clamp(dt, 0, .05);
   283	    game.totalTime += dt;
   284	    game.phaseTime += dt;
   285	    game.productionPulse += dt;
   286	    game.shake = Math.max(0, game.shake - dt * 1.8);
   287	    game.flash = Math.max(0, game.flash - dt * 1.8);
   288	    game.toastTime = Math.max(0, game.toastTime - dt);
   289	
   290	    const living = aliveSurvivors();
   291	    const hunts = countJob('hunt');
   292	    const chops = countJob('chop');
   293	    const menders = countJob('mend');
   294	    if (game.buildings.hunter) resource('food', hunts * (game.phase === 'calm' ? .62 : .26) * dt);
   295	    if (game.buildings.woodyard) resource('wood', chops * (game.phase === 'calm' ? .72 : .18) * dt);
   296	    resource('food', -living.length * .018 * dt);
   297	
   298	    const burn = Math.min(game.burnRate, game.resources.coal > 0 ? game.resources.coal / (.36 * dt) : 0);
   299	    resource('coal', -burn * .36 * dt);
   300	    if (game.phase === 'blizzard') {
   301	      const drain = 4.4 + game.cycle * .45;
   302	      const warmth = burn * 3.25;
   303	      game.heat = clamp(game.heat + (warmth - drain) * dt, 0, 100);
   304	      living.forEach((survivor) => {
   305	        if (game.heat < 38) {
   306	          survivor.cold += dt * (game.heat < 18 ? 1.8 : .8);
   307	          survivor.hp -= dt * (game.heat < 18 ? 1.05 : .3);
   308	          if (survivor.cold > 3.5) survivor.sick = Math.min(9, survivor.sick + dt * .25);
   309	        } else {
   310	          survivor.cold = Math.max(0, survivor.cold - dt * .4);
   311	        }
   312	        if (survivor.job === 'mend' && game.buildings.infirmary && survivor.sick > 0) survivor.sick = Math.max(0, survivor.sick - dt * .5);
   313	      });
   314	    } else {
   315	      game.heat = clamp(game.heat + (1.4 + burn * 1.4) * dt, 0, 100);
   316	      living.forEach((survivor) => {
   317	        if (survivor.job === 'mend' && game.buildings.infirmary) {
   318	          survivor.sick = Math.max(0, survivor.sick - dt * .8);
   319	          survivor.hp = Math.min(100, survivor.hp + dt * .28);
   320	        }
   321	      });
   322	    }
   323	    if (game.resources.food <= 0) living.forEach((survivor) => { survivor.hp -= dt * .18; });
   324	    living.forEach((survivor) => {
   325	      if (survivor.hp <= 0) { survivor.hp = 0; survivor.alive = false; survivor.job = 'dead'; burst(65 + survivor.id * 43, 500, COLORS.hot, 12); }
   326	    });
   327	
   328	    if (game.raid && !game.raid.resolved) {
   329	      game.raid.timer -= dt;
   330	      game.raid.defense = calcDefense();
   331	      if (game.raid.timer <= 0) resolveRaid();
   332	    }
   333	
   334	    if (game.phaseTime >= phaseDuration()) {
   335	      if (game.phase === 'calm') {
   336	        setPhase('blizzard');
   337	      } else if (game.cycle >= 10) {
   338	        endGame('win');
   339	      } else {
   340	        game.cycle += 1;
   341	        setPhase('calm');
   342	      }
   343	    }
   344	    if (!aliveSurvivors().length) endGame('loss');
   345	
   346	    updateSnow(dt);
   347	    updateParticles(dt);
   348	    updateFloats(dt);
   349	  }
   350	
   351	  function updateSnow(dt) {
   352	    if (game.phase !== 'blizzard') return;
   353	    game.snow.forEach((flake) => {
   354	      flake.y += flake.speed * dt;
   355	      flake.x += flake.drift * dt * 6;
   356	      if (flake.y > VH + 8) { flake.y = -8; flake.x = Math.random() * VW; }
   357	      if (flake.x < -5) flake.x = VW + 5;
   358	      if (flake.x > VW + 5) flake.x = -5;
   359	    });
   360	  }
   380	  }
   381	
   382	  function endGame(result) {
   383	    if (game.ended) return;
   384	    game.ended = true;
   385	    game.result = result;
   386	    const tally = aliveSurvivors().length;
   387	    if (tally > game.best) { game.best = tally; saveBest(tally); }
   388	    game.flash = .65;
   389	    game.shake = 1.3;
   390	    burst(VW / 2, 230, result === 'win' ? COLORS.cyan : COLORS.hot, 42);
   391	    beep(result === 'win' ? 'win' : 'danger');
   392	  }
   393	
   394	  function buildingAt(x, y) {
   395	    return BUILDINGS.find((b) => x >= b.x && x <= b.x + b.w && y >= b.y && y <= b.y + b.h);
   396	  }
   397	
   398	  function rosterAt(x, y) {
   399	    const living = game.survivors;
   400	    for (let i = 0; i < living.length; i += 1) {
   401	      const col = i % 3;
   402	      const row = Math.floor(i / 3);
   403	      const rect = { x: 12 + col * 125, y: 461 + row * 50, w: 116, h: 48 };
   404	      if (x >= rect.x && x <= rect.x + rect.w && y >= rect.y && y <= rect.y + rect.h) return living[i];
   405	    }
   406	    return null;
   407	  }
   408	
   409	  function jobAt(x, y) {
   410	    return JOBS.find((job) => x >= job.x && x <= job.x + job.w && y >= job.y && y <= job.y + job.h);
   411	  }
   412	
   413	  function hit(x, y) {
   414	    if (game.ended && x >= 84 && x <= 306 && y >= 468 && y <= 524) return { kind: 'restart', action: 'restart' };
   415	    if (x >= 14 && x <= 64 && y >= 619 && y <= 667) return { kind: 'burn-', action: 'burn-' };
   416	    if (x >= 326 && x <= 376 && y >= 619 && y <= 667) return { kind: 'burn+', action: 'burn+' };
   417	    if (x >= 14 && x <= 376 && y >= 565 && y <= 613) return { kind: 'build', action: 'build' };
   418	    if (x >= GUARD_RECT.x && x <= GUARD_RECT.x + GUARD_RECT.w && y >= GUARD_RECT.y && y <= GUARD_RECT.y + GUARD_RECT.h) return { kind: 'guard', action: 'guard' };
   419	    const building = buildingAt(x, y);
   420	    if (building) return { kind: 'building', building, action: 'building' };
   421	    const survivor = rosterAt(x, y);
   422	    if (survivor) return { kind: 'survivor', survivor, action: 'survivor' };
   423	    const job = jobAt(x, y);
   424	    if (job) return { kind: 'job', job, action: 'job' };
   425	    return null;
   426	  }
   427	
   428	  function assign(id, job) {
   429	    const survivor = game.survivors.find((s) => s.id === id);
   430	    if (!survivor || !survivor.alive) return;
   431	    if (job !== 'guard' && !game.buildings[JOBS.find((j) => j.id === job).building]) {
   432	      toast(`Build the ${job === 'hunt' ? 'HUNTER LODGE' : job === 'chop' ? 'WOODYARD' : 'INFIRMARY'} first.`);
   433	      beep('danger');
   434	      return;
   435	    }
   436	    survivor.job = job;
   437	    game.selectedSurvivor = id;
   438	    addFloat(`${survivor.name} → ${job.toUpperCase()}`, VW / 2, 335, COLORS.cyan, 1.3);
   439	    burst(195, 390, COLORS.cyan, 8);
   440	    beep('good');
   441	  }
   442	
   443	  function buildSelected() {
   444	    const b = BUILDINGS.find((item) => item.id === game.selectedSlot);
   445	    if (!b || game.buildings[b.id]) {
   446	      toast('That shelter is already standing.');
   447	      return;
   448	    }
   449	    if (game.resources.wood < b.cost) {
   450	      toast(`Need ${b.cost - Math.floor(game.resources.wood)} more wood.`);
   451	      beep('danger');
   452	      return;
   453	    }
   454	    resource('wood', -b.cost);
   455	    game.buildings[b.id] = true;
   456	    game.flash = .3;
   457	    burst(b.x + b.w / 2, b.y + b.h / 2, b.accent, 24);
   458	    addFloat(`${b.name} RAISED`, b.x + b.w / 2, b.y - 8, b.accent, 1.8);
   459	    beep('build');
   460	  }
   461	
   462	  function interact(action) {
   463	    if (action === 'burn-') { game.burnRate = clamp(game.burnRate - 1, 0, 5); addFloat(`BURN ${game.burnRate}`, 195, 110, COLORS.warm, .8); beep('good'); }
   464	    if (action === 'burn+') { game.burnRate = clamp(game.burnRate + 1, 0, 5); addFloat(`BURN ${game.burnRate}`, 195, 110, COLORS.warm, .8); beep('good'); }
   465	    if (action === 'build') buildSelected();
   466	    if (action === 'restart') startGame();
   467	  }
   468	
   469	  function pointFromEvent(event) {
   470	    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   471	    return { x: (event.clientX - rect.left) * VW / rect.width, y: (event.clientY - rect.top) * VH / rect.height };
   472	  }
   473	
   474	  function pointerDown(event) {
   475	    event.preventDefault();
   476	    unlockAudio();
   477	    if (!game.started) return;
   478	    const p = pointFromEvent(event);
   479	    const target = hit(p.x, p.y);
   480	    if (!target) return;
   481	    if (target.kind === 'survivor') {
   482	      if (!target.survivor.alive) return;
   483	      pointerMap.set(event.pointerId, { kind: 'drag', survivorId: target.survivor.id, startX: p.x, startY: p.y, moved: false });
   484	      activeDrags.set(event.pointerId, { survivorId: target.survivor.id, x: p.x, y: p.y });
   485	      game.selectedSurvivor = target.survivor.id;
   486	      try { canvas.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId); } catch (_) { /* pointer capture is optional */ }
   487	      return;
   488	    }
   489	    pointerMap.set(event.pointerId, { kind: target.kind, action: target.action, downX: p.x, downY: p.y });
   490	    if (target.kind === 'building') {
   491	      game.selectedSlot = target.building.id;
   492	      beep('good');
   493	    } else if (target.kind === 'job') {
   494	      assign(game.selectedSurvivor, target.job.id);
   495	    } else if (target.kind === 'guard') {
   496	      assign(game.selectedSurvivor, 'guard');
   497	    } else {
   498	      interact(target.action);
   499	    }
   500	  }
   501	
   502	  function pointerMove(event) {
   503	    event.preventDefault();
   504	    const control = pointerMap.get(event.pointerId);
   505	    if (!control) return;
   506	    if (control.kind === 'drag') {
   507	      const p = pointFromEvent(event);
   508	      if (Math.hypot(p.x - control.startX, p.y - control.startY) > 7) control.moved = true;
   509	      activeDrags.set(event.pointerId, { survivorId: control.survivorId, x: p.x, y: p.y });
   510	    }
   511	  }
   512	
   513	  function pointerUp(event, cancelled = false) {
   514	    event.preventDefault();
   515	    const control = pointerMap.get(event.pointerId);
   516	    if (control && control.kind === 'drag' && !cancelled) {
   517	      const p = pointFromEvent(event);
   518	      const job = jobAt(p.x, p.y);
   519	      if (job) assign(control.survivorId, job.id);
   520	      else if (p.x >= GUARD_RECT.x && p.x <= GUARD_RECT.x + GUARD_RECT.w && p.y >= GUARD_RECT.y && p.y <= GUARD_RECT.y + GUARD_RECT.h) assign(control.survivorId, 'guard');
   521	    }
   522	    pointerMap.delete(event.pointerId);
   523	    activeDrags.delete(event.pointerId);
   524	    try { canvas.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId); } catch (_) { /* optional */ }
   525	  }
   526	
   527	  function keyDown(event) {
   528	    const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
   529	    if ([' ', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright', 'arrowup', 'arrowdown'].includes(key)) event.preventDefault();
   530	    if (keys.has(key)) return;
   531	    keys.add(key);
   532	    if (!game.started) return;
   533	    if (/^[1-6]$/.test(key)) {
   534	      const building = BUILDINGS[Number(key) - 1];
   535	      if (building) { game.selectedSlot = building.id; beep('good'); }
   536	    } else if (key === ' ') {
   537	      buildSelected();
   538	    } else if (key === '+' || key === '=') {
   539	      interact('burn+');
   540	    } else if (key === '-' || key === '_') {
   840	    if (!game.started) {
   841	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(4, 10, 17, .25)';
   842	      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, VW, VH);
   843	    }
   844	    drawEndCard();
   845	    ctx.restore();
   846	  }
   847	
   848	  function frame(now) {
   849	    const dt = Math.min(.05, Math.max(0, (now - lastTime) / 1000));
   850	    lastTime = now;
   851	    updateSimulation(dt);
   852	    render();
   853	    window.requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   854	  }
   855	
   856	  startButton.addEventListener('click', startGame);
   857	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', pointerDown, { passive: false });
   858	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', pointerMove, { passive: false });
   859	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', (event) => pointerUp(event), { passive: false });
   860	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', (event) => pointerUp(event, true), { passive: false });
   861	  canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', (event) => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   862	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', (event) => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   863	  canvas.addEventListener('touchend', (event) => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   864	  window.addEventListener('keydown', keyDown, { passive: false });
   865	  window.addEventListener('keyup', (event) => keys.delete(event.key.toLowerCase()));
   866	  window.addEventListener('blur', resetInput);
   867	  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { lastTime = performance.now(); if (document.hidden) resetInput(); });
   868	  window.addEventListener('resize', () => { updateOrientation(); resize(); });
   869	  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', () => { updateOrientation(); resize(); });
   870	  updateOrientation();
   871	  resize();
   872	  window.requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   873	})();

   561	    const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
   562	    canvas.width = Math.min(960, Math.max(1, Math.round(rect.width * dpr)));
   563	    canvas.height = Math.min(960, Math.max(1, Math.round(rect.height * dpr)));
   564	    scaleX = canvas.width / VW;
   565	    scaleY = canvas.height / VH;

   362	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   363	    for (let i = game.particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
   364	      const p = game.particles[i];
   365	      p.life -= dt;
   366	      p.x += p.vx * dt;
   367	      p.y += p.vy * dt;
   368	      p.vy += 30 * dt;
   369	      if (p.life <= 0) game.particles.splice(i, 1);
   370	    }
   371	  }
   372	
   373	  function updateFloats(dt) {
   374	    for (let i = game.floats.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
   375	      const f = game.floats[i];
   376	      f.life -= dt;
   377	      f.y -= 10 * dt;
   378	      if (f.life <= 0) game.floats.splice(i, 1);
   379	    }
   380	  }
