   126	      const rung = safeInt(parsed.rung, 1, 1, 8);
   127	      const deck = validDeck(parsed.deck) ? parsed.deck.slice() : fallback.deck.slice();
   128	      return { rung, deck };
   129	    } catch (error) {
   130	      return fallback;
   131	    }
   132	  }
   133	
   134	  function saveProgress() {
   135	    try {
   136	      const payload = { rung: safeInt(progress.rung, 1, 1, 8), deck: validDeck(progress.deck) ? progress.deck.slice() : defaultDeck() };
   137	      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
   138	    } catch (error) {
   139	      // Storage is a convenience only; a private window or a full quota must not break play.
   140	    }
   141	  }
   142	
   143	  function clearScheduledTimeouts() {
   144	    scheduledTimeouts.forEach((id) => clearTimeout(id));
   145	    scheduledTimeouts.clear();
   146	  }
   147	
   148	  function schedule(fn, ms) {
   149	    if (scheduledTimeouts.size >= MAX_TIMEOUTS) return null;
   150	    const id = setTimeout(() => { scheduledTimeouts.delete(id); fn(); }, ms);
   151	    scheduledTimeouts.add(id);
   152	    return id;
   153	  }
   154	
   155	  function resetInput() {
   156	    pointerMap.forEach((_, pointerId) => {
   157	      try { canvas.releasePointerCapture(pointerId); } catch (error) { /* pointer may already be gone */ }
   158	    });
   159	    pointerMap.clear();
   160	    activeDrag = null;
   161	    keys.clear();
   162	    queuedActions.length = 0;
   163	    clearScheduledTimeouts();
   164	  }
   165	
   166	  function queueAction(action) {
   167	    if (queuedActions.length >= MAX_ACTIONS) queuedActions.shift();
   168	    queuedActions.push(action);
   169	  }
   170	
   171	  function unlockAudio() {
   172	    if (!audio) {
   173	      try {
   174	        const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
   175	        if (!AudioContext) return;
   176	        audio = new AudioContext();
   177	      } catch (error) { return; }
   178	    }
   179	    if (audio.state === 'suspended') audio.resume().catch(() => {});
   180	  }
   180	  }
   181	
   182	  function tone(frequency, duration, type = 'sine', volume = .035) {
   183	    if (!audio || audio.state !== 'running') return;
   184	    try {
   185	      const oscillator = audio.createOscillator();
   186	      const gain = audio.createGain();
   187	      oscillator.type = type;
   188	      oscillator.frequency.value = frequency;
   189	      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(volume, audio.currentTime);
   190	      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001, audio.currentTime + duration);
   191	      oscillator.connect(gain).connect(audio.destination);
   192	      oscillator.start();
   193	      oscillator.stop(audio.currentTime + duration);
   194	    } catch (error) { /* audio is decorative */ }
   195	  }
   196	
   197	  function resizeCanvas() {
   198	    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   199	    cssWidth = Math.max(1, rect.width);
   200	    cssHeight = Math.max(1, rect.height);
   201	    const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2, 960 / Math.max(cssWidth, cssHeight));
   202	    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssWidth * dpr));
   203	    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssHeight * dpr));
   204	    ctx.setTransform(canvas.width / W, 0, 0, canvas.height / H, 0, 0);
   205	    orientationPaused = isLandscape();
   206	    rotateOverlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', orientationPaused ? 'false' : 'true');
   207	    lastFrame = performance.now();
   208	  }
   209	
   210	  function mapPointer(event) {
   211	    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   212	    return {
   213	      x: clamp((event.clientX - rect.left) / Math.max(1, rect.width) * W, 0, W),
   214	      y: clamp((event.clientY - rect.top) / Math.max(1, rect.height) * H, 0, H)
   215	    };
   216	  }
   217	
   218	  function pointIn(x, y, rect) { return x >= rect.x && x <= rect.x + rect.w && y >= rect.y && y <= rect.y + rect.h; }
   219	
   220	  function startGame(rung = progress.rung) {
   221	    resetInput();
   222	    unlockAudio();
   223	    const rungIndex = clamp(rung, 1, 8) - 1;
   224	    const bot = LADDER[rungIndex];
   225	    game = {
   226	      rung: rungIndex + 1,
   227	      bot,
   228	      time: 180,
   229	      overtime: false,
   230	      status: 'playing',
   231	      playerTower: 1000,
   232	      enemyTower: 1000,
   233	      playerEnergy: 5,
   234	      enemyEnergy: 5,
   235	      maxEnergy: 10,
   236	      units: [],
   237	      projectiles: [],
   238	      particles: [],
   239	      floaters: [],
   240	      hand: progress.deck.slice(0, 4),
   241	      deckIndex: 4,
   242	      selectedHand: 0,
   243	      laneFocus: 0,
   244	      aiTimer: .65,
   245	      pulse: 0,
   246	      shake: 0,
   247	      flash: 0,
   248	      nextEntityId: 1,
   249	      message: 'Drag a card into a lane · 1–4 choose · ←→ lane · Space deploy',
   250	      messageTime: 6,
   251	      lastTowerHit: 0
   252	    };
   253	    screen = 'game';
   254	    lastFrame = performance.now();
   255	    tone(260, .12, 'triangle', .045);
   256	  }
   257	
   258	  function goMenu() {
   259	    resetInput();
   260	    game = null;
   261	    screen = 'menu';
   262	  }
   263	
   264	  function openForge() {
   265	    resetInput();
   266	    screen = 'forge';
   267	  }
   268	
   269	  function createEntity(cardId, team, lane, y) {
   270	    if (!game || game.units.length >= MAX_ENTITIES) return null;
   271	    const c = card(cardId);
   272	    const entity = {
   273	      id: game.nextEntityId,
   274	      cardId,
   275	      team,
   276	      lane,
   277	      x: ARENA.laneCenters[lane] + rand(-7, 7),
   278	      y,
   279	      hp: c.hp,
   280	      maxHp: c.hp,
   281	      attackTimer: rand(.05, .3),
   282	      freeze: 0,
   283	      slow: 0,
   284	      hit: 0,
   285	      building: Boolean(c.building),
   286	      phase: rand(0, TAU)
   287	    };
   288	    game.nextEntityId = (game.nextEntityId % 99999) + 1;
   289	    game.units.push(entity);
   290	    return entity;
   291	  }
   292	
   293	  function spend(team, cost) {
   294	    if (team === 'player') game.playerEnergy -= cost;
   295	    else game.enemyEnergy -= cost;
   296	  }
   297	
   298	  function deployCard(cardId, team, lane, targetY) {
   299	    const c = card(cardId);
   300	    if (!game || game.status !== 'playing') return false;
   301	    const energy = team === 'player' ? game.playerEnergy : game.enemyEnergy;
   302	    if (energy + .001 < c.cost) {
   303	      if (team === 'player') {
   304	        game.message = 'Need more energy';
   305	        game.messageTime = 1.2;
   306	        tone(115, .08, 'square', .022);
   307	      }
   308	      return false;
   309	    }
   310	    spend(team, c.cost);
   311	    const safeLane = clamp(lane | 0, 0, 1);
   312	    if (c.role === 'spell') {
   313	      castSpell(c, team, safeLane, clamp(targetY || (team === 'player' ? 330 : 340), ARENA.top + 12, ARENA.bottom - 12));
   314	    } else {
   315	      const baseY = c.building ? (team === 'player' ? 485 : 173) : (team === 'player' ? 518 : 141);
   316	      const direction = team === 'player' ? -1 : 1;
   317	      const count = clamp(c.count || 1, 1, 6);
   318	      for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {
   319	        const offset = (i - (count - 1) / 2) * 12;
   320	        createEntity(cardId, team, safeLane, baseY + offset * direction);
   321	      }
   322	      burst(ARENA.laneCenters[safeLane], baseY, c.color, c.building ? 12 : 7);
   323	      if (team === 'player') {
   324	        game.message = `${c.name} deployed · ${c.blurb}`;
   325	        game.messageTime = 1.7;
   326	        tone(390 + c.cost * 22, .09, 'triangle', .028);
   327	      }
   328	    }
   329	    return true;
   330	  }
   331	
   332	  function rotateHand() {
   333	    if (!game) return;
   334	    game.hand[game.selectedHand] = progress.deck[game.deckIndex % progress.deck.length];
   335	    game.deckIndex = (game.deckIndex + 1) % progress.deck.length;
   336	    game.selectedHand = clamp(game.selectedHand, 0, 3);
   337	  }
   338	
   339	  function deployPlayer(cardIndex, lane, targetY) {
   340	    if (!game || game.status !== 'playing') return;
   341	    const index = clamp(cardIndex | 0, 0, 3);
   342	    const id = game.hand[index];
   343	    if (deployCard(id, 'player', lane, targetY)) {
   344	      game.selectedHand = index;
   345	      rotateHand();
   346	    }
   347	  }
   348	
   349	  function castSpell(c, team, lane, targetY) {
   350	    const enemyTeam = team === 'player' ? 'enemy' : 'player';
   351	    const centerX = ARENA.laneCenters[lane];
   352	    burst(centerX, targetY, c.color, c.spell === 'meteor' ? 24 : 14);
   353	    game.flash = .14;
   354	    game.shake = Math.max(game.shake, c.spell === 'meteor' ? 8 : 4);
   355	    if (c.spell === 'heal') {
   356	      game.units.forEach((unit) => {
   357	        if (unit.team === team && unit.lane === lane && Math.abs(unit.y - targetY) <= c.radius) {
   358	          unit.hp = Math.min(unit.maxHp, unit.hp + c.heal);
   359	          unit.hit = .15;
   360	        }
   361	      });
   362	      if (team === 'player') addFloater(centerX, targetY - 20, `+${c.heal}`, COLORS.mint);
   363	      tone(680, .16, 'sine', .035);
   364	      return;
   365	    }
   366	    let affected = 0;
   367	    game.units.forEach((unit) => {
   368	      if (unit.team === enemyTeam && unit.lane === lane && Math.abs(unit.y - targetY) <= c.radius) {
   369	        unit.hp -= c.damage;
   370	        unit.hit = .2;
   371	        affected += 1;
   372	        if (c.spell === 'shock') unit.freeze = Math.max(unit.freeze, 1.1);
   373	        if (c.spell === 'freeze') unit.slow = Math.max(unit.slow, 2.2);
   374	      }
   375	    });
   376	    const towerY = enemyTeam === 'enemy' ? 79 : 579;
   377	    if (Math.abs(targetY - towerY) < 52) damageTower(enemyTeam, c.damage * (c.spell === 'meteor' ? .58 : .2), c.color);
   378	    if (team === 'player') addFloater(centerX, targetY - 20, affected ? `${affected} tagged` : 'impact', c.color);
   379	    tone(c.spell === 'meteor' ? 92 : 260, c.spell === 'meteor' ? .22 : .12, 'sawtooth', .032);
   380	  }
   380	  }
   381	
   382	  function matchupMultiplier(attacker, target) {
   383	    const a = card(attacker.cardId);
   384	    const t = card(target.cardId);
   385	    let mult = 1;
   386	    if (a.counter === t.role) mult *= 1.36;
   387	    if (a.role === 'splash' && t.role === 'swarm') mult *= 1.22;
   388	    if (a.role === 'building' && t.role === 'rush') mult *= 1.22;
   389	    if (a.role === 'tank' && t.role === 'building') mult *= .88;
   390	    return mult;
   391	  }
   392	
   393	  function findTarget(attacker) {
   394	    const opponents = game.units.filter((unit) => unit.team !== attacker.team && unit.lane === attacker.lane && unit.hp > 0);
   395	    let best = null;
   396	    let bestDistance = Infinity;
   397	    opponents.forEach((opponent) => {
   398	      const distance = Math.abs(opponent.y - attacker.y);
   399	      const isAhead = attacker.team === 'player' ? opponent.y < attacker.y + 14 : opponent.y > attacker.y - 14;
   400	      if (isAhead && distance < bestDistance) {
   401	        best = opponent;
   402	        bestDistance = distance;
   403	      }
   404	    });
   405	    return best;
   406	  }
   407	
   408	  function hitUnit(target, damage, attacker, splash = 0) {
   409	    if (!target || target.hp <= 0) return;
   410	    const amount = damage * matchupMultiplier(attacker, target);
   411	    target.hp -= amount;
   412	    target.hit = .16;
   413	    burst(target.x, target.y, card(attacker.cardId).color, 3);
   414	    if (splash > 0) {
   415	      game.units.forEach((near) => {
   416	        if (near !== target && near.team !== attacker.team && near.lane === target.lane && Math.abs(near.y - target.y) <= splash) {
   417	          near.hp -= damage * .62;
   418	          near.hit = .12;
   419	        }
   420	      });
   421	    }
   422	  }
   423	
   424	  function fire(attacker, target) {
   425	    const c = card(attacker.cardId);
   426	    const distance = Math.abs(target.y - attacker.y);
   427	    const damage = c.damage || 0;
   428	    if (c.range > 40 && distance > 32) {
   429	      if (game.projectiles.length >= MAX_PROJECTILES) game.projectiles.shift();
   430	      game.projectiles.push({ x: attacker.x, y: attacker.y, tx: target.x, ty: target.y, life: .13, maxLife: .13, attackerId: attacker.id, targetId: target.id, damage, splash: c.splash || 0, color: c.color });
   431	    } else {
   432	      hitUnit(target, damage, attacker, c.splash || 0);
   433	    }
   434	    attacker.attackTimer = c.attack;
   435	    attacker.hit = .08;
   436	  }
   437	
   438	  function damageTower(which, amount, color = COLORS.coral) {
   439	    if (!game || game.status !== 'playing') return;
   440	    if (game.overtime) {
   441	      if (which === 'enemy') game.enemyTower = 0;
   442	      else game.playerTower = 0;
   443	    } else if (which === 'enemy') {
   444	      game.enemyTower = Math.max(0, game.enemyTower - amount);
   445	    } else {
   446	      game.playerTower = Math.max(0, game.playerTower - amount);
   447	    }
   448	    const x = W / 2;
   449	    const y = which === 'enemy' ? 79 : 579;
   450	    burst(x, y, color, game.overtime ? 25 : 12);
   451	    game.shake = Math.max(game.shake, game.overtime ? 13 : 6);
   452	    game.flash = .12;
   453	    game.lastTowerHit = .35;
   454	    addFloater(x, y + (which === 'enemy' ? 30 : -30), `-${Math.round(amount)}`, color);
   455	    tone(which === 'enemy' ? 150 : 105, .14, 'square', .035);
   456	    if (game.enemyTower <= 0) finishGame('victory');
   457	    if (game.playerTower <= 0) finishGame('defeat');
   458	  }
   459	
   460	  function finishGame(result) {
   461	    if (!game || game.status !== 'playing') return;
   462	    game.status = result;
   463	    resetInput();
   464	    if (result === 'victory') {
   465	      if (game.rung >= progress.rung) progress.rung = Math.min(8, game.rung + (game.rung < 8 ? 1 : 0));
   466	      saveProgress();
   467	      if (game.rung >= 8) screen = 'champion';
   468	      tone(520, .18, 'triangle', .05);
   469	      schedule(() => tone(780, .26, 'triangle', .045), 110);
   470	    } else {
   471	      tone(100, .22, 'sawtooth', .045);
   472	    }
   473	  }
   474	
   475	  function beginOvertime() {
   476	    if (!game || game.overtime || game.status !== 'playing') return;
   477	    game.overtime = true;
   478	    game.time = 0;
   479	    game.message = 'SUDDEN DEATH · next core hit decides it';
   480	    game.messageTime = 5;
   481	    game.flash = .28;
   482	    game.shake = 8;
   483	    burst(W / 2, 337, COLORS.gold, 28);
   484	    tone(230, .3, 'sawtooth', .04);
   485	  }
   486	
   487	  function updatePlayerEnergy(dt) { game.playerEnergy = Math.min(game.maxEnergy, game.playerEnergy + dt * 1.28); }
   488	  function updateEnemyEnergy(dt) { game.enemyEnergy = Math.min(game.maxEnergy, game.enemyEnergy + dt * 1.18); }
   489	
   490	  function enemyHasRole(role, lane) {
   491	    return game.units.some((unit) => unit.team === 'enemy' && unit.lane === lane && card(unit.cardId).role === role && unit.hp > 0);
   492	  }
   493	
   494	  function chooseBotCard() {
   495	    const available = game.bot.deck.filter((id) => card(id).cost <= game.enemyEnergy + .001);
   496	    if (!available.length) return null;
   497	    const playerUnits = game.units.filter((unit) => unit.team === 'player' && unit.hp > 0);
   498	    const dangerous = playerUnits.sort((a, b) => b.y - a.y)[0];
   499	    let wanted = available[0];
   500	    if (dangerous) {
   501	      const dangerRole = card(dangerous.cardId).role;
   502	      const counter = available.find((id) => card(id).counter === dangerRole || (dangerRole === 'swarm' && card(id).role === 'splash'));
   503	      if (counter) wanted = counter;
   504	    }
   505	    if (!dangerous || Math.random() > game.bot.skill) {
   506	      wanted = available[Math.floor(Math.random() * available.length)];
   507	    }
   508	    return wanted;
   509	  }
   510	
   511	  function updateBot(dt) {
   512	    game.aiTimer -= dt;
   513	    if (game.aiTimer > 0) return;
   514	    game.aiTimer = game.bot.gap * rand(.82, 1.18);
   515	    const cardId = chooseBotCard();
   516	    if (cardId === null) return;
   517	    const c = card(cardId);
   518	    const playerUnits = game.units.filter((unit) => unit.team === 'player' && unit.hp > 0);
   519	    let lane = Math.random() < .5 ? 0 : 1;
   520	    let targetY = rand(220, 455);
   521	    if (playerUnits.length) {
   522	      const threat = playerUnits.sort((a, b) => (b.team === 'player' ? b.y : 0) - (a.team === 'player' ? a.y : 0))[0];
   523	      if (game.bot.skill > .5 && threat) lane = threat.lane;
   524	      if (c.role === 'spell' && threat) targetY = threat.y;
   525	    }
   526	    if (c.role === 'spell' && c.spell !== 'heal') targetY = playerUnits.length ? playerUnits[0].y : rand(240, 430);
   527	    if (c.role === 'spell' && c.spell === 'heal') targetY = 220;
   528	    if (deployCard(cardId, 'enemy', lane, targetY)) {
   529	      if (game.bot.skill > .7) {
   530	        const nextThreat = game.units.find((unit) => unit.team === 'player' && unit.lane !== lane);
   531	        if (nextThreat && c.role === 'building' && !enemyHasRole('building', lane)) { /* deliberate lane pressure */ }
   532	      }
   533	    }
   534	  }
   535	
   536	  function updateUnits(dt) {
   537	    const dead = [];
   538	    game.units.forEach((unit) => {
   539	      if (unit.hp <= 0) { dead.push(unit); return; }
   540	      const c = card(unit.cardId);
   541	      unit.hit = Math.max(0, unit.hit - dt);
   542	      unit.freeze = Math.max(0, unit.freeze - dt);
   543	      unit.slow = Math.max(0, unit.slow - dt);
   544	      unit.attackTimer -= dt;
   545	      if (unit.freeze > 0) return;
   546	      const target = findTarget(unit);
   547	      const distance = target ? Math.abs(target.y - unit.y) : Infinity;
   548	      const reach = Math.max(22, c.range || 20);
   549	      if (target && distance <= reach) {
   550	        if (unit.attackTimer <= 0) fire(unit, target);
   551	      } else if (!unit.building) {
   552	        const speed = (c.speed || 20) * (unit.slow > 0 ? .42 : 1);
   553	        unit.y += (unit.team === 'player' ? -1 : 1) * speed * dt;
   554	        unit.x = ARENA.laneCenters[unit.lane] + Math.sin(game.pulse * 2 + unit.phase) * 4;
   555	        if ((unit.team === 'player' && unit.y < 101) || (unit.team === 'enemy' && unit.y > 557)) {
   556	          damageTower(unit.team === 'player' ? 'enemy' : 'player', c.damage * (c.role === 'rush' ? 1.22 : .72), c.color);
   557	          dead.push(unit);
   558	        }
   559	      }
   560	    });
   561	    if (dead.length) {
   562	      const deadSet = new Set(dead);
   563	      game.units = game.units.filter((unit) => !deadSet.has(unit));
   564	      dead.forEach((unit) => burst(unit.x, unit.y, card(unit.cardId).color, 7));
   565	    }
   566	  }
   567	
   568	  function updateProjectiles(dt) {
   569	    const keep = [];
   570	    game.projectiles.forEach((projectile) => {
   571	      projectile.life -= dt;
   572	      if (projectile.life <= 0) {
   573	        const attacker = game.units.find((unit) => unit.id === projectile.attackerId && unit.hp > 0);
   574	        const target = game.units.find((unit) => unit.id === projectile.targetId && unit.hp > 0);
   575	        if (attacker && target) hitUnit(target, projectile.damage, attacker, projectile.splash);
   576	        return;
   577	      }
   578	      keep.push(projectile);
   579	    });
   580	    game.projectiles = keep;
   581	  }
   582	
   583	  function updateEffects(dt) {
   584	    game.particles.forEach((particle) => {
   585	      particle.x += particle.vx * dt;
   586	      particle.y += particle.vy * dt;
   587	      particle.vy += 34 * dt;
   588	      particle.life -= dt;
   589	    });
   590	    game.particles = game.particles.filter((particle) => particle.life > 0).slice(-MAX_PARTICLES);
   591	    game.floaters.forEach((floater) => { floater.y -= 25 * dt; floater.life -= dt; });
   592	    game.floaters = game.floaters.filter((floater) => floater.life > 0).slice(-MAX_FLOATERS);
   593	    game.shake = Math.max(0, game.shake - dt * 28);
   594	    game.flash = Math.max(0, game.flash - dt);
   595	    game.messageTime = Math.max(0, game.messageTime - dt);
   596	    game.lastTowerHit = Math.max(0, game.lastTowerHit - dt);
   597	  }
   598	
   599	  function updateGame(dt) {
   600	    if (!game || game.status !== 'playing') { updateEffects(dt); return; }
   601	    game.pulse += dt;
   602	    if (!game.overtime) {
   603	      game.time = Math.max(0, game.time - dt);
   604	      if (game.time <= 0) {
   605	        const difference = Math.abs(game.playerTower - game.enemyTower);
   606	        if (difference < 1) beginOvertime();
   607	        else finishGame(game.enemyTower < game.playerTower ? 'victory' : 'defeat');
   608	      }
   609	    }
   610	    updatePlayerEnergy(dt);
   611	    updateEnemyEnergy(dt);
   612	    while (queuedActions.length) {
   613	      const action = queuedActions.shift();
   614	      if (action.type === 'deploy') deployPlayer(action.cardIndex, action.lane, action.targetY);
   615	    }
   616	    updateBot(dt);
   617	    updateUnits(dt);
   618	    updateProjectiles(dt);
   619	    updateEffects(dt);
   620	  }
   621	
   622	  function burst(x, y, color, count) {
   623	    if (!game) return;
   624	    const room = Math.max(0, MAX_PARTICLES - game.particles.length);
   625	    const amount = Math.min(count, room);
   626	    for (let i = 0; i < amount; i += 1) {
   627	      const angle = rand(0, TAU);
   628	      const speed = rand(18, 72);
   629	      game.particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed, vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed - 18, life: rand(.24, .7), maxLife: .7, size: rand(1.5, 4), color });
   630	    }
   631	  }
   632	
   633	  function addFloater(x, y, text, color) {
   634	    if (!game) return;
   635	    if (game.floaters.length >= MAX_FLOATERS) game.floaters.shift();
   636	    game.floaters.push({ x, y, text: String(text).slice(0, 24), color, life: 1, maxLife: 1 });
   637	  }
   638	
   639	  function pathRoundRect(x, y, w, h, radius) {
   640	    const r = Math.min(Math.max(0, radius), Math.min(w, h) / 2);
   940	    else drawMenu();
   941	  }
   942	
   943	  function forgeHit(x, y) {
   944	    if (pointIn(x, y, { x: 12, y: 58, w: 88, h: 48 })) return { type: 'back' };
   945	    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i += 1) {
   946	      const col = i % 4; const row = Math.floor(i / 4);
   947	      const rect = { x: 8 + col * 95, y: 111 + row * 55, w: 88, h: 49 };
   948	      if (pointIn(x, y, rect)) return { type: 'slot', index: i };
   949	    }
   950	    for (let i = 0; i < CARDS.length; i += 1) {
   951	      const col = i % 4; const row = Math.floor(i / 4);
   952	      const rect = { x: 8 + col * 95, y: 242 + row * 63, w: 88, h: 56 };
   953	      if (pointIn(x, y, rect)) return { type: 'card', index: i };
   954	    }
   955	    return null;
   956	  }
   957	
   958	  function gameButtonHit(x, y) {
   959	    if (pointIn(x, y, { x: 48, y: 385, w: 138, h: 52 })) return 'retry';
   960	    if (pointIn(x, y, { x: 204, y: 385, w: 138, h: 52 })) return 'ladder';
   961	    return null;
   962	  }
   963	
   964	  function handIndexAt(x, y) {
   965	    if (y < 620 || y > 696) return -1;
   966	    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) if (pointIn(x, y, { x: 8 + i * 96, y: 622, w: 88, h: 69 })) return i;
   967	    return -1;
   968	  }
   969	
   970	  function laneAt(x) { return x < W / 2 ? 0 : 1; }
   971	
   972	  function handlePointerDown(event) {
   973	    event.preventDefault();
   974	    unlockAudio();
   975	    const pointerId = event.pointerId;
   976	    const p = mapPointer(event);
   977	    if (pointerMap.size >= MAX_POINTERS) {
   978	      const oldest = pointerMap.keys().next().value;
   979	      pointerMap.delete(oldest);
   980	      try { canvas.releasePointerCapture(oldest); } catch (error) { /* pointer may already be gone */ }
   981	    }
   982	    pointerMap.set(pointerId, { pointerId, x: p.x, y: p.y, startX: p.x, startY: p.y, moved: false, screen });
   983	    try { canvas.setPointerCapture(pointerId); } catch (error) { /* capture is not available in every embedded webview */ }
   984	    if (orientationPaused || hiddenPaused) return;
   985	    if (screen === 'menu') {
   986	      if (pointIn(p.x, p.y, { x: 15, y: 194, w: 218, h: 52 })) startGame(progress.rung);
   987	      else if (pointIn(p.x, p.y, { x: 242, y: 194, w: 133, h: 52 })) openForge();
   988	      return;
   989	    }
   990	    if (screen === 'forge') {
   991	      const hit = forgeHit(p.x, p.y);
   992	      if (hit && hit.type === 'back') goMenu();
   993	      else if (hit && hit.type === 'slot') selectedDeckSlot = hit.index;
   994	      else if (hit && hit.type === 'card' && hit.index < progress.rung * 3) {
   995	        progress.deck[selectedDeckSlot] = hit.index;
   996	        selectedDeckSlot = (selectedDeckSlot + 1) % 8;
   997	        saveProgress();
   998	        tone(440, .08, 'triangle', .03);
   999	      }
  1000	      return;
  1001	    }
  1002	    if (screen === 'champion') {
  1003	      if (pointIn(p.x, p.y, { x: 42, y: 458, w: 145, h: 54 })) startGame(8);
  1004	      else if (pointIn(p.x, p.y, { x: 203, y: 458, w: 145, h: 54 })) openForge();
  1005	      return;
  1006	    }
  1007	    if (screen !== 'game' || !game) return;
  1008	    if (game.status !== 'playing') {
  1009	      const action = gameButtonHit(p.x, p.y);
  1010	      if (action === 'retry') startGame(game.rung);
  1011	      else if (action === 'ladder') goMenu();
  1012	      return;
  1013	    }
  1014	    const handIndex = handIndexAt(p.x, p.y);
  1015	    if (handIndex >= 0) {
  1016	      activeDrag = { pointerId, cardIndex: handIndex, x: p.x, y: p.y };
  1017	      return;
  1018	    }
  1019	    if (p.y >= ARENA.top && p.y <= ARENA.bottom) {
  1020	      game.laneFocus = laneAt(p.x);
  1021	      pointerMap.get(pointerId).laneTap = true;
  1022	    }
  1023	  }
  1024	
  1025	  function handlePointerMove(event) {
  1026	    event.preventDefault();
  1027	    const pointerId = event.pointerId;
  1028	    const record = pointerMap.get(pointerId);
  1029	    if (!record) return;
  1030	    const p = mapPointer(event);
  1031	    record.x = p.x; record.y = p.y;
  1032	    if (Math.hypot(p.x - record.startX, p.y - record.startY) > 8) record.moved = true;
  1033	    if (activeDrag && activeDrag.pointerId === pointerId) { activeDrag.x = p.x; activeDrag.y = p.y; }
  1034	  }
  1035	
  1036	  function handlePointerUp(event, canceled = false) {
  1037	    event.preventDefault();
  1038	    const pointerId = event.pointerId;
  1039	    const record = pointerMap.get(pointerId);
  1040	    if (!record) return;
  1041	    const p = mapPointer(event);
  1042	    if (!canceled && screen === 'game' && game && game.status === 'playing') {
  1043	      if (activeDrag && activeDrag.pointerId === pointerId) {
  1044	        if (record.moved && p.y >= ARENA.top - 10 && p.y <= ARENA.bottom + 12) queueAction({ type: 'deploy', cardIndex: activeDrag.cardIndex, lane: laneAt(p.x), targetY: p.y });
  1045	        else game.selectedHand = activeDrag.cardIndex;
  1046	        activeDrag = null;
  1047	      } else if (record.laneTap && !record.moved) {
  1048	        queueAction({ type: 'deploy', cardIndex: game.selectedHand, lane: laneAt(p.x), targetY: p.y });
  1049	      }
  1050	    }
  1051	    pointerMap.delete(pointerId);
  1052	    try { canvas.releasePointerCapture(pointerId); } catch (error) { /* already released */ }
  1053	  }
  1054	
  1055	  function handleKeyDown(event) {
  1056	    const key = event.key;
  1057	    if (['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', ' ', 'Enter'].includes(key)) event.preventDefault();
  1058	    unlockAudio();
  1059	    if (key === 'Escape') {
  1060	      if (screen === 'forge' || screen === 'champion') goMenu();
  1061	      else if (screen === 'game') goMenu();
  1062	      return;
  1063	    }
  1064	    if (screen === 'menu' && (key === 'Enter' || key === ' ')) { startGame(progress.rung); return; }
  1065	    if (screen === 'champion' && (key === 'Enter' || key === ' ')) { startGame(8); return; }
  1066	    if (screen === 'forge') {
  1067	      if (/^[1-8]$/.test(key)) selectedDeckSlot = Number(key) - 1;
  1068	      return;
  1069	    }
  1070	    if (screen !== 'game' || !game || game.status !== 'playing') return;
  1071	    if (/^[1-4]$/.test(key)) { game.selectedHand = Number(key) - 1; tone(300 + game.selectedHand * 35, .05, 'triangle', .018); }
  1072	    if (key === 'ArrowLeft') game.laneFocus = 0;
  1073	    if (key === 'ArrowRight') game.laneFocus = 1;
  1074	    if (key === 'ArrowUp') game.selectedHand = (game.selectedHand + 3) % 4;
  1075	    if (key === 'ArrowDown') game.selectedHand = (game.selectedHand + 1) % 4;
  1076	    if (key === ' ' || key === 'Enter') queueAction({ type: 'deploy', cardIndex: game.selectedHand, lane: game.laneFocus, targetY: game.laneFocus === 0 ? 390 : 390 });
  1077	    if (keys.size >= MAX_KEYS) keys.clear();
  1078	    keys.add(key);
  1079	  }
  1080	
  1081	  function handleKeyUp(event) { keys.delete(event.key); }

  1083	  function updatePauseState() {
  1084	    orientationPaused = isLandscape();
  1085	    hiddenPaused = document.hidden;
  1086	    rotateOverlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', orientationPaused ? 'false' : 'true');
  1087	    lastFrame = performance.now();
  1088	  }
  1089	
  1090	  function frame(now) {
  1091	    const raw = Math.max(0, (now - lastFrame) / 1000);
  1092	    lastFrame = now;
  1093	    const dt = Math.min(raw, 0.033);
  1094	    if (!orientationPaused && !hiddenPaused) updateGame(dt);
  1095	    render();
  1096	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  1097	  }

  1099	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown, { passive: false });
  1100	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove, { passive: false });
  1101	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp, { passive: false });
  1102	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', (event) => handlePointerUp(event, true), { passive: false });
  1103	  canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu', (event) => event.preventDefault());

  1109	  window.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp, { passive: false });
  1110	  window.addEventListener('blur', () => { resetInput(); });
  1111	  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', updatePauseState);
  1112	  window.addEventListener('resize', resizeCanvas);
  1113	  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', () => { updatePauseState(); resizeCanvas(); });

   241	    state.partyHp = state.partyHp.map((hp, i) => Math.min(HEROES[i].maxHp, hp + Math.round(HEROES[i].maxHp * 0.18)));

   243	    flash = 0.5; shake = 5; tone(plan.boss ? 880 : 620, 0.16, 'triangle', 0.045);
   244	    if (state.battleIndex >= BATTLE_PLAN.length - 1) {
   245	      state.battleIndex = BATTLE_PLAN.length;
   246	      state.scene = 'win'; state.won = true;
   247	      state.bestScore = Math.max(state.bestScore, state.score);
   248	    } else {
   249	      state.battleIndex++;
   250	      state.storyLine = 0;
   251	      state.scene = 'story';
   252	    }
   253	    state.battle = null;
   254	    saveGame();
   255	  }
   256	
   257	  function failBattle() {
   258	    state.scene = 'fail';
   259	    saveGame();
   260	    notice('The crew was scattered. The route remains open.');
   261	    tone(110, 0.24, 'sawtooth', 0.04);
   262	  }
   263	
   264	  function playerAction(kind) {
   265	    if (state.scene !== 'battle' || !state.battle) return;
   266	    const id = state.battle.activeHero;
   267	    const hero = HEROES[id];
   268	    if (!hero || state.partyHp[id] <= 0) { state.battle.activeHero = firstLivingHero(); return; }
   269	    chooseTarget();
   270	    const enemy = state.battle.enemies[state.battle.target];
   271	    if (!enemy || enemy.hp <= 0) { notice('Choose a live target.'); return; }
   272	    if (kind === 'skill' && state.sp < 1) { notice('Need 1 shared SP. Basics restore it.'); tone(160, 0.08, 'square', 0.025); return; }
   273	    if (kind === 'ultimate' && state.ults[id] < 100) { notice(`${hero.call} is at ${Math.floor(state.ults[id])}%.`); tone(160, 0.08, 'square', 0.025); return; }
   274	    const power = kind === 'basic' ? hero.basic : kind === 'skill' ? hero.skill : hero.ult;
   275	    const breakPower = kind === 'basic' ? hero.break : kind === 'skill' ? hero.break + 12 : hero.break + 24;
   276	    let damage = Math.round(power * (0.94 + Math.random() * 0.12));
   277	    const isWeak = enemy.weak === hero.element;
   278	    if (enemy.broken) damage = Math.round(damage * 1.34);
   279	    if (isWeak) { enemy.break = Math.min(enemy.breakMax, enemy.break + breakPower); }
   280	    if (kind === 'basic') state.sp = Math.min(5, state.sp + 1);
   281	    if (kind === 'skill') state.sp = Math.max(0, state.sp - 1);
   282	    if (kind === 'ultimate') state.ults[id] = 0;
   283	    else state.ults[id] = Math.min(100, state.ults[id] + (kind === 'basic' ? 24 : 16));
   284	    state.ults = state.ults.map((v, i) => i === id ? v : Math.min(100, v + 4));
   285	    enemy.hp = Math.max(0, enemy.hp - damage);
   286	    state.score += damage;
   287	    burst(enemyX(state.battle.target), enemyY(state.battle.target), ELEMENTS.find(e => e.id === hero.element).color, kind === 'ultimate' ? 22 : 10);
   288	    popup(enemyX(state.battle.target), enemyY(state.battle.target) - 52, `${damage}`, ELEMENTS.find(e => e.id === hero.element).soft, kind === 'ultimate' ? 24 : 18);
   289	    if (isWeak) popup(enemyX(state.battle.target), enemyY(state.battle.target) - 78, 'WEAK', '#fff1a5', 14);
   290	    if (hero.role === 'MENDER' && kind !== 'basic') healParty(kind === 'ultimate' ? 26 : 15);
   291	    if (hero.role === 'ANCHOR' && kind === 'skill') healParty(7);
   292	    if (hero.role === 'TEMPO' && kind === 'skill') state.sp = Math.min(5, state.sp + 1);
   293	    if (enemy.break >= enemy.breakMax && !enemy.broken) {
   294	      enemy.broken = true; enemy.break = enemy.breakMax;
   295	      popup(enemyX(state.battle.target), enemyY(state.battle.target) - 106, 'BROKEN', '#ffe08b', 16);
   296	      burst(enemyX(state.battle.target), enemyY(state.battle.target), '#ffe08b', 28); shake = 8; flash = 0.22;
   297	    }
   298	    tone(kind === 'ultimate' ? 760 : isWeak ? 570 : 390, kind === 'ultimate' ? 0.18 : 0.07, kind === 'ultimate' ? 'sawtooth' : 'triangle', 0.04);
   299	    if (livingEnemies().length === 0) { finishBattle(); return; }
   300	    state.battle.actions++;
   301	    const aliveCount = livingParty().length;
   302	    if (state.battle.actions >= Math.max(1, aliveCount)) enemyPhase();
   303	    else state.battle.activeHero = nextLivingHero(id);
   304	    chooseTarget();
   305	    saveGame();
   306	  }
   307	
   308	  function nextLivingHero(current) {
   309	    const list = state.activeParty;
   310	    const at = Math.max(0, list.indexOf(current));
   311	    for (let n = 1; n <= list.length; n++) { const id = list[(at + n) % list.length]; if (state.partyHp[id] > 0) return id; }
   312	    return firstLivingHero();
   313	  }
   314	
   315	  function healParty(amount) {
   316	    const targets = state.activeParty.filter(id => state.partyHp[id] > 0).sort((a, b) => state.partyHp[a] / HEROES[a].maxHp - state.partyHp[b] / HEROES[b].maxHp);
   317	    const id = targets[0];
   318	    if (id === undefined) return;
   319	    state.partyHp[id] = Math.min(HEROES[id].maxHp, state.partyHp[id] + amount);
   320	    popup(heroX(id), 376, `+${amount}`, '#d9ffad', 13);
   321	  }
   322	
   323	  function enemyPhase() {
   324	    state.battle.actions = 0; state.battle.round++;
   325	    livingEnemies().forEach(enemy => {
   326	      if (enemy.broken) {
   327	        enemy.broken = false; enemy.break = 0;
   328	        popup(enemyX(state.battle.enemies.indexOf(enemy)), enemyY(state.battle.enemies.indexOf(enemy)) - 75, 'STUN', '#ffe08b', 15);
   329	        return;
   330	      }
   331	      const alive = livingParty();
   332	      if (!alive.length) return;
   333	      const target = alive[Math.floor(Math.random() * alive.length)];
   334	      const damage = Math.max(1, Math.round(enemy.atk * (0.9 + Math.random() * 0.18) + state.battle.round * 0.5));
   335	      state.partyHp[target] = Math.max(0, state.partyHp[target] - damage);
   336	      popup(heroX(target), 376, `-${damage}`, '#ff9b91', 15);
   337	      burst(heroX(target), 400, '#ff6f65', 9); shake = 4;
   338	      if (state.partyHp[target] <= 0) notice(`${HEROES[target].name} is down.`);
   339	    });
   340	    state.battle.activeHero = firstLivingHero();
   341	    if (!livingParty().length) failBattle();
   342	    else saveGame();
   343	  }
   344	
   345	  function enemyX(index) {
   346	    const count = state.battle ? state.battle.enemies.length : 1;
   347	    return count === 1 ? 195 : index === 0 ? 118 : 272;
   348	  }
   349	  function enemyY(index) { return state.battle && state.battle.enemies.length > 1 ? 168 + (index === 1 ? 5 : 0) : 164; }
   350	  function heroX(id) { const at = state.activeParty.indexOf(id); return 55 + Math.max(0, at) * 94; }
   440	    if (!action) return;
   441	    if (action.type === 'continue') { startFromOverlay(true); return; }
   442	    if (action.type === 'new') { startFromOverlay(false); return; }
   443	    if (action.type === 'start') { startFromOverlay(saveAvailable); return; }
   444	    if (action.type === 'story') { unlockAudio(); advanceStory(); return; }
   445	    if (action.type === 'roster') { state.scene = 'roster'; saveGame(); return; }
   446	    if (action.type === 'rosterHero') { toggleRoster(action.id); return; }
   447	    if (action.type === 'rosterDone') { state.scene = 'story'; saveGame(); return; }
   448	    if (action.type === 'retry') { restartBattle(); return; }
   449	    if (action.type === 'restart') { restartBattle(); return; }
   450	    if (action.type === 'target' && state.battle && state.battle.enemies[action.index]?.hp > 0) { state.battle.target = action.index; notice(`${state.battle.enemies[action.index].name} targeted.`); tone(300, 0.05, 'sine', 0.02); return; }
   451	    if (action.type === 'inspect') { inspectElement = action.element; const foe = state.battle?.enemies[state.battle.target]; notice(`${action.element.toUpperCase()} ${foe && foe.weak === action.element ? 'breaks this target.' : 'does not break this target.'}`); return; }
   452	    if (action.type === 'hero') { if (state.partyHp[action.id] > 0) { state.battle.activeHero = action.id; notice(`${HEROES[action.id].name}'s turn.`); } else notice('That hero needs a rest.'); return; }
   453	    if (action.type === 'action') { playerAction(action.action); }
   454	  }

   456	  function onPointerDown(e) {
   457	    e.preventDefault(); unlockAudio();
   458	    if (pointerControls.size >= MAX_POINTERS) return;
   459	    if (!view.portrait) return;
   460	    const p = localPoint(e), control = state.scene === 'start' ? (saveAvailable && p.y >= 512 && p.y <= 585 ? { type: 'continue' } : { type: 'new' }) : hitTest(p.x, p.y);
   461	    pointerControls.set(e.pointerId, { control, x: p.x, y: p.y });
   462	    try { canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {}
   463	    if (control) enqueue(control);
   464	  }
   465	  function onPointerMove(e) { const p = pointerControls.get(e.pointerId); if (p) { const q = localPoint(e); p.x = q.x; p.y = q.y; } }
   466	  function onPointerUp(e) { e.preventDefault(); pointerControls.delete(e.pointerId); try { canvas.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {} }
   467	  function onPointerCancel(e) { pointerControls.delete(e.pointerId); }

   469	  function onKeyDown(e) {
   470	    const key = e.key.toLowerCase();
   471	    const allowed = ['arrowleft', 'arrowright', 'arrowup', 'arrowdown', '1', '2', '3', 'enter', 'r', 'p', 'n', ' '];
   472	    if (!allowed.includes(key)) return;
   473	    e.preventDefault();
   474	    if (heldKeys.has(key)) return;
   475	    if (heldKeys.size >= 32) return;
   476	    heldKeys.add(key);
   477	    unlockAudio();
   478	    if (!state.started && (key === 'enter' || key === ' ')) enqueue({ type: saveAvailable ? 'continue' : 'new' });
   479	    else if (key === 'n') enqueue({ type: 'new' });
   480	    else if (key === 'r' && (state.scene === 'battle' || state.scene === 'fail')) enqueue({ type: 'restart' });
   481	    else if (key === 'p' && state.scene === 'story') enqueue({ type: 'roster' });
   482	    else if (state.scene === 'story' && (key === 'enter' || key === ' ')) enqueue({ type: 'story' });
   483	    else if (state.scene === 'fail' && (key === 'enter' || key === ' ')) enqueue({ type: 'retry' });
   484	    else if (state.scene === 'roster') {
   485	      if (key === 'enter' || key === ' ') enqueue({ type: 'rosterDone' });
   486	      else if (key === 'arrowleft' || key === 'arrowright' || key === 'arrowup' || key === 'arrowdown') rosterMove(key);
   487	    } else if (state.scene === 'battle') {
   488	      if (key === 'arrowleft') enqueue({ type: 'targetMove', dir: -1 });
   489	      if (key === 'arrowright') enqueue({ type: 'targetMove', dir: 1 });
   490	      if (key === 'arrowup') enqueue({ type: 'heroMove', dir: -1 });
   491	      if (key === 'arrowdown') enqueue({ type: 'heroMove', dir: 1 });
   492	      if (key === '1') enqueue({ type: 'action', action: 'basic' });
   493	      if (key === '2') enqueue({ type: 'action', action: 'skill' });
   494	      if (key === '3') enqueue({ type: 'action', action: 'ultimate' });
   495	    }
   496	  }
   497	  function rosterMove(key) {
   498	    const delta = key === 'arrowleft' ? -1 : key === 'arrowright' ? 1 : key === 'arrowup' ? -2 : 2;
   499	    const unlocked = state.unlocked, current = state.rosterCursor;
   500	    const next = Math.max(0, Math.min(unlocked.length - 1, current + delta));
   501	    state.rosterCursor = next % 4;
   502	    const hero = unlocked[next]; if (hero !== undefined) enqueue({ type: 'rosterHero', id: hero });
   503	  }
   504	  function keyboardMove(action) {
   505	    if (!state.battle) return;
   506	    if (action.type === 'targetMove') {
   507	      const alive = state.battle.enemies.map((e, i) => e.hp > 0 ? i : -1).filter(i => i >= 0);
   508	      if (alive.length) { const at = Math.max(0, alive.indexOf(state.battle.target)); state.battle.target = alive[(at + action.dir + alive.length) % alive.length]; notice(`${state.battle.enemies[state.battle.target].name} targeted.`); }
   509	    }
   510	    if (action.type === 'heroMove') {
   511	      const alive = state.activeParty.filter(id => state.partyHp[id] > 0); if (alive.length) { const at = Math.max(0, alive.indexOf(state.battle.activeHero)); state.battle.activeHero = alive[(at + action.dir + alive.length) % alive.length]; notice(`${HEROES[state.battle.activeHero].name}'s turn.`); }
   512	    }
   513	  }
   514	
   515	  function update(dt) {
   516	    if (!view.portrait) return;
   517	    nowTime += dt; updateFx(dt);
   518	    if (!state.started) {
   519	      if (actionQueue.length) handleAction(actionQueue.shift());
   520	      return;
   521	    }
   522	    if (actionQueue.length) { const action = actionQueue.shift(); if (action.type === 'targetMove' || action.type === 'heroMove') keyboardMove(action); else handleAction(action); }
   523	    if (state.scene === 'battle') {
   524	      state.runTime += dt; state.saveClock += dt;
   525	      if (state.saveClock > 1.2) { state.saveClock = 0; saveGame(); }
   526	    }
   527	  }

   529	  function txt(value, x, y, size, color, align = 'left', weight = 500) {
   530	    ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.font = `${weight} ${size}px system-ui, sans-serif`; ctx.textAlign = align; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.fillText(value, x, y);

   689	    if (!view.portrait) { drawRotate(); return; }
   690	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(view.ox, view.oy); ctx.scale(view.scale, view.scale); ctx.translate((Math.random() - 0.5) * shake, (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake);
   691	    if (state.scene === 'start') drawStart(); else if (state.scene === 'story') drawStory(); else if (state.scene === 'roster') drawRoster(); else if (state.scene === 'battle') drawBattle(); else if (state.scene === 'fail') drawFail(); else drawWin();
   692	    ctx.restore();
   693	  }

   695	  function frame(t) {
   696	    const dt = lastFrame ? Math.min(0.05, Math.max(0, (t - lastFrame) / 1000)) : 0; lastFrame = t;
   697	    update(dt); draw(); requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   698	  }

    27	    { id: 3, name: 'CLOUDSTEP', x: 1120, y: 815, color: '#f8d285', ability: 'glide', gift: 'GLIDE · cross the high wind' }
    28	  ];
    29	  const chests = [
    30	    { id: 0, x: 855, y: 2310 }, { id: 1, x: 205, y: 1360 }, { id: 2, x: 930, y: 1470 },
    31	    { id: 3, x: 1830, y: 1760 }, { id: 4, x: 870, y: 690 }, { id: 5, x: 1930, y: 420 }

    35	  let viewport = { w: 390, h: 700, scale: 1 };
    36	  let orientationPaused = false;
    37	  let rafId = 0;
    38	  let previousTime = 0;
    39	  let audioContext = null;
    40	  let toastTimer = 0;
    41	  const timeoutIds = new Set();
    42	  const input = {
    43	    keys: new Set(), queued: [], held: { attack: false }, keySkillDown: false,
    44	    stick: { x: 0, y: 0, mag: 0 }, stickPointerId: null, controlPointers: new Map()
    45	  };

    51	  function finite(value, fallback, min, max) {
    52	    return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value)) : fallback;
    53	  }
    54	
    55	  function readSave() {
    56	    const blank = { shrineBits: 1, chestBits: 0, best: 0 };
    57	    try {
    58	      const raw = localStorage.getItem(SAVE_KEY);
    59	      if (typeof raw !== 'string' || raw.length > 2000) return blank;
    60	      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    61	      if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed)) return blank;
    62	      const shrineBits = Number.isInteger(parsed.shrineBits) ? (parsed.shrineBits & 15) | 1 : 1;
    63	      const chestBits = Number.isInteger(parsed.chestBits) ? (parsed.chestBits & 63) : 0;
    64	      const best = finite(parsed.best, 0, 0, 999999);
    65	      return { shrineBits, chestBits, best };
    66	    } catch (_) { return blank; }
    67	  }
    68	
    69	  function persist() {
    70	    try {
    71	      const safe = { shrineBits: Number.isInteger(saveData.shrineBits) ? saveData.shrineBits & 15 : 1, chestBits: Number.isInteger(saveData.chestBits) ? saveData.chestBits & 63 : 0, best: finite(saveData.best, 0, 0, 999999) };
    72	      localStorage.setItem(SAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(safe));
    73	    } catch (_) { /* Storage can be disabled; the run remains playable. */ }
    74	  }
    75	
    76	  function makePlayer() {
    77	    return { x: shrines[0].x, y: shrines[0].y, hp: 100, maxHp: 100, facing: -Math.PI / 2, char: 0, attackCd: 0, skillCd: 0, invuln: 0, dashTime: 0, dashX: 0, dashY: 0, respawnTime: 0, lastShrine: 0 };
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  function makeEnemies() {
    81	    const guardianPositions = [[330, 1740], [1660, 1510], [1120, 815]];
    82	    const list = [
    83	      { x: 930, y: 2230, kind: 'mote', hp: 42, damage: 8 }, { x: 630, y: 1920, kind: 'mote', hp: 48, damage: 9 },
    84	      { x: 790, y: 1310, kind: 'mote', hp: 45, damage: 9 }, { x: 1410, y: 1770, kind: 'guard', hp: 68, damage: 11 },
    85	      { x: 1870, y: 1380, kind: 'guard', hp: 72, damage: 12 }, { x: 1460, y: 1050, kind: 'mote', hp: 55, damage: 10 },
    86	      { x: 460, y: 1670, kind: 'guardian', hp: 105, damage: 13, shrine: 1 }, { x: 1510, y: 1550, kind: 'guardian', hp: 120, damage: 14, shrine: 2 },
    87	      { x: 1260, y: 750, kind: 'guardian', hp: 132, damage: 15, shrine: 3 },
    88	      { x: 1500, y: 310, kind: 'boss', hp: 270, damage: 18, boss: true }
    89	    ];
    90	    return list.slice(0, LIMITS.enemies).map((source, index) => {
    91	      const maxHp = source.hp;
    92	      const unlocked = source.shrine !== undefined && (saveData.shrineBits & (1 << source.shrine));
    93	      return { id: index, x: source.x, y: source.y, homeX: source.x, homeY: source.y, kind: source.kind, boss: !!source.boss, shrine: source.shrine, hp: unlocked ? 0 : maxHp, maxHp, damage: source.damage, element: source.shrine !== undefined ? 'frost' : '', statusTime: 0, hitCd: 0, stun: 0, attackTimer: 0, dead: !!unlocked, wake: false };
    94	    });
    95	  }
    96	
    97	  function resetRun() {
    98	    clearAllInput();
    99	    clearTrackedTimeouts();
   100	    particles.length = 0; projectiles.length = 0; floatTexts.length = 0;
   101	    player = makePlayer(); enemies = makeEnemies();
   102	    game = { phase: 'play', time: 0, score: 0, best: saveData.best, shake: 0, flash: 0, zone: 'MEADOW', respawnLock: false, hint: 'Walk the valley. Attune shrines to earn movement gifts.', abilities: { dash: !!(saveData.shrineBits & 2), lift: !!(saveData.shrineBits & 4), glide: !!(saveData.shrineBits & 8) } };
   103	    refs.modal.classList.remove('visible');
   104	    refs.hint.textContent = game.hint;
   105	    if (game.abilities.dash) showToast('YOUR MAP REMEMBERS · DASH READY', 1800);
   106	    updateUi();
   107	  }
   108	
   109	  function schedule(callback, delay) {
   110	    if (timeoutIds.size >= LIMITS.timeouts) return 0;
   111	    const id = window.setTimeout(() => { timeoutIds.delete(id); callback(); }, delay);
   112	    timeoutIds.add(id);
   113	    return id;
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  function clearTrackedTimeouts() {
   117	    timeoutIds.forEach(id => window.clearTimeout(id));
   118	    timeoutIds.clear();
   119	    if (toastTimer) { window.clearTimeout(toastTimer); toastTimer = 0; }
   120	  }
   121	
   122	  function showToast(message, duration = 1500) {
   123	    if (toastTimer) window.clearTimeout(toastTimer);
   124	    refs.toast.textContent = message;
   125	    refs.toast.classList.add('show');
   126	    toastTimer = schedule(() => { refs.toast.classList.remove('show'); toastTimer = 0; }, duration);
   127	  }
   128	
   129	  function unlockAudio() {
   130	    try {
   131	      if (!audioContext) audioContext = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
   132	      if (audioContext.state === 'suspended') audioContext.resume();
   133	    } catch (_) { audioContext = null; }
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  function tone(frequency, length = .07, type = 'sine', volume = .025) {
   137	    if (!audioContext) return;
   138	    try {
   139	      const now = audioContext.currentTime;
   140	      const osc = audioContext.createOscillator(); const gain = audioContext.createGain();
   141	      osc.type = type; osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, now); osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(45, frequency * .72), now + length);
   142	      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(volume, now); gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001, now + length);
   143	      osc.connect(gain).connect(audioContext.destination); osc.start(now); osc.stop(now + length + .01);
   144	    } catch (_) { /* Audio is decoration, never a dependency. */ }
   145	  }
   146	
   147	  function resize() {
   148	    const w = Math.max(1, window.innerWidth); const h = Math.max(1, window.innerHeight);
   149	    const dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
   150	    const scale = Math.min(dpr, 960 / Math.max(w, h));
   151	    viewport = { w, h, scale: Math.max(.1, scale) };
   152	    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(w * viewport.scale)); canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(h * viewport.scale));
   153	    ctx.setTransform(viewport.scale, 0, 0, viewport.scale, 0, 0);
   154	    checkOrientation();
   155	  }
   156	
   157	  function checkOrientation() {
   158	    const show = window.innerWidth > window.innerHeight;
   159	    if (show !== orientationPaused) {
   160	      orientationPaused = show;
   161	      refs.rotate.classList.toggle('visible', show);
   162	      clearAllInput();
   163	      previousTime = performance.now();
   164	    } else refs.rotate.classList.toggle('visible', show);
   165	  }
   166	
   167	  function clearAllInput() {
   168	    input.keys.clear(); input.queued.length = 0; input.held.attack = false; input.keySkillDown = false;
   169	    input.stick.x = 0; input.stick.y = 0; input.stick.mag = 0; input.stickPointerId = null; input.controlPointers.clear();
   170	    refs.thumb.style.transform = 'translate(0, 0)'; refs.attack.classList.remove('held');
   171	  }
   172	
   173	  function queueAction(action) {
   174	    if (input.queued.length >= 12) input.queued.shift();
   175	    input.queued.push(action);
   176	  }
   177	
   178	  function takeAction(action) {
   179	    const index = input.queued.indexOf(action);
   180	    if (index < 0) return false;
   181	    input.queued.splice(index, 1); return true;
   182	  }
   183	
   184	  function bindControls() {
   185	    const press = (element, name, onDown, onUp) => {
   186	      element.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
   187	        event.preventDefault(); unlockAudio();
   188	        if (input.controlPointers.has(name)) return;
   189	        input.controlPointers.set(name, event.pointerId); element.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId); onDown(event);
   190	      }, { passive: false });
   191	      const release = event => { if (input.controlPointers.get(name) !== event.pointerId) return; event.preventDefault(); input.controlPointers.delete(name); onUp?.(event); };
   192	      element.addEventListener('pointerup', release, { passive: false }); element.addEventListener('pointercancel', release, { passive: false });
   193	    };
   194	    refs.stick.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
   195	      event.preventDefault(); unlockAudio(); if (input.stickPointerId !== null) return;
   196	      input.stickPointerId = event.pointerId; refs.stick.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId); updateStick(event);
   197	    }, { passive: false });
   198	    const endStick = event => { if (input.stickPointerId !== event.pointerId) return; event.preventDefault(); input.stickPointerId = null; input.stick.x = 0; input.stick.y = 0; input.stick.mag = 0; refs.thumb.style.transform = 'translate(0, 0)'; };
   199	    refs.stick.addEventListener('pointermove', event => { if (input.stickPointerId === event.pointerId) { event.preventDefault(); updateStick(event); } }, { passive: false });
   200	    refs.stick.addEventListener('pointerup', endStick, { passive: false }); refs.stick.addEventListener('pointercancel', endStick, { passive: false });
   201	    press(refs.attack, 'attack', () => { input.held.attack = true; refs.attack.classList.add('held'); }, () => { input.held.attack = false; refs.attack.classList.remove('held'); });
   202	    press(refs.skill, 'skill', () => queueAction('skill'));
   203	    refs.chips.forEach(chip => press(chip, `chip-${chip.dataset.slot}`, () => queueAction(`swap-${chip.dataset.slot}`)));
   204	    press(refs.restart, 'restart', () => resetRun());
   205	    canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => { event.preventDefault(); unlockAudio(); }, { passive: false });
   206	    window.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
   207	      unlockAudio(); const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
   208	      if (['w', 'a', 's', 'd', 'j', 'k', '1', '2', '3', ' '].includes(key)) event.preventDefault();
   209	      if (key === 'j') { input.keys.add(key); input.held.attack = true; refs.attack.classList.add('held'); }
   210	      else if (key === 'k') { if (!input.keySkillDown) queueAction('skill'); input.keySkillDown = true; input.keys.add(key); }
   211	      else if (key >= '1' && key <= '3') queueAction(`swap-${Number(key) - 1}`);
   212	      else if (key === 'w' || key === 'a' || key === 's' || key === 'd') input.keys.add(key);
   213	    }, { passive: false });
   214	    window.addEventListener('keyup', event => { const key = event.key.toLowerCase(); input.keys.delete(key); if (key === 'j') { input.held.attack = false; refs.attack.classList.remove('held'); } if (key === 'k') input.keySkillDown = false; }, { passive: false });
   215	    window.addEventListener('blur', clearAllInput);
   216	    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if (document.hidden) clearAllInput(); });
   217	    document.addEventListener('touchmove', event => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   218	  }
   219	
   220	  function updateStick(event) {
   221	    const rect = refs.stick.getBoundingClientRect(); const cx = rect.left + rect.width / 2; const cy = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
   222	    const max = rect.width * .37; let dx = event.clientX - cx; let dy = event.clientY - cy; const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dy); const mag = Math.min(1, distance / max);
   223	    if (distance > max) { dx = dx / distance * max; dy = dy / distance * max; }
   224	    input.stick.x = dx / max; input.stick.y = dy / max; input.stick.mag = mag; refs.thumb.style.transform = `translate(${dx}px, ${dy}px)`;
   225	  }
   226	
   227	  function keyVector() {
   228	    let x = 0, y = 0; if (input.keys.has('a')) x -= 1; if (input.keys.has('d')) x += 1; if (input.keys.has('w')) y -= 1; if (input.keys.has('s')) y += 1;
   229	    const length = Math.hypot(x, y); return length ? { x: x / length, y: y / length, mag: 1 } : input.stick;
   230	  }
   230	  }
   231	
   232	  function clampWorld(x, y) { return { x: Math.max(32, Math.min(WORLD.w - 32, x)), y: Math.max(32, Math.min(WORLD.h - 32, y)) }; }
   233	  function dist(a, b) { return Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y); }
   234	  function insideLake(x, y) { const dx = (x - WORLD.lake.x) / WORLD.lake.rx, dy = (y - WORLD.lake.y) / WORLD.lake.ry; return dx * dx + dy * dy < 1; }
   235	  function shrineUnlocked(id) { return !!(saveData.shrineBits & (1 << id)); }
   236	
   237	  function gateMessage(x, y) {
   238	    if (!game.abilities.dash && x > 1240 && x < 1300 && y > 1400 && y < 1980) return 'DRIFTGLASS SHRINE: find a way through the blue break.';
   239	    if (!game.abilities.lift && y < 1080 && y > 1000 && x > 1000 && x < 1900) return 'THE RUIN STAIR IS PINNED. SEEK THE ARCHIVE SHRINE.';
   240	    if (!game.abilities.glide && y < 620 && y > 560 && x > 950 && x < 1850) return 'THE HIGH WIND NEEDS A GLIDE. ATTUNE CLOUDSTEP.';
   241	    return '';
   242	  }
   243	
   244	  function canMoveTo(x, y) {
   245	    if (player.dashTime > 0) return true;
   246	    return !gateMessage(x, y);
   247	  }
   248	
   249	  function movement(dt) {
   250	    const move = keyVector(); if (!move.mag) return;
   251	    if (player.dashTime > 0) { const dashNext = clampWorld(player.x + player.dashX * dt, player.y + player.dashY * dt); player.x = dashNext.x; player.y = dashNext.y; return; }
   252	    player.facing = Math.atan2(move.y, move.x);
   253	    const speed = 205; const nx = player.x + move.x * speed * move.mag * dt; const ny = player.y + move.y * speed * move.mag * dt; const next = clampWorld(nx, ny);
   254	    if (canMoveTo(next.x, player.y)) player.x = next.x;
   255	    if (canMoveTo(player.x, next.y)) player.y = next.y;
   256	  }
   257	
   258	  function performDash() {
   259	    if (!game.abilities.dash || player.skillCd > 0) return false;
   260	    const move = keyVector(); if (!move.mag) return false;
   261	    player.dashTime = .24; player.dashX = move.x * 880; player.dashY = move.y * 880; player.skillCd = .38; player.invuln = .32;
   262	    burst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.mist, 16, 2); tone(230, .12, 'triangle', .04); game.shake = Math.max(game.shake, 5); return true;
   263	  }
   264	
   265	  function processActions() {
   266	    if (takeAction('skill') && !performDash()) useSkill();
   267	    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) if (takeAction(`swap-${i}`)) swapCharacter(i);
   268	    if (input.held.attack) attack();
   269	  }
   270	
   271	  function swapCharacter(index) {
   272	    if (!characters[index] || player.char === index) return;
   273	    player.char = index; player.attackCd = .12; burst(player.x, player.y, characters[index].color, 8, 1); tone(180 + index * 80, .07, 'sine', .025); showToast(`${characters[index].name} · ${characters[index].role}`, 650);
   274	  }
   275	
   276	  function attack() {
   277	    if (player.attackCd > 0 || game.phase !== 'play') return;
   278	    const current = characters[player.char]; player.attackCd = player.char === 1 ? .36 : .28; tone(player.char === 0 ? 120 : player.char === 1 ? 430 : 690, .07, player.char === 0 ? 'sawtooth' : 'sine', .035);
   279	    if (player.char === 0) {
   280	      burst(player.x + Math.cos(player.facing) * 42, player.y + Math.sin(player.facing) * 42, COLORS.ember, 9, 1);
   281	      enemies.forEach(enemy => { if (!enemy.dead && dist(player, enemy) < (enemy.boss ? 74 : 82) && inFront(enemy, 1.15)) applyHit(enemy, 25, 'strike'); });
   282	    } else if (player.char === 1) fireProjectile(player.x, player.y, player.facing, 620, 22, 'frost', COLORS.frost, 1);
   283	    else fireProjectile(player.x, player.y, player.facing, 440, 20, 'spark', COLORS.spark, 2);
   284	  }
   285	
   286	  function useSkill() {
   287	    if (player.skillCd > 0 || game.phase !== 'play') return;
   288	    player.skillCd = player.char === 1 ? 2.7 : 2.2; const angle = player.facing;
   289	    if (player.char === 0) {
   290	      burst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.ember, 28, 3); game.flash = .12;
   291	      enemies.forEach(enemy => { if (!enemy.dead && dist(player, enemy) < 140) applyHit(enemy, 48, 'ember'); }); tone(150, .2, 'sawtooth', .05);
   292	    } else if (player.char === 1) {
   293	      for (let i = -2; i <= 2; i++) fireProjectile(player.x, player.y, angle + i * .12, 550, 28, 'frost', COLORS.frost, 1);
   294	      burst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.frost, 18, 2); tone(480, .18, 'sine', .045);
   295	    } else {
   296	      burst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.spark, 22, 2);
   297	      enemies.forEach(enemy => { if (!enemy.dead && dist(player, enemy) < 190) applyHit(enemy, 36, 'spark'); });
   298	      tone(740, .2, 'square', .035);
   299	    }
   300	  }
   301	
   302	  function inFront(target, halfAngle) {
   303	    const targetAngle = Math.atan2(target.y - player.y, target.x - player.x); let delta = Math.atan2(Math.sin(targetAngle - player.facing), Math.cos(targetAngle - player.facing)); return Math.abs(delta) < halfAngle;
   304	  }
   305	
   306	  function fireProjectile(x, y, angle, speed, damage, element, color, owner) {
   307	    if (projectiles.length >= LIMITS.projectiles) projectiles.splice(0, Math.ceil(LIMITS.projectiles * .08));
   308	    projectiles.push({ x: x + Math.cos(angle) * 24, y: y + Math.sin(angle) * 24, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed, vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed, life: 1.1, damage, element, color, owner, radius: owner === 2 ? 13 : 8 });
   309	  }
   310	
   311	  function applyHit(target, baseDamage, element, chained = false) {
   312	    if (!target || target.dead) return;
   313	    let damage = baseDamage; let combo = '';
   314	    const old = target.element;
   315	    if ((element === 'spark' && old === 'wet') || (element === 'wet' && old === 'spark')) { damage *= 1.8; combo = 'CHAIN SHOCK'; target.stun = 1.1; shockChain(target); }
   316	    else if ((element === 'strike' && old === 'frost') || (element === 'frost' && old === 'strike')) { damage *= 1.7; combo = 'SHATTER'; target.stun = .7; burst(target.x, target.y, COLORS.paper, 18, 3); }
   317	    else if ((element === 'ember' && old === 'wet') || (element === 'wet' && old === 'ember')) { player.hp = Math.min(player.maxHp, player.hp + 8); combo = 'STEAM HEAL +8'; burst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.mist, 12, 2); }
   318	    target.hp -= damage; target.element = element; target.statusTime = 4;
   319	    floatText(target.x, target.y - 30, combo || `-${Math.round(damage)}`, combo ? COLORS.paper : colorForElement(element));
   320	    burst(target.x, target.y, colorForElement(element), combo ? 11 : 5, combo ? 2 : 1); game.shake = Math.max(game.shake, combo ? 7 : 2);
   321	    if (combo) { floatText(target.x, target.y - 52, combo, COLORS.paper); tone(combo === 'CHAIN SHOCK' ? 780 : combo === 'SHATTER' ? 220 : 360, .14, 'triangle', .04); }
   322	    if (target.hp <= 0) defeatEnemy(target);
   323	  }
   324	
   325	  function shockChain(source) {
   326	    let chained = 0;
   327	    enemies.forEach(enemy => { if (enemy !== source && !enemy.dead && chained < 2 && dist(source, enemy) < 155) { enemy.stun = .65; enemy.hp -= 12; burst(enemy.x, enemy.y, COLORS.spark, 7, 1); chained++; if (enemy.hp <= 0) defeatEnemy(enemy); } });
   328	  }
   329	
   330	  function colorForElement(element) { return element === 'ember' ? COLORS.ember : element === 'frost' ? COLORS.frost : element === 'spark' ? COLORS.spark : element === 'wet' ? COLORS.wet : COLORS.strike; }
   331	
   332	  function defeatEnemy(enemy) {
   333	    if (enemy.dead) return; enemy.dead = true; enemy.hp = 0; game.score += enemy.boss ? 1500 : enemy.shrine !== undefined ? 260 : 80; burst(enemy.x, enemy.y, enemy.boss ? COLORS.paper : colorForElement(enemy.element || 'strike'), enemy.boss ? 70 : 24, enemy.boss ? 4 : 2); game.shake = enemy.boss ? 16 : 7; tone(enemy.boss ? 90 : 180, enemy.boss ? .5 : .16, 'triangle', .05);
   334	    if (enemy.boss) { completeRun(); return; }
   335	    if (enemy.shrine !== undefined) showToast('GUARDIAN SILENCED · ATTUNE THE SHRINE', 1500);
   336	  }
   337	
   338	  function updateProjectiles(dt) {
   339	    for (let i = projectiles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   340	      const shot = projectiles[i]; shot.x += shot.vx * dt; shot.y += shot.vy * dt; shot.life -= dt;
   341	      let remove = shot.life <= 0 || shot.x < 0 || shot.x > WORLD.w || shot.y < 0 || shot.y > WORLD.h;
   342	      if (!remove && shot.owner !== 0) {
   343	        for (const enemy of enemies) if (!enemy.dead && Math.hypot(enemy.x - shot.x, enemy.y - shot.y) < shot.radius + (enemy.boss ? 25 : 18)) { applyHit(enemy, shot.damage, shot.element); remove = true; break; }
   344	      } else if (!remove && shot.owner === 0 && Math.hypot(player.x - shot.x, player.y - shot.y) < shot.radius + 18) { harmPlayer(shot.damage); remove = true; }
   345	      if (remove) projectiles.splice(i, 1);
   346	    }
   347	  }
   348	
   349	  function updateEnemies(dt) {
   350	    enemies.forEach(enemy => {
   351	      if (enemy.dead) return; enemy.hitCd -= dt; enemy.stun -= dt; enemy.statusTime -= dt; enemy.attackTimer -= dt;
   352	      if (enemy.statusTime <= 0 && enemy.element !== 'wet') enemy.element = '';
   353	      if (insideLake(enemy.x, enemy.y)) { enemy.element = 'wet'; enemy.statusTime = Math.max(enemy.statusTime, .2); }
   354	      const distance = dist(player, enemy); const aggro = enemy.boss ? 680 : enemy.kind === 'guardian' ? 470 : 360;
   355	      if (distance < aggro) enemy.wake = true;
   356	      if (enemy.stun > 0) return;
   357	      if (enemy.boss && enemy.wake && enemy.attackTimer <= 0) { enemy.attackTimer = 1.25; const a = Math.atan2(player.y - enemy.y, player.x - enemy.x); fireProjectile(enemy.x, enemy.y, a, 250, 10, 'spark', COLORS.spark, 0); burst(enemy.x, enemy.y, COLORS.spark, 7, 1); }
   358	      if (enemy.wake && distance > (enemy.boss ? 100 : 42)) { const angle = Math.atan2(player.y - enemy.y, player.x - enemy.x); const speed = enemy.boss ? 56 : enemy.kind === 'guardian' ? 46 : 64; enemy.x += Math.cos(angle) * speed * dt; enemy.y += Math.sin(angle) * speed * dt; }
   359	      if (distance < (enemy.boss ? 96 : 45) && enemy.hitCd <= 0) { enemy.hitCd = enemy.boss ? 1.05 : 1.3; harmPlayer(enemy.damage); }
   360	    });
   361	  }
   362	
   363	  function harmPlayer(damage) {
   364	    if (player.invuln > 0 || player.respawnTime > 0 || game.phase !== 'play') return;
   365	    player.hp -= damage; player.invuln = .42; game.flash = .14; game.shake = Math.max(game.shake, 7); floatText(player.x, player.y - 35, `-${damage}`, '#ff8b79'); tone(80, .12, 'sawtooth', .035);
   366	    if (player.hp <= 0) respawnAtShrine();
   367	  }
   368	
   369	  function respawnAtShrine() {
   370	    const shrine = shrines[player.lastShrine] || shrines[0]; player.x = shrine.x; player.y = shrine.y; player.hp = player.maxHp; player.respawnTime = 1.05; player.invuln = 1.4; game.score = Math.max(0, game.score - 30); burst(player.x, player.y, shrine.color, 32, 3); showToast(`RETURNED TO ${shrine.name}`, 1250); tone(280, .25, 'sine', .04);
   371	  }
   372	
   373	  function updateShrines() {
   374	    shrines.forEach(shrine => {
   375	      if (shrineUnlocked(shrine.id)) { if (dist(player, shrine) < 105) player.lastShrine = shrine.id; return; }
   376	      if (dist(player, shrine) < 100) {
   377	        const guardian = enemies.find(enemy => enemy.shrine === shrine.id);
   378	        if (guardian && guardian.dead) {
   379	          saveData.shrineBits |= 1 << shrine.id; persist(); game.abilities[shrine.ability] = true; player.lastShrine = shrine.id; game.score += 300; burst(shrine.x, shrine.y, shrine.color, 50, 3); floatText(shrine.x, shrine.y - 85, shrine.gift, shrine.color); showToast(`${shrine.name} ATTUNED · ${shrine.gift}`, 2200); tone(390 + shrine.id * 120, .34, 'triangle', .05);
   380	        } else if (game.time % 2 < .03) showToast('A GUARDIAN HOLDS THIS SHRINE', 900);
   381	      }
   382	    });
   383	  }
   384	
   385	  function updateChests() {
   386	    chests.forEach(chest => {
   387	      if (saveData.chestBits & (1 << chest.id)) return;
   388	      if (Math.hypot(player.x - chest.x, player.y - chest.y) < 48) {
   389	        saveData.chestBits |= 1 << chest.id; persist(); player.maxHp = Math.min(150, player.maxHp + 8); player.hp = Math.min(player.maxHp, player.hp + 25); game.score += 120; burst(chest.x, chest.y, COLORS.spark, 20, 2); showToast('CACHE OPEN · VITAL +8', 1100); tone(560, .18, 'sine', .04);
   390	      }
   391	    });
   392	  }
   393	
   394	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   395	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const p = particles[i]; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= .97; p.vy *= .97; p.life -= dt; if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1); }
   396	    for (let i = floatTexts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const t = floatTexts[i]; t.y -= 22 * dt; t.life -= dt; if (t.life <= 0) floatTexts.splice(i, 1); }
   397	  }
   398	
   399	  function addParticle(x, y, color, life, size, vx, vy) {
   400	    if (particles.length >= LIMITS.particles) particles.splice(0, Math.ceil(LIMITS.particles * .08)); particles.push({ x, y, color, life, maxLife: life, size, vx, vy });
   401	  }
   402	
   403	  function burst(x, y, color, count = 8, force = 1) {
   404	    const safeCount = Math.min(count, 70);
   405	    for (let i = 0; i < safeCount; i++) { const a = Math.random() * TAU; const speed = (20 + Math.random() * 75) * force; addParticle(x, y, color, .35 + Math.random() * .5, 2 + Math.random() * 3, Math.cos(a) * speed, Math.sin(a) * speed); }
   406	  }
   407	
   408	  function floatText(x, y, text, color) {
   409	    if (floatTexts.length >= LIMITS.texts) floatTexts.splice(0, 2); floatTexts.push({ x, y, text, color, life: 1.05 });
   410	  }
   411	
   412	  function zoneAt(x, y) { if (y < 950) return 'PEAK'; if (x > 1250 && y < 2150) return 'RUIN'; if (insideLake(x, y)) return 'LAKE'; return 'MEADOW'; }
   413	
   414	  function update(dt) {
   415	    if (game.phase !== 'play' || orientationPaused) return;
   416	    game.time += dt; player.attackCd = Math.max(0, player.attackCd - dt); player.skillCd = Math.max(0, player.skillCd - dt); player.invuln = Math.max(0, player.invuln - dt); player.respawnTime = Math.max(0, player.respawnTime - dt); player.dashTime = Math.max(0, player.dashTime - dt); game.shake = Math.max(0, game.shake - dt * 20); game.flash = Math.max(0, game.flash - dt);
   417	    processActions(); movement(dt); updateProjectiles(dt); updateEnemies(dt); updateShrines(); updateChests(); updateParticles(dt);
   418	    game.zone = zoneAt(player.x, player.y); refs.zone.textContent = game.zone;
   419	    const active = characters[player.char]; game.hint = !game.abilities.dash ? 'Find the lake guardian, then touch the Driftglass shrine.' : !game.abilities.lift ? 'Dash through the blue break. The ruin guardian guards LIFT.' : !game.abilities.glide ? 'Lift the ruin stair. Cloudstep waits in the pale wind.' : 'Glide through the high wind and silence the valley boss.';
   420	    refs.hint.textContent = game.hint; updateUi();
   421	    if (game.time > 99999) game.time = 0;
   422	  }

   424	  function updateUi() {

   426	  }

   503	  function drawPlayer() {

# Skyshard Vale
Move: left stick or WASD. Attack: ATTACK or J; Skill: SKILL or K; swap with party chips or 1–3.
Explore meadow, lake, ruin, and peak; defeat shrine guardians to earn Dash, Lift, and Glide.
Wet + Spark = Chain Shock; Frost + Strike = Shatter; Ember + Wet = Steam Heal. Chests persist.
Defeat returns you to your last shrine; defeat the high shard boss to clear the valley.

   302	  function inFront(target, halfAngle) {
   303	    const targetAngle = Math.atan2(target.y - player.y, target.x - player.x); let delta = Math.atan2(Math.sin(targetAngle - player.facing), Math.cos(targetAngle - player.facing)); return Math.abs(delta) < halfAngle;
   304	  }

   306	  function fireProjectile(x, y, angle, speed, damage, element, color, owner) {
   307	    if (projectiles.length >= LIMITS.projectiles) projectiles.splice(0, Math.ceil(LIMITS.projectiles * .08));
   308	    projectiles.push({ x: x + Math.cos(angle) * 24, y: y + Math.sin(angle) * 24, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed, vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed, life: 1.1, damage, element, color, owner, radius: owner === 2 ? 13 : 8 });
   309	  }

   311	  function applyHit(target, baseDamage, element, chained = false) {
   312	    if (!target || target.dead) return;
   313	    let damage = baseDamage; let combo = '';
   314	    const old = target.element;
   315	    if ((element === 'spark' && old === 'wet') || (element === 'wet' && old === 'spark')) { damage *= 1.8; combo = 'CHAIN SHOCK'; target.stun = 1.1; shockChain(target); }
   316	    else if ((element === 'strike' && old === 'frost') || (element === 'frost' && old === 'strike')) { damage *= 1.7; combo = 'SHATTER'; target.stun = .7; burst(target.x, target.y, COLORS.paper, 18, 3); }
   317	    else if ((element === 'ember' && old === 'wet') || (element === 'wet' && old === 'ember')) { player.hp = Math.min(player.maxHp, player.hp + 8); combo = 'STEAM HEAL +8'; burst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.mist, 12, 2); }
   318	    target.hp -= damage; target.element = element; target.statusTime = 4;
   319	    floatText(target.x, target.y - 30, combo || `-${Math.round(damage)}`, combo ? COLORS.paper : colorForElement(element));
   320	    burst(target.x, target.y, colorForElement(element), combo ? 11 : 5, combo ? 2 : 1); game.shake = Math.max(game.shake, combo ? 7 : 2);
   321	    if (combo) { floatText(target.x, target.y - 52, combo, COLORS.paper); tone(combo === 'CHAIN SHOCK' ? 780 : combo === 'SHATTER' ? 220 : 360, .14, 'triangle', .04); }
   322	    if (target.hp <= 0) defeatEnemy(target);
   323	  }
   324	
   325	  function shockChain(source) {
   326	    let chained = 0;
   327	    enemies.forEach(enemy => { if (enemy !== source && !enemy.dead && chained < 2 && dist(source, enemy) < 155) { enemy.stun = .65; enemy.hp -= 12; burst(enemy.x, enemy.y, COLORS.spark, 7, 1); chained++; if (enemy.hp <= 0) defeatEnemy(enemy); } });
   328	  }
   329	
   330	  function colorForElement(element) { return element === 'ember' ? COLORS.ember : element === 'frost' ? COLORS.frost : element === 'spark' ? COLORS.spark : element === 'wet' ? COLORS.wet : COLORS.strike; }

    70	  function finiteNumber(value, fallback, min, max) {
    71	    return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? clamp(value, min, max) : fallback;

   103	  function isKnownBaseType(type) { return type === 'core' || type === 'storage' || !!BUILDING_META[type]; }
   104	  function validateSave(value) {
   105	    if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return null;
   106	    const buildings = Array.isArray(value.baseBuildings) ? value.baseBuildings.slice(0, MAX_BUILDINGS).filter(item => item && typeof item === 'object' && isKnownBaseType(item.type)) : [];
   107	    const safeBuildings = buildings.map(item => ({
   108	      type: item.type, col: finiteInt(item.col, 0, 0, 7), row: finiteInt(item.row, 0, 0, 7), level: finiteInt(item.level, 1, 1, 5)
   109	    }));
   110	    const stars = Array.isArray(value.progressStars) ? value.progressStars.slice(0, 12).map(n => finiteInt(n, 0, 0, 3)) : Array(12).fill(0);
   111	    while (stars.length < 12) stars.push(0);
   112	    const logs = Array.isArray(value.defenseLog) ? value.defenseLog.slice(0, MAX_DEFENSE_LOG).filter(item => item && typeof item === 'object').map(item => ({
   113	      at: finiteNumber(item.at, Date.now(), 0, Date.now() + 86400000), bot: typeof item.bot === 'string' ? item.bot.slice(0, 24) : 'Unknown scout',
   114	      result: typeof item.result === 'string' ? item.result.slice(0, 16) : 'SCOUTED', loss: finiteInt(item.loss, 0, 0, 9999), note: typeof item.note === 'string' ? item.note.slice(0, 28) : 'quiet night'
   115	    })) : [];
   116	    return {
   117	      gold: finiteInt(value.gold, 460, 0, 999999), mana: finiteInt(value.mana, 360, 0, 999999), trophies: finiteInt(value.trophies, 0, 0, 9999),
   118	      progressStars: stars, crown: stars.every(n => n > 0), baseBuildings: safeBuildings.length ? safeBuildings : defaultBuildings(),
   119	      defenseLog: logs.length ? logs : defaultDefenseLog(), lastSeen: finiteNumber(value.lastSeen, Date.now(), 0, Date.now() + 86400000)
   120	    };
   121	  }
   122	  function readSave() {
   123	    try {
   124	      const raw = localStorage.getItem(SAVE_KEY);
   125	      if (!raw) return null;
   126	      return validateSave(JSON.parse(raw));
   127	    } catch (error) { return null; }
   128	  }
   129	  function saveGame() {
   130	    try {
   131	      const payload = {
   132	        gold: finiteInt(state.gold, 0, 0, 999999), mana: finiteInt(state.mana, 0, 0, 999999), trophies: finiteInt(state.trophies, 0, 0, 9999),
   133	        progressStars: state.progressStars.slice(0, 12).map(n => finiteInt(n, 0, 0, 3)), crown: !!state.crown,
   134	        baseBuildings: state.baseBuildings.slice(0, MAX_BUILDINGS), defenseLog: state.defenseLog.slice(0, MAX_DEFENSE_LOG), lastSeen: Date.now()
   135	      };
   136	      localStorage.setItem(SAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
   137	    } catch (error) { /* private mode and full storage are valid play conditions */ }
   138	  }
   139	  function simulateBetweenSessions() {
   140	    const previous = state.lastSeen;
   141	    const gap = Date.now() - previous;
   142	    if (previous > 0 && gap > 90000) {
   143	      const index = Math.floor(previous / 3600000) % 12;
   144	      const bot = VILLAGES[index].name;
   145	      const loss = Math.min(state.gold, 16 + (index * 7) % 34);
   146	      state.gold = Math.max(0, state.gold - loss);
   147	      state.defenseLog.unshift({ at: Date.now(), bot, result: loss ? 'SCRAPED' : 'REPELLED', loss, note: loss ? 'fair simulated raid' : 'walls held' });
   148	      state.defenseLog = state.defenseLog.slice(0, MAX_DEFENSE_LOG);
   149	    }
   150	    state.lastSeen = Date.now();
   151	  }
