   473	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(unit.x, unit.y, unit.slot === 4 ? 11 : 8, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = '#14293a'; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   474	      text(unit.slot === 0 ? 'B' : unit.slot === 1 ? 'A' : unit.slot === 2 ? 'W' : unit.slot === 3 ? '+' : 'G', unit.x, unit.y + 4, 9, color, 900, 'center');
   475	      const ratio = clamp(unit.hp / unit.maxHp, 0, 1); ctx.fillStyle = '#07111b'; ctx.fillRect(unit.x - 10, unit.y - 16, 20, 2); ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.aqua; ctx.fillRect(unit.x - 10, unit.y - 16, 20 * ratio, 2);
   476	    });
   477	  }
   478	  function drawDeployCursor(raid) {
   479	    const p = raid.cursor; ctx.globalAlpha = .22; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 20, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.aqua; ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 11, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.aqua; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke(); line(p.x - 16, p.y, p.x + 16, p.y, COLORS.aqua, 1); line(p.x, p.y - 16, p.x, p.y + 16, COLORS.aqua, 1);
   480	  }
   481	  function drawTroopHand(raid) {
   482	    TROOPS.forEach((troop, index) => {
   483	      const x = 5 + index * 77, y = 582, selected = raid.selected === index;
   484	      roundRect(x, y, 72, 108, 10, selected ? '#203d49' : '#111e2e', selected ? troop.color : COLORS.line, selected ? 2 : 1);
   485	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x + 36, y + 25, 14, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = troop.color; ctx.globalAlpha = .2; ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   486	      text(index + 1, x + 12, y + 15, 9, selected ? troop.color : COLORS.muted, 900); text(troop.key === 'breaker' ? 'W' : troop.key[0].toUpperCase(), x + 36, y + 31, 17, troop.color, 900, 'center');
   487	      label(troop.label === 'WALL-BREAKER' ? 'BREAKER' : troop.label, x + 36, y + 52, 7, COLORS.text, 'center');
   488	      text(`x${raid.hand[index]}`, x + 36, y + 74, 13, raid.hand[index] ? troop.color : COLORS.danger, 900, 'center'); label(troop.role.toUpperCase(), x + 36, y + 94, 7, COLORS.muted, 'center');
   489	    });
   490	  }
   491	  function drawRaidResult(raid) {
   492	    ctx.globalAlpha = .9; roundRect(29, 184, 332, 220, 16, '#0b1422', '#5a7386', 2); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   493	    label(raid.result === 'VICTORY' ? 'VILLAGE BREACHED' : 'RAID ENDED', 195, 218, 10, raid.result === 'VICTORY' ? COLORS.aqua : COLORS.danger, 'center');
   494	    text(raid.result, 195, 250, 28, COLORS.text, 900, 'center');
   495	    text(raid.stars ? '★'.repeat(raid.stars) + '☆'.repeat(3 - raid.stars) : '☆☆☆', 195, 284, 24, raid.stars === 3 ? COLORS.gold : COLORS.aqua, 900, 'center');
   496	    text(`${Math.floor(raid.percent)}% destroyed`, 195, 311, 12, COLORS.muted, 700, 'center');
   497	    text(`+${raid.lootGold} ✦    +${raid.lootMana} ◆`, 195, 337, 14, COLORS.gold, 900, 'center');
   498	    roundRect(47, 357, 137, 38, 9, '#1d3e4d', COLORS.aqua, 1); label('REPLAY', 115, 381, 10, COLORS.aqua, 'center');
   499	    roundRect(206, 357, 137, 38, 9, '#18273b', COLORS.line, 1); label('LADDER', 274, 381, 10, COLORS.text, 'center');
   500	  }
   501	
   502	  function nearestRaidTarget(unit, raid) {
   503	    const alive = raid.buildings.filter(item => item.alive);
   504	    if (!alive.length) return null;
   505	    let candidates = alive;
   506	    if (TROOPS[unit.slot].role === 'breaker') {
   507	      const walls = alive.filter(item => item.type === 'wall'); candidates = walls.length ? walls : alive;
   508	    } else if (TROOPS[unit.slot].role === 'giant') {
   509	      const defenses = alive.filter(item => item.type === 'cannon' || item.type === 'archer'); candidates = defenses.length ? defenses : alive;
   510	    } else if (TROOPS[unit.slot].role === 'healer') {
   511	      const wounded = raid.units.filter(item => !item.dead && item.slot !== 3 && item.hp < item.maxHp * .9);
   512	      if (wounded.length) { wounded.sort((a, b) => dist(unit.x, unit.y, a.x, a.y) - dist(unit.x, unit.y, b.x, b.y)); return wounded[0]; }
   513	      const friends = raid.units.filter(item => !item.dead && item.slot !== 3); if (friends.length) { friends.sort((a, b) => dist(unit.x, unit.y, a.x, a.y) - dist(unit.x, unit.y, b.x, b.y)); return friends[0]; }
   514	      return null;
   515	    }
   516	    candidates.sort((a, b) => dist(unit.x, unit.y, a.x, a.y) - dist(unit.x, unit.y, b.x, b.y)); return candidates[0];
   517	  }
   518	  function finishRaid(raid) {
   519	    if (raid.over) return;
   520	    const total = raid.buildings.length, destroyed = raid.buildings.filter(item => !item.alive).length;
   521	    raid.percent = total ? destroyed / total * 100 : 0;
   522	    const coreGone = raid.buildings.some(item => item.type === 'core' && !item.alive);
   523	    raid.stars = coreGone ? 1 : 0; if (raid.percent >= 50) raid.stars = Math.max(raid.stars, 2); if (raid.percent >= 99.9) raid.stars = 3;
   524	    const village = raid.village, previous = state.progressStars[raid.index] || 0, newStars = Math.max(previous, raid.stars);
   525	    const starGain = Math.max(0, newStars - previous);
   526	    raid.lootGold = Math.round(village.loot[0] * raid.percent / 100); raid.lootMana = Math.round(village.loot[1] * raid.percent / 100);
   527	    state.gold += raid.lootGold; state.mana += raid.lootMana; state.progressStars[raid.index] = newStars; state.trophies += starGain * 70 + (starGain ? Math.floor(raid.percent / 20) : 0); state.crown = state.progressStars.every(n => n > 0);
   528	    raid.result = raid.stars ? 'VICTORY' : 'HOLD FAILED'; raid.over = true; state.shake = .22; state.flash = .18; emit(195, 320, raid.stars ? COLORS.gold : COLORS.danger, 24, 4); popup(raid.stars ? `${raid.stars} STAR CLEAR` : 'NO STARS', raid.stars ? COLORS.gold : COLORS.danger); sound(raid.stars ? 'win' : 'hit'); saveGame();
   529	  }
   530	  function deployPoint(point) {
   531	    const left = RAID_BOARD.x + 8, right = RAID_BOARD.x + RAID_BOARD.w - 8, top = RAID_BOARD.y + 8, bottom = RAID_BOARD.y + RAID_BOARD.h - 8;
   532	    const p = { x: clamp(point.x, left, right), y: clamp(point.y, top, bottom) };
   533	    const distances = [{ edge: 'top', d: Math.abs(p.y - top) }, { edge: 'right', d: Math.abs(p.x - right) }, { edge: 'bottom', d: Math.abs(p.y - bottom) }, { edge: 'left', d: Math.abs(p.x - left) }];
   534	    distances.sort((a, b) => a.d - b.d); const edge = distances[0].edge;
   535	    if (edge === 'top') p.y = top; else if (edge === 'right') p.x = right; else if (edge === 'bottom') p.y = bottom; else p.x = left;
   536	    return p;
   537	  }
   538	  function deploySelected(raid, point) {
   539	    const slot = raid.selected, troop = TROOPS[slot];
   540	    if (raid.over || !troop || raid.hand[slot] <= 0) { popup('NO TROOPS LEFT', COLORS.danger); return; }
   541	    if (raid.units.length >= MAX_UNITS) { popup('DEPLOY LIMIT', COLORS.danger); return; }
   542	    const edge = deployPoint(point), unit = { slot, x: edge.x + (Math.random() - .5) * 5, y: edge.y + (Math.random() - .5) * 5, hp: troop.hp, maxHp: troop.hp, cooldown: .1, dead: false };
   543	    raid.units.push(unit); raid.hand[slot] -= 1; raid.cursor = edge; emit(edge.x, edge.y, troop.color, 8, 3); sound('select');
   544	    if (raid.hand[slot] <= 0) { const next = raid.hand.findIndex(n => n > 0); if (next >= 0) raid.selected = next; }
   545	  }
   546	  function updateRaid(raid, dt) {
   547	    if (!raid || raid.over) return;
   548	    raid.elapsed += dt;
   549	    raid.buildings.forEach(building => {
   550	      if (!building.alive || (building.type !== 'cannon' && building.type !== 'archer')) return;
   551	      building.cooldown -= dt; if (building.cooldown > 0) return;
   552	      const target = raid.units.filter(unit => !unit.dead && dist(building.x, building.y, unit.x, unit.y) < 145).sort((a, b) => dist(building.x, building.y, a.x, a.y) - dist(building.x, building.y, b.x, b.y))[0];
   553	      if (!target) { building.cooldown = .25; return; }
   554	      target.hp -= raidDamage(building.type, building.level); building.cooldown = building.type === 'cannon' ? 1.35 : .82; emit(target.x, target.y, building.type === 'cannon' ? COLORS.orange : COLORS.aqua, 3, 2); state.shake = .04; sound('hit');
   555	      if (target.hp <= 0) { target.dead = true; emit(target.x, target.y, COLORS.danger, 10, 3); }
   556	    });
   557	    raid.units.forEach(unit => {
   558	      if (unit.dead) return;
   559	      const troop = TROOPS[unit.slot], target = nearestRaidTarget(unit, raid);
   560	      if (!target) return;
   561	      const targetX = target.x, targetY = target.y, d = dist(unit.x, unit.y, targetX, targetY), range = troop.range;
   562	      if (troop.role === 'healer') {
   563	        if (d <= range) { const healTarget = target; healTarget.hp = Math.min(healTarget.maxHp, healTarget.hp + 18 * dt); emit(unit.x, unit.y, troop.color, 1, 1); }
   564	        else { unit.x += (targetX - unit.x) / Math.max(d, 1) * troop.speed * dt; unit.y += (targetY - unit.y) / Math.max(d, 1) * troop.speed * dt; }
   565	        return;
   566	      }
   567	      if (d > range) { unit.x += (targetX - unit.x) / Math.max(d, 1) * troop.speed * dt; unit.y += (targetY - unit.y) / Math.max(d, 1) * troop.speed * dt; }
   568	      else { unit.cooldown -= dt; if (unit.cooldown <= 0) { const bonus = troop.role === 'breaker' && target.type === 'wall' ? 1.9 : 1; target.hp -= troop.damage * bonus; unit.cooldown = unit.slot === 1 ? .72 : .95; emit(target.x, target.y, troop.color, 3, 2); state.shake = .025; if (target.hp <= 0) { target.alive = false; target.hp = 0; const color = target.type === 'wall' ? COLORS.wall : COLORS.gold; emit(target.x, target.y, color, 16, 3); floatText('BREACHED', target.x, target.y - 16, color); sound('hit'); } } }
   569	    });
   570	    raid.units = raid.units.filter(unit => !unit.dead || Math.random() > dt * 2.5);
   571	    const destroyed = raid.buildings.filter(item => !item.alive).length; raid.percent = destroyed / raid.buildings.length * 100;
   572	    const active = raid.units.some(unit => !unit.dead), handEmpty = raid.hand.every(count => count <= 0);
   573	    if (raid.percent >= 99.9 || (handEmpty && !active) || raid.elapsed >= 90) finishRaid(raid);
   574	  }
   575	
   600	      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) if (inRect(point, 5 + i * 77, 582, 72, 108)) return `raidSlot${i}`;
   601	      if (inRect(point, RAID_BOARD.x, RAID_BOARD.y, RAID_BOARD.w, RAID_BOARD.h)) return 'raidBoard';
   602	      return null;
   603	    }
   604	    if (inRect(point, 278, 15, 52, 42)) return 'navBase';
   605	    if (inRect(point, 334, 15, 52, 42)) return state.screen === 'log' ? 'navLadder' : 'navLog';
   606	    if (state.screen === 'base') {
   607	      if (point.y >= 570 && point.y <= 695) return 'basePalette' + clamp(Math.floor((point.x - 5) / 77), 0, 4);
   608	      if (inRect(point, 18, 112, 354, 416)) return 'baseBoard';
   609	    } else if (state.screen === 'ladder') {
   610	      for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) if (inRect(point, 18, 113 + i * 45, 354, 40)) return `ladderRow${i}`;
   611	    }
   612	    return null;
   613	  }
   614	  function goToScreen(screen) {
   615	    cancelPending(); resetInput(); state.screen = screen; if (screen !== 'raid') state.raid = screen === 'base' || screen === 'ladder' || screen === 'log' ? state.raid : null; sound('select');
   616	  }
   617	  function handleControlDown(control, point) {
   618	    if (control === 'baseBoard') basePointerDown(point, control);
   619	    else if (control.startsWith('basePalette')) { const index = Number(control.slice(-1)); const types = ['gold', 'elixir', 'cannon', 'archer', 'wall']; state.selectedBuild = state.selectedBuild === types[index] ? null : types[index]; sound('select'); }
   620	    else if (control === 'raidBoard' && state.raid && !state.raid.over) state.raid.cursor = deployPoint(point);
   621	    else if (control.startsWith('raidSlot') && state.raid && !state.raid.over) { state.raid.selected = Number(control.slice(-1)); sound('select'); }
   622	  }
   623	  function handleControlMove(control, point) {
   624	    if (control === 'baseBoard') basePointerMove(point);
   625	    else if (control === 'raidBoard' && state.raid && !state.raid.over) state.raid.cursor = deployPoint(point);
   626	  }
   627	  function handleControlUp(control, point, cancelled) {
   628	    if (cancelled) { if (control === 'baseBoard') state.input.drag = null; return; }
   629	    if (control === 'baseBoard') basePointerUp(point);
   630	    else if (control === 'raidBoard' && state.raid && !state.raid.over) deploySelected(state.raid, state.raid.cursor);
   631	    else if (control === 'raidBack') goToScreen('ladder');
   632	    else if (control === 'raidReplay' && state.raid) startRaid(state.raid.index);
   633	    else if (control === 'raidLadder') goToScreen('ladder');
   634	    else if (control === 'navBase') goToScreen('base');
   635	    else if (control === 'navLog') goToScreen('log');
   636	    else if (control === 'navLadder') goToScreen('ladder');
   637	    else if (control.startsWith('ladderRow')) {
   638	      const index = Number(control.slice(10)), unlocked = index === 0 || state.progressStars[index - 1] > 0;
   639	      if (unlocked) startRaid(index); else popup('CLEAR THE PRIOR RIVAL', COLORS.danger);
   640	    }
   641	  }
   642	  function pointerDown(event) {
   643	    event.preventDefault(); if (!state.started || state.orientationPaused || state.hiddenPaused) return;
   644	    const point = pointFromEvent(event), control = controlAt(point); if (!control || state.input.controls.has(control)) return;
   645	    state.input.controls.set(control, event.pointerId); state.input.pointers.set(event.pointerId, { control, point });
   646	    try { canvas.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId); } catch (error) {}
   647	    handleControlDown(control, point);
   648	  }
   649	  function pointerMove(event) {
   650	    event.preventDefault(); const info = state.input.pointers.get(event.pointerId); if (!info || state.orientationPaused) return;
   651	    const point = pointFromEvent(event); info.point = point; handleControlMove(info.control, point);
   652	  }
   653	  function pointerUp(event, cancelled) {
   654	    event.preventDefault(); const info = state.input.pointers.get(event.pointerId); if (!info) return;
   655	    const point = pointFromEvent(event); state.input.pointers.delete(event.pointerId); if (state.input.controls.get(info.control) === event.pointerId) state.input.controls.delete(info.control);
   656	    try { canvas.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId); } catch (error) {}
   657	    handleControlUp(info.control, point, cancelled);
   658	  }
   659	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', pointerDown, { passive: false });
   660	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', pointerMove, { passive: false });
   661	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', event => pointerUp(event, false), { passive: false });
   662	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', event => pointerUp(event, true), { passive: false });
   663	  canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', event => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   664	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', event => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   665	
   666	  function moveRaidCursor(dx, dy) {
   667	    const raid = state.raid; if (!raid || raid.over) return;
   668	    raid.cursor = deployPoint({ x: raid.cursor.x + dx * 22, y: raid.cursor.y + dy * 22 });
   669	  }
   670	  function keyDown(event) {
   671	    if (!state.started || state.orientationPaused) return;
   672	    state.input.keys.add(event.key);
   673	    if (state.screen === 'raid' && state.raid && !state.raid.over) {
   674	      if (/^[1-5]$/.test(event.key)) { state.raid.selected = Number(event.key) - 1; sound('select'); event.preventDefault(); }
   675	      else if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') { moveRaidCursor(-1, 0); event.preventDefault(); }
   676	      else if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') { moveRaidCursor(1, 0); event.preventDefault(); }
   677	      else if (event.key === 'ArrowUp') { moveRaidCursor(0, -1); event.preventDefault(); }
   678	      else if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') { moveRaidCursor(0, 1); event.preventDefault(); }
   679	      else if (event.key === ' ' || event.key === 'Enter') { deploySelected(state.raid, state.raid.cursor); event.preventDefault(); }
   680	      else if (event.key === 'Escape') goToScreen('ladder');
   681	    } else if (event.key.toLowerCase() === 'b') { goToScreen('base'); event.preventDefault(); }
   682	    else if (event.key.toLowerCase() === 'l') { goToScreen('ladder'); event.preventDefault(); }
   683	    else if (event.key.toLowerCase() === 'd') { goToScreen('log'); event.preventDefault(); }
   684	    else if (event.key.toLowerCase() === 'n') { fullReset(); popup('NEW OUTPOST', COLORS.aqua); event.preventDefault(); }
   685	  }
   686	  function keyUp(event) { state.input.keys.delete(event.key); }
   687	  window.addEventListener('keydown', keyDown, { passive: false }); window.addEventListener('keyup', keyUp, { passive: false });
   688	  window.addEventListener('blur', resetInput); window.addEventListener('resize', resize); window.addEventListener('orientationchange', resize);
   689	  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { state.hiddenPaused = document.visibilityState !== 'visible'; if (state.hiddenPaused) { resetInput(); saveGame(); } });
   690	  window.addEventListener('beforeunload', saveGame);
   691	
   692	  function startGame() { unlockAudio(); state.started = true; audioOverlay.style.display = 'none'; state.lastSeen = Date.now(); saveGame(); popup('BUILD A STRONGHOLD', COLORS.aqua); }
   693	  startButton.addEventListener('click', startGame);
   694	
   695	  function update(dt) {
   696	    updateEffects(dt);
   697	    if (state.screen === 'raid') updateRaid(state.raid, dt);
   698	  }
   699	  function render() {
   700	    ctx.setTransform(view.dpr, 0, 0, view.dpr, 0, 0); ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.ink; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, view.cssW, view.cssH);
   701	    ctx.setTransform(view.dpr * view.scale, 0, 0, view.dpr * view.scale, view.ox * view.dpr, view.oy * view.dpr);
   702	    ctx.save(); if (state.shake > 0) ctx.translate((Math.random() - .5) * state.shake * 14, (Math.random() - .5) * state.shake * 14);
   703	    if (state.screen === 'base') drawBase(); else if (state.screen === 'ladder') drawLadder(); else if (state.screen === 'log') drawDefenseLog(); else drawRaid();
   704	    drawEffects(); ctx.restore();
   705	  }
   706	  let lastTime = performance.now();
   707	  function frame(time) {
   708	    const dt = clamp((time - lastTime) / 1000, 0, .05); lastTime = time;
   709	    if (state.started && !state.orientationPaused && !state.hiddenPaused) update(dt);
   710	    render(); requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   711	  }
   712	  resize(); requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   713	})();

Tap kits to build, tap structures to upgrade, drag to move; B/L/D switch base, ladder, log.
Tap an open ladder rival, then tap the raid rim to deploy; 1–5 select troops, arrows move, Space deploys.
Everything builds instantly from earned ✦ gold and ◆ mist; clear all 12 rivals to earn the ladder crown.
Raid outcomes, base layout, loot, trophies, and the between-session defense log persist on this device.

     6	FZ.lerp = function (a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; };

     9	FZ.rng = function (seed) {
    10	  var s = (seed >>> 0) || 0x9e3779b9;
    11	  return function () {
    12	    s ^= s << 13; s >>>= 0;
    13	    s ^= s >>> 17;

    24	  function blank() { return { stars: {}, best: 0, unlocked: 1, mute: false }; }

    26	  function num(v, lo, hi, dflt) {
    27	    if (typeof v !== 'number' || !isFinite(v)) return dflt;
    28	    v = Math.floor(v);
    29	    if (v !== v) return dflt;
    30	    return FZ.clamp(v, lo, hi);
    31	  }

     7	  FZ.bndPattern = function (bp, c, cols, rows) {
     8	    var m = (cols - 1) / 2, h = rows;
     9	    switch (bp) {
    10	      case 0: return Math.round(h * 0.45);                                  // flat
    11	      case 1: return Math.round(h * 0.25 + (c / (cols - 1)) * h * 0.45);    // ramp
    12	      case 2: return Math.round(h * 0.68 - Math.abs(c - m) * (h * 0.10));   // valley (fizz bowl)
    13	      case 3: return Math.round(h * 0.25 + Math.abs(c - m) * (h * 0.11));   // dome
    14	      case 4: return Math.round(h * 0.46 + Math.sin(c * 1.05) * h * 0.20);  // wave

    48	    return {
    49	      seed: 90001 + r * 7717,
    50	      colors: r < 2 ? 5 : 6,
    51	      moves: 20,
    52	      caps: 999,
    53	      seals: Math.min(4, 1 + ((r / 2) | 0)),
    54	      bp: r % 7,
    55	      wave: Math.min(3, 1 + ((r / 3) | 0)),
    56	      every: r < 3 ? 4 : (r < 8 ? 3 : 2),
    57	      endless: true
    58	    };
    59	  };
    60	
    61	  FZ.starsFor = function (cfg, movesLeft) {
    62	    var f = movesLeft / Math.max(1, cfg.moves);
    63	    if (f >= 0.35) return 3;
    64	    if (f >= 0.12) return 2;
    65	    return 1;
    66	  };
    67	
    68	})(window.FZ);
     1	/* Fizzlift - dual-gravity match board.
     2	   Air region (rows < bnd[c]) falls DOWN. Fizz region (rows >= bnd[c]) floats UP.
     3	   Pieces pack against the fizz surface from both sides; refill enters from the
     4	   top (air) and the bottom (fizz). Caps that reach the surface row pop free. */
     5	(function (FZ) {
     6	
     7	  var COLS = 7, ROWS = 9;
     8	  FZ.COLS = COLS; FZ.ROWS = ROWS;
     9	
    10	  var K = { NORM: 0, CAP: 1, SEAL: 2, BOMB: 3, SURGE: 4 };
    11	  FZ.K = K;
    12	
    13	  var nextId = 1;
    14	
    15	  function mkCell(col, k, r, c, ry) {
    16	    return { col: col, k: k, hp: (k === K.SEAL ? 2 : 0), r: r, c: c, rx: c, ry: (ry === undefined ? r : ry), id: nextId++, dying: 0, born: 1 };
    17	  }
    18	
    19	  function idx(r, c) { return r * COLS + c; }
    20	  FZ.idx = idx;
    21	
    22	  function matchable(cell) { return !!cell && cell.col >= 0 && !cell.dying; }
    23	  function swappable(cell) { return !!cell && (cell.k === K.NORM || cell.k === K.BOMB || cell.k === K.SURGE); }
    24	  FZ.swappable = swappable;
    25	
    26	  /* ---------- boundaries ---------- */
    27	  function recomputeBnd(b) {
    28	    for (var c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
    29	      var w = b.wave ? Math.round(b.wave * Math.sin(b.phase + c * 0.85)) : 0;
    30	      b.bnd[c] = FZ.clamp(b.base[c] + b.valve[c] + w, 1, ROWS - 1);
    31	    }
    32	  }
    33	  FZ.recomputeBnd = recomputeBnd;
    34	
    35	  /* ---------- construction ---------- */
    36	  FZ.makeBoard = function (cfg) {
    37	    var b = {
    38	      cfg: cfg,
    39	      colors: FZ.clamp(cfg.colors || 5, 3, 6),
    40	      g: new Array(COLS * ROWS),
    41	      base: new Int8Array(COLS),
    42	      valve: new Int8Array(COLS),
    43	      bnd: new Int8Array(COLS),
    44	      wave: cfg.wave || 0,
    45	      phase: 0,
    46	      rnd: FZ.rng(cfg.seed),
    47	      capsOut: 0,       // caps surfaced
    48	      capsLive: 0,      // caps on the board
    49	      sealsLeft: 0,
    50	      sealsTotal: 0,
    51	      score: 0,
    52	      spawnCaps: true
    53	    };
    54	    for (var c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
    55	      b.base[c] = FZ.clamp(FZ.bndPattern(cfg.bp, c, COLS, ROWS), 1, ROWS - 1);
    56	      b.valve[c] = 0;
    57	    }
    58	    recomputeBnd(b);
    59	
    60	    /* fill with no starting matches */
    61	    for (var r = 0; r < ROWS; r++) {
    62	      for (c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
    63	        var tries = 0, col;
    64	        do {
    65	          col = (b.rnd() * b.colors) | 0;
    66	          tries++;
    67	        } while (tries < 24 && makesRun(b, r, c, col));
    68	        b.g[idx(r, c)] = mkCell(col, K.NORM, r, c);
    69	      }
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    /* seals: scattered, never on the same cell, biased into the fizz half */
    73	    var want = FZ.clamp(cfg.seals | 0, 0, 8), placed = 0, guard = 0;
    74	    while (placed < want && guard++ < 400) {
    75	      var sc = (b.rnd() * COLS) | 0;
    76	      var lo = b.bnd[sc], hi = ROWS - 1;
    77	      var sr = lo + ((b.rnd() * Math.max(1, hi - lo + 1)) | 0);
    78	      sr = FZ.clamp(sr, 0, ROWS - 1);
    79	      var cell = b.g[idx(sr, sc)];
    80	      if (!cell || cell.k !== K.NORM) continue;
    81	      if (neighbourKind(b, sr, sc, K.SEAL)) continue;
    82	      b.g[idx(sr, sc)] = mkCell(-1, K.SEAL, sr, sc);
    83	      placed++;
    84	    }
    85	    b.sealsLeft = placed; b.sealsTotal = placed;
    86	
    87	    /* a couple of starter caps low in the fizz */
    88	    var caps = Math.min(2, cfg.caps | 0), cg = 0;
    89	    while (caps > 0 && cg++ < 200) {
    90	      var cc = (b.rnd() * COLS) | 0;
    91	      var cr = ROWS - 1 - ((b.rnd() * 2) | 0);
    92	      if (cr < b.bnd[cc]) continue;
    93	      var t = b.g[idx(cr, cc)];
    94	      if (!t || t.k !== K.NORM) continue;
    95	      b.g[idx(cr, cc)] = mkCell(-1, K.CAP, cr, cc);
    96	      b.capsLive++; caps--;
    97	    }
    98	
    99	    clearStartingRuns(b);
   100	    return b;
   101	  };
   102	
   103	  function neighbourKind(b, r, c, k) {
   104	    var d = [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1]];
   105	    for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
   106	      var rr = r + d[i][0], cc = c + d[i][1];
   107	      if (rr < 0 || rr >= ROWS || cc < 0 || cc >= COLS) continue;
   108	      var t = b.g[idx(rr, cc)];
   109	      if (t && t.k === k) return true;
   110	    }
   111	    return false;
   112	  }
   113	
   114	  function makesRun(b, r, c, col) {
   115	    var a = c >= 2 && b.g[idx(r, c - 1)] && b.g[idx(r, c - 2)] &&
   116	      b.g[idx(r, c - 1)].col === col && b.g[idx(r, c - 2)].col === col;
   117	    var v = r >= 2 && b.g[idx(r - 1, c)] && b.g[idx(r - 2, c)] &&
   118	      b.g[idx(r - 1, c)].col === col && b.g[idx(r - 2, c)].col === col;
   119	    return a || v;
   120	  }
   121	
   122	  function clearStartingRuns(b) {
   123	    for (var pass = 0; pass < 12; pass++) {
   124	      var runs = FZ.findRuns(b);
   125	      if (!runs.length) return;
   125	      if (!runs.length) return;
   126	      for (var i = 0; i < runs.length; i++) {
   127	        var run = runs[i];
   128	        var t = b.g[run[(run.length / 2) | 0]];
   129	        if (t && t.k === K.NORM) {
   130	          var col = t.col;
   131	          for (var g = 0; g < 8; g++) { var n = (b.rnd() * b.colors) | 0; if (n !== col) { t.col = n; break; } }
   132	        }
   133	      }
   134	    }
   135	  }
   136	
   137	  /* ---------- matching ---------- */
   138	  FZ.findRuns = function (b) {
   139	    var runs = [], r, c, i;
   140	    for (r = 0; r < ROWS; r++) {
   141	      c = 0;
   142	      while (c < COLS) {
   143	        var cell = b.g[idx(r, c)];
   144	        if (!matchable(cell)) { c++; continue; }
   145	        var e = c + 1;
   146	        while (e < COLS) { var n = b.g[idx(r, e)]; if (matchable(n) && n.col === cell.col) e++; else break; }
   147	        if (e - c >= 3) { var run = []; for (i = c; i < e; i++) run.push(idx(r, i)); run.horiz = true; runs.push(run); }
   148	        c = e;
   149	      }
   150	    }
   151	    for (c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
   152	      r = 0;
   153	      while (r < ROWS) {
   154	        var cl = b.g[idx(r, c)];
   155	        if (!matchable(cl)) { r++; continue; }
   156	        var e2 = r + 1;
   157	        while (e2 < ROWS) { var n2 = b.g[idx(e2, c)]; if (matchable(n2) && n2.col === cl.col) e2++; else break; }
   158	        if (e2 - r >= 3) { var run2 = []; for (i = r; i < e2; i++) run2.push(idx(i, c)); run2.horiz = false; runs.push(run2); }
   159	        r = e2;
   160	      }
   161	    }
   162	    return runs;
   163	  };
   164	
   165	  FZ.hasMove = function (b) {
   166	    for (var r = 0; r < ROWS; r++) {
   167	      for (var c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
   168	        if (c + 1 < COLS && testSwap(b, r, c, r, c + 1)) return true;
   169	        if (r + 1 < ROWS && testSwap(b, r, c, r + 1, c)) return true;
   170	      }
   171	    }
   172	    return false;
   173	  };
   174	
   175	  function testSwap(b, r1, c1, r2, c2) {
   176	    var a = b.g[idx(r1, c1)], d = b.g[idx(r2, c2)];
   177	    if (!swappable(a) || !swappable(d)) return false;
   178	    b.g[idx(r1, c1)] = d; b.g[idx(r2, c2)] = a;
   179	    var ok = FZ.findRuns(b).length > 0;
   180	    b.g[idx(r1, c1)] = a; b.g[idx(r2, c2)] = d;
   181	    return ok;
   182	  }
   183	  FZ.testSwap = testSwap;
   184	
   185	  FZ.doSwap = function (b, r1, c1, r2, c2) {
   186	    var a = b.g[idx(r1, c1)], d = b.g[idx(r2, c2)];
   187	    b.g[idx(r1, c1)] = d; b.g[idx(r2, c2)] = a;
   188	    if (a) { a.r = r2; a.c = c2; }
   189	    if (d) { d.r = r1; d.c = c1; }
   190	  };
   191	
   192	  FZ.shuffle = function (b) {
   193	    var pool = [], i, cell;
   194	    for (i = 0; i < b.g.length; i++) { cell = b.g[i]; if (cell && cell.k === K.NORM) pool.push(cell); }
   195	    for (var pass = 0; pass < 30; pass++) {
   196	      for (i = pool.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
   197	        var j = (b.rnd() * (i + 1)) | 0;
   198	        var t = pool[i].col; pool[i].col = pool[j].col; pool[j].col = t;
   199	      }
   200	      if (FZ.findRuns(b).length === 0 && FZ.hasMove(b)) return true;
   201	    }
   202	    return false;
   203	  };
   204	
   205	  /* ---------- gravity ---------- */
   206	  /* Returns true if any cell changed row/col or was spawned. */
   207	  FZ.settle = function (b) {
   208	    var moved = false;
   209	    for (var c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
   210	      var bn = b.bnd[c], r, i, cell, list;
   211	
   212	      /* AIR: rows 0..bn-1, pack to the bottom of the region */
   213	      list = [];
   214	      for (r = 0; r < bn; r++) { cell = b.g[idx(r, c)]; if (cell) { list.push(cell); b.g[idx(r, c)] = null; } }
   215	      var start = bn - list.length;
   216	      for (i = 0; i < list.length; i++) moved = place(b, start + i, c, list[i]) || moved;
   217	      for (r = 0; r < start; r++) {
   218	        var nc = spawn(b, c, false, start - r);
   219	        place(b, r, c, nc); moved = true;
   220	      }
   221	
   222	      /* FIZZ: rows bn..ROWS-1, pack to the top of the region (the surface) */
   223	      list = [];
   224	      for (r = bn; r < ROWS; r++) { cell = b.g[idx(r, c)]; if (cell) { list.push(cell); b.g[idx(r, c)] = null; } }
   225	      for (i = 0; i < list.length; i++) moved = place(b, bn + i, c, list[i]) || moved;
   226	      for (r = bn + list.length; r < ROWS; r++) {
   227	        var nc2 = spawn(b, c, true, r - (bn + list.length) + 1);
   228	        place(b, r, c, nc2); moved = true;
   229	      }
   230	    }
   231	    return moved;
   232	  };
   233	
   234	  function place(b, r, c, cell) {
   235	    var changed = (cell.r !== r || cell.c !== c);
   236	    cell.r = r; cell.c = c;
   237	    b.g[idx(r, c)] = cell;
   238	    return changed;
   239	  }
   240	
   240	
   241	  function spawn(b, c, fromFizz, dist) {
   242	    var wantCap = false;
   243	    if (fromFizz && b.spawnCaps) {
   244	      var need = (b.cfg.caps | 0) - b.capsOut - b.capsLive;
   245	      if (need > 0 && b.rnd() < (b.cfg.endless ? 0.20 : 0.24)) wantCap = true;
   246	    }
   247	    var cell;
   248	    if (wantCap) {
   249	      cell = mkCell(-1, K.CAP, 0, c);
   250	      b.capsLive++;
   251	    } else {
   252	      cell = mkCell((b.rnd() * b.colors) | 0, K.NORM, 0, c);
   253	    }
   254	    cell.ry = fromFizz ? (ROWS - 1 + dist) : (-dist);
   255	    cell.rx = c;
   256	    return cell;
   257	  }
   258	
   259	  /* ---------- clearing ---------- */
   260	  /* Marks cells for destruction. Returns {list, specials, chainSeals} or null. */
   261	  FZ.planClear = function (b, swapIdx) {
   262	    var runs = FZ.findRuns(b);
   263	    if (!runs.length) return null;
   264	    var mark = new Uint8Array(COLS * ROWS);
   265	    var specials = [];
   266	    var i, j;
   267	
   268	    for (i = 0; i < runs.length; i++) {
   269	      var run = runs[i];
   270	      for (j = 0; j < run.length; j++) mark[run[j]] = 1;
   271	      if (run.length >= 4) {
   272	        var at = -1;
   273	        if (swapIdx >= 0 && run.indexOf(swapIdx) >= 0) at = swapIdx;
   274	        else at = run[(run.length / 2) | 0];
   275	        var cellAt = b.g[at];
   276	        specials.push({ at: at, kind: run.length >= 5 ? K.SURGE : K.BOMB, col: cellAt ? cellAt.col : 0 });
   277	      }
   278	    }
   279	
   280	    /* chain-detonate specials caught in the blast */
   281	    var queue = [];
   282	    for (i = 0; i < mark.length; i++) if (mark[i]) queue.push(i);
   283	    var guard = 0;
   284	    while (queue.length && guard++ < 400) {
   285	      var q = queue.pop();
   286	      var cell = b.g[q];
   287	      if (!cell) continue;
   288	      if (cell.k === K.BOMB && !cell.detonated) {
   289	        cell.detonated = true;
   290	        var r0 = (q / COLS) | 0, c0 = q % COLS;
   291	        for (var dr = -1; dr <= 1; dr++) for (var dc = -1; dc <= 1; dc++) {
   292	          var rr = r0 + dr, cc = c0 + dc;
   293	          if (rr < 0 || rr >= ROWS || cc < 0 || cc >= COLS) continue;
   294	          var k2 = idx(rr, cc);
   295	          if (!mark[k2]) { mark[k2] = 1; queue.push(k2); }
   296	        }
   297	      } else if (cell.k === K.SURGE && !cell.detonated) {
   298	        cell.detonated = true;
   299	        var cx = q % COLS;
   300	        for (var rr2 = 0; rr2 < ROWS; rr2++) {
   301	          var k3 = idx(rr2, cx);
   302	          if (!mark[k3]) { mark[k3] = 1; queue.push(k3); }
   303	        }
   304	      }
   305	    }
   306	
   307	    /* caps and seals are never destroyed by being marked */
   308	    var list = [], seals = [];
   309	    var seen = {};
   310	    for (i = 0; i < mark.length; i++) {
   311	      if (!mark[i]) continue;
   312	      var cl = b.g[i];
   313	      if (!cl) continue;
   314	      if (cl.k === K.CAP || cl.k === K.SEAL) { mark[i] = 0; continue; }
   315	      list.push(i);
   316	    }
   317	    /* seal damage from adjacency */
   318	    for (i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
   319	      var p = list[i], pr = (p / COLS) | 0, pc = p % COLS;
   320	      var d = [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1]];
   321	      for (j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
   322	        var nr = pr + d[j][0], ncc = pc + d[j][1];
   323	        if (nr < 0 || nr >= ROWS || ncc < 0 || ncc >= COLS) continue;
   324	        var ki = idx(nr, ncc);
   325	        var sc = b.g[ki];
   326	        if (sc && sc.k === K.SEAL && !seen[ki]) { seen[ki] = 1; seals.push(ki); }
   327	      }
   328	    }
   329	    if (!list.length) return null;
   330	    return { list: list, specials: specials, seals: seals };
   331	  };
   332	
   333	  /* Applies a plan. fx = callback(type, r, c, colour) for juice. */
   334	  FZ.applyClear = function (b, plan, chain, fx) {
   335	    var i, cleared = 0;
   336	    var specialAt = {};
   337	    for (i = 0; i < plan.specials.length; i++) specialAt[plan.specials[i].at] = plan.specials[i];
   338	
   339	    for (i = 0; i < plan.list.length; i++) {
   340	      var p = plan.list[i], cell = b.g[p];
   341	      if (!cell) continue;
   342	      var r = (p / COLS) | 0, c = p % COLS;
   343	      var sp = specialAt[p];
   344	      if (sp) {
   345	        b.g[p] = mkCell(sp.col, sp.kind, r, c);
   346	        fx('special', r, c, sp.col);
   347	      } else {
   348	        b.g[p] = null;
   349	        cleared++;
   350	        fx('clear', r, c, cell.col);
   351	      }
   352	    }
   353	    /* seal damage */
   354	    for (i = 0; i < plan.seals.length; i++) {
   355	      var si = plan.seals[i], sc = b.g[si];
   356	      if (!sc || sc.k !== K.SEAL) continue;
   357	      sc.hp--;
   358	      var sr = (si / COLS) | 0, scc = si % COLS;
   359	      if (sc.hp <= 0) {
   360	        b.g[si] = null;
   361	        b.sealsLeft = Math.max(0, b.sealsLeft - 1);
   362	        fx('valve', sr, scc, -1);
   363	        openValve(b, scc);
   364	      } else {
   365	        fx('crack', sr, scc, -1);
   366	      }
   367	    }
   368	    b.score += cleared * 12 * (1 + chain);
   369	    return cleared;
   370	  };
   371	
   372	  /* A broken seal opens a fizz valve: the fizz line rises permanently nearby. */
   373	  function openValve(b, c) {
   374	    for (var d = -1; d <= 1; d++) {
   375	      var cc = c + d;
   376	      if (cc < 0 || cc >= COLS) continue;
   377	      b.valve[cc] = FZ.clamp(b.valve[cc] - (d === 0 ? 2 : 1), -3, 3);
   378	    }
   379	    recomputeBnd(b);
   380	  }
   381	
   382	  /* Caps sitting exactly on the fizz surface break free. */
   383	  FZ.collectCaps = function (b, fx) {
   384	    var n = 0;
   385	    for (var c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
   386	      var r = b.bnd[c];
   387	      n += pop(b, r, c, fx);
   388	      if (r - 1 >= 0) n += pop(b, r - 1, c, fx);
   389	    }
   390	    return n;
   391	  };
   392	
   393	  function pop(b, r, c, fx) {
   394	    var cell = b.g[idx(r, c)];
   395	    if (!cell || cell.k !== K.CAP) return 0;
   396	    b.g[idx(r, c)] = null;
   397	    b.capsLive = Math.max(0, b.capsLive - 1);
   398	    b.capsOut++;
   399	    b.score += 150;
   400	    fx('cap', r, c, -1);
   401	    return 1;
   402	  }
   403	
   404	  FZ.advanceWave = function (b) {
   405	    if (!b.wave) return false;
   406	    b.phase += 0.9;
   407	    if (b.phase > 1e6) b.phase = b.phase % (Math.PI * 2);
   408	    var old = [];
   409	    for (var c = 0; c < COLS; c++) old.push(b.bnd[c]);
   410	    recomputeBnd(b);
   411	    for (c = 0; c < COLS; c++) if (old[c] !== b.bnd[c]) return true;
   412	    return false;
   413	  };
   414	
   415	})(window.FZ);

    21	  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
    22	
    23	  /* ---------------- input state (hardening #2/#3) ---------------- */
    24	  var input = { ptrId: -1, sx: 0, sy: 0, r: -1, c: -1, dragged: false, keys: Object.create(null) };
    25	
    26	  function resetInput() {
    27	    input.ptrId = -1; input.sx = 0; input.sy = 0; input.r = -1; input.c = -1; input.dragged = false;
    28	    input.keys = Object.create(null);
    29	    if (st) { st.sel = null; st.pendingDir = 0; }
    30	  }
    31	
    32	  function clearTimers() {
    33	    for (var i = 0; i < timers.length; i++) { try { clearTimeout(timers[i]); } catch (e) { } }
    34	    timers.length = 0;
    35	  }
    36	
    37	  /* ---------------- fx ---------------- */
    38	  function addPart(x, y, vx, vy, col, size, life) {
    39	    if (parts.length >= MAXP) parts.shift();
    40	    parts.push({ x: x, y: y, vx: vx, vy: vy, col: col, s: size, l: life, m: life });
    41	  }
    42	  function addText(x, y, txt, col) {
    43	    if (texts.length >= MAXT) texts.shift();
    44	    texts.push({ x: x, y: y, t: txt, l: 0.9, col: col || '#fff' });
    45	  }
    46	  function shake(v) { if (st) st.shake = Math.min(16, st.shake + v); }
    47	
    48	  function fxAt(type, r, c, col) {
    49	    if (!st) return;
    50	    var g = geom(), x = g.x + (c + 0.5) * g.t, y = g.y + (r + 0.5) * g.t;
    51	    var i, a;
    52	    if (type === 'clear') {
    53	      for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    54	        a = Math.random() * 6.283;
    55	        addPart(x, y, Math.cos(a) * 90, Math.sin(a) * 90 - 30, COLORS[col % 6] || '#fff', 3 + Math.random() * 3, 0.45);
    56	      }
    57	    } else if (type === 'special') {
    58	      for (i = 0; i < 10; i++) { a = Math.random() * 6.283; addPart(x, y, Math.cos(a) * 150, Math.sin(a) * 150, '#fff', 3, 0.4); }
    59	      shake(4);
    60	    } else if (type === 'cap') {
    61	      for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) { a = Math.random() * 6.283; addPart(x, y, Math.cos(a) * 170, Math.sin(a) * 170 - 90, '#ffe9a8', 3 + Math.random() * 4, 0.7); }
    62	      addText(x, y, 'CAP!', '#ffe9a8'); shake(6); st.flash = Math.max(st.flash, 0.35);
    63	    } else if (type === 'crack') {
    64	      for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) { a = Math.random() * 6.283; addPart(x, y, Math.cos(a) * 120, Math.sin(a) * 120, '#cfd6de', 3, 0.4); }
    65	      shake(3);
    66	    } else if (type === 'valve') {
    67	      for (i = 0; i < 24; i++) { a = Math.random() * 6.283; addPart(x, y, Math.cos(a) * 210, Math.sin(a) * 210, '#9ee44a', 3 + Math.random() * 4, 0.8); }
    68	      addText(x, y, 'VALVE OPEN', '#9ee44a'); shake(9); st.flash = Math.max(st.flash, 0.5);
    69	    }
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  /* ---------------- geometry ---------------- */
    73	  function geom() {
    74	    var pad = 8;
    75	    var t = Math.min((W - pad * 2) / COLS, (H - pad * 2) / ROWS);
    76	    var bw = t * COLS, bh = t * ROWS;
    77	    return { t: t, x: (W - bw) / 2, y: (H - bh) / 2, w: bw, h: bh };
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  /* ---------------- level lifecycle ---------------- */
    81	  function startLevel(n, endless) {
    82	    clearTimers();
    83	    resetInput();
    84	    parts.length = 0; bubbles.length = 0; texts.length = 0;
    85	    var cfg = endless ? FZ.endlessCfg(0) : FZ.LEVELS[FZ.clamp(n - 1, 0, FZ.LEVELS.length - 1)];
    86	    var b = FZ.makeBoard(cfg);
    87	    FZ.settle(b);
    88	    var noop = function () { };
    89	    for (var q = 0; q < 24; q++) {
    90	      var pl = FZ.planClear(b, -1);
    91	      if (!pl) break;
    92	      FZ.applyClear(b, pl, 0, noop);
    93	      FZ.settle(b);
    94	    }
    95	    if (!FZ.hasMove(b)) FZ.shuffle(b);
    96	    b.score = 0;
    97	    for (var i = 0; i < b.g.length; i++) if (b.g[i]) { b.g[i].ry = b.g[i].r; b.g[i].rx = b.g[i].c; b.g[i].dying = 0; }
    98	    st = {
    99	      level: n, endless: !!endless, cfg: cfg, b: b,
   100	      moves: cfg.moves, phase: 'idle', timer: 0, chain: 0,
   101	      sel: null, cur: { r: (ROWS / 2) | 0, c: (COLS / 2) | 0 },
   102	      plan: null, swapValid: false, swapA: null, swapB: null,
   103	      shake: 0, flash: 0, over: 0, turnShifted: false, capsThisRun: 0,
   104	      hintT: 0, denyT: 0
   105	    };
   106	    hideOverlays();
   107	    syncHUD();
   108	  }
   109	
   110	  function restart() {
   111	    if (!st) return;
   112	    startLevel(st.level, st.endless);
   113	  }
   114	
   115	  /* ---------------- flow ---------------- */
   116	  function setPhase(p, t) { st.phase = p; st.timer = t || 0; }
   117	
   118	  function startClear() {
   119	    var plan = FZ.planClear(st.b, st.swapIdx === undefined ? -1 : st.swapIdx);
   120	    if (!plan) return false;
   121	    st.swapIdx = -1;
   122	    st.plan = plan;
   123	    for (var i = 0; i < plan.list.length; i++) { var cl = st.b.g[plan.list[i]]; if (cl) cl.dying = 1; }
   124	    FZ.audio.match(st.chain);
   125	    if (st.chain > 0) addTextChain(st.chain);
   126	    shake(2 + st.chain);
   127	    setPhase('clear', 0.17);
   128	    return true;
   129	  }
   130	
   131	  function addTextChain(ch) {
   132	    var g = geom();
   133	    addText(g.x + g.w * 0.5, g.y + g.h * 0.35, 'CASCADE x' + (ch + 1), '#fff');
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  function endTurn() {
   137	    if (!st.turnShifted) {
   138	      st.turnShifted = true;
   139	      var used = st.cfg.moves - st.moves;
   140	      if (st.endless) used = st.movesUsedEndless = (st.movesUsedEndless || 0) + 1;
   141	      if (st.cfg.every > 0 && used > 0 && used % st.cfg.every === 0) {
   142	        if (FZ.advanceWave(st.b)) {
   143	          FZ.audio.fizz(); shake(6);
   144	          addText(geom().x + geom().w * 0.5, geom().y + geom().h * 0.12, 'FIZZ SHIFT', '#3ad1c6');
   145	        }
   146	      }
   147	      if (FZ.settle(st.b)) { setPhase('end', 0.5); return; }
   148	    }
   149	    finishTurn();
   150	  }
   151	
   152	  function finishTurn() {
   153	    var b = st.b;
   154	    if (!st.endless && b.capsOut >= st.cfg.caps && b.sealsLeft === 0) { win(); return; }
   155	    if (st.moves <= 0) { lose(); return; }
   156	    if (!FZ.hasMove(b)) {
   157	      FZ.shuffle(b);
   158	      addText(geom().x + geom().w * 0.5, geom().y + geom().h * 0.5, 'NO MOVES - RESTIRRED', '#fff');
   159	      shake(6);
   160	    }
   161	    setPhase('idle', 0);
   162	    syncHUD();
   163	  }
   164	
   165	  function step() {
   166	    var b = st.b;
   167	    switch (st.phase) {
   168	      case 'swap':
   169	        if (st.swapValid) {
   170	          st.moves--; st.chain = 0; st.turnShifted = false;
   171	          syncHUD();
   172	          if (!startClear()) endTurn();
   173	        } else {
   174	          FZ.doSwap(b, st.swapA.r, st.swapA.c, st.swapB.r, st.swapB.c);
   175	          setPhase('revert', 0.13);
   176	        }
   177	        break;
   178	      case 'revert':
   179	        setPhase('idle', 0);
   180	        break;
   181	      case 'clear':
   182	        FZ.applyClear(b, st.plan, st.chain, fxAt);
   183	        st.plan = null;
   184	        FZ.settle(b);
   185	        syncHUD();
   186	        setPhase('fall', 0.55);
   187	        break;
   188	      case 'fall': {
   189	        var n = FZ.collectCaps(b, fxAt);
   190	        if (n > 0) {
   191	          FZ.audio.pop();
   192	          st.capsThisRun += n;
   193	          if (st.endless) {
   194	            st.moves += 2 * n;
   195	            if (st.capsThisRun % 10 === 0) { b.wave = Math.min(3, b.wave + 1); FZ.recomputeBnd(b); }
   196	          }
   197	          syncHUD();
   198	          setPhase('cap', 0.2);
   199	        } else {
   200	          st.chain++;
   201	          if (!startClear()) endTurn();
   202	        }
   203	        break;
   204	      }
   205	      case 'cap':
   206	        FZ.settle(b);
   207	        setPhase('fall', 0.55);
   208	        break;
   209	      case 'end': {
   210	        var n2 = FZ.collectCaps(b, fxAt);
   211	        if (n2 > 0) {
   212	          FZ.audio.pop(); st.capsThisRun += n2;
   213	          if (st.endless) st.moves += 2 * n2;
   214	          syncHUD();
   215	          setPhase('cap', 0.2);
   216	        } else if (!startClear()) finishTurn();
   217	        break;
   218	      }
   219	    }
   220	  }
   221	
   222	  function win() {
   223	    st.over = 1;
   224	    syncHUD();
   225	    FZ.audio.win();
   226	    var stars = FZ.starsFor(st.cfg, st.moves);
   227	    var key = String(st.level);
   228	    if (!save.stars[key] || save.stars[key] < stars) save.stars[key] = stars;
   229	    if (st.level + 1 > save.unlocked) save.unlocked = Math.min(FZ.LEVELS.length, st.level + 1);
   230	    if (st.b.score > save.best) save.best = st.b.score;
   231	    FZ.store.save(save);
   232	    el.endTitle.textContent = 'GLASS CLEARED';
   233	    el.endBody.innerHTML = starRow(stars) + '<div class="big">' + st.b.score + '</div>' +
   234	      '<div class="sub">Level ' + st.level + ' &middot; ' + st.moves + ' moves left &middot; best ' + save.best + '</div>';
   235	    el.endNext.style.display = (st.level < FZ.LEVELS.length) ? '' : 'none';
   236	    show(el.endOv);
   237	    setPhase('idle', 0);
   238	  }
   239	
   240	  function lose() {
   240	  function lose() {
   241	    st.over = 2;
   242	    syncHUD();
   243	    FZ.audio.lose();
   244	    if (st.b.score > save.best) { save.best = st.b.score; FZ.store.save(save); }
   245	    el.endTitle.textContent = st.endless ? 'FLAT' : 'OUT OF MOVES';
   246	    el.endBody.innerHTML = '<div class="big">' + st.b.score + '</div><div class="sub">' +
   247	      (st.endless ? st.capsThisRun + ' caps floated' : (st.b.capsOut + '/' + st.cfg.caps + ' caps &middot; ' + st.b.sealsLeft + ' seals left')) +
   248	      ' &middot; best ' + save.best + '</div>';
   249	    el.endNext.style.display = 'none';
   250	    show(el.endOv);
   251	    setPhase('idle', 0);
   252	  }
   253	
   254	  function starRow(n) {
   255	    var s = '<div class="stars">';
   256	    for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) s += '<span class="' + (i < n ? 'on' : '') + '">&#9733;</span>';
   257	    return s + '</div>';
   258	  }
   259	
   260	  /* ---------------- interaction ---------------- */
   261	  function canPlay() { return st && !paused && !rotate && st.over === 0 && st.phase === 'idle'; }
   262	
   263	  function attemptSwap(r1, c1, r2, c2) {
   264	    if (!canPlay()) return;
   265	    if (r2 < 0 || r2 >= ROWS || c2 < 0 || c2 >= COLS) return;
   266	    var b = st.b;
   267	    var a = b.g[idx(r1, c1)], d = b.g[idx(r2, c2)];
   268	    if (!FZ.swappable(a) || !FZ.swappable(d)) { FZ.audio.deny(); st.denyT = 0.25; shake(3); st.sel = null; return; }
   269	    st.swapValid = FZ.testSwap(b, r1, c1, r2, c2);
   270	    st.swapA = { r: r1, c: c1 }; st.swapB = { r: r2, c: c2 };
   271	    FZ.doSwap(b, r1, c1, r2, c2);
   272	    st.swapIdx = idx(r2, c2);
   273	    st.sel = null;
   274	    FZ.audio.blip();
   275	    if (!st.swapValid) { st.denyT = 0.25; shake(2); }
   276	    setPhase('swap', 0.14);
   277	  }
   278	
   279	  function cellAt(px, py) {
   280	    var g = geom();
   281	    var c = Math.floor((px - g.x) / g.t), r = Math.floor((py - g.y) / g.t);
   282	    if (r < 0 || r >= ROWS || c < 0 || c >= COLS) return null;
   283	    return { r: r, c: c };
   284	  }
   285	
   286	  function localPos(e) {
   287	    var rect = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
   288	    return { x: (e.clientX - rect.left) * (W / Math.max(1, rect.width)), y: (e.clientY - rect.top) * (H / Math.max(1, rect.height)) };
   289	  }
   290	
   291	  function onDown(e) {
   292	    FZ.audio.unlock();
   293	    e.preventDefault();
   294	    if (!canPlay()) return;
   295	    if (input.ptrId !== -1) return;            /* one pointer owns the board */
   296	    var p = localPos(e), cell = cellAt(p.x, p.y);
   297	    if (!cell) return;
   298	    input.ptrId = (e.pointerId === undefined ? 1 : e.pointerId);
   299	    input.sx = p.x; input.sy = p.y; input.r = cell.r; input.c = cell.c; input.dragged = false;
   300	    st.cur = { r: cell.r, c: cell.c };
   301	    try { cv.setPointerCapture && e.pointerId !== undefined && cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) { }
   302	  }
   303	
   304	  function onMove(e) {
   305	    if (input.ptrId === -1) return;
   306	    var pid = (e.pointerId === undefined ? 1 : e.pointerId);
   307	    if (pid !== input.ptrId) return;
   308	    e.preventDefault();
   309	    if (input.dragged || !canPlay()) return;
   310	    var p = localPos(e);
   311	    var dx = p.x - input.sx, dy = p.y - input.sy;
   312	    var g = geom(), th = g.t * 0.38;
   313	    if (Math.abs(dx) < th && Math.abs(dy) < th) return;
   314	    input.dragged = true;
   315	    var r2 = input.r, c2 = input.c;
   316	    if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) c2 += dx > 0 ? 1 : -1; else r2 += dy > 0 ? 1 : -1;
   317	    attemptSwap(input.r, input.c, r2, c2);
   318	    releasePtr(e);
   319	  }
   320	
   321	  function onUp(e) {
   322	    var pid = (e.pointerId === undefined ? 1 : e.pointerId);
   323	    if (input.ptrId === -1 || pid !== input.ptrId) return;
   324	    e.preventDefault();
   325	    if (!input.dragged && canPlay()) {
   326	      var r = input.r, c = input.c;
   327	      if (st.sel && st.sel.r === r && st.sel.c === c) st.sel = null;
   328	      else if (st.sel && Math.abs(st.sel.r - r) + Math.abs(st.sel.c - c) === 1) attemptSwap(st.sel.r, st.sel.c, r, c);
   329	      else {
   330	        var cell = st.b.g[idx(r, c)];
   331	        st.sel = FZ.swappable(cell) ? { r: r, c: c } : null;
   332	        if (st.sel) FZ.audio.blip(); else { FZ.audio.deny(); st.denyT = 0.2; }
   333	      }
   334	    }
   335	    releasePtr(e);
   336	  }
   337	
   338	  function releasePtr(e) {
   339	    if (e && e.pointerId !== undefined) { try { cv.releasePointerCapture && cv.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) { } }
   340	    input.ptrId = -1; input.dragged = false; input.r = -1; input.c = -1;
   341	  }
   342	
   343	  function onCancel(e) {
   344	    var pid = (e && e.pointerId !== undefined) ? e.pointerId : input.ptrId;
   345	    if (pid !== input.ptrId) return;
   346	    releasePtr(e);
   347	  }
   348	
   349	  function onKey(e) {
   350	    var k = e.key;
   351	    if (k === undefined) return;
   352	    if (k === 'm' || k === 'M') { toggleMute(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
   353	    if (k === 'r' || k === 'R') { restart(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
   354	    if (k === 'Escape') { if (visible(el.lvOv)) { hideOverlays(); } else if (st) st.sel = null; e.preventDefault(); return; }
   355	    if (!canPlay()) {
   356	      if ((k === 'Enter' || k === ' ') && st && st.over) { e.preventDefault(); if (st.over === 1 && st.level < FZ.LEVELS.length && !st.endless) startLevel(st.level + 1, false); else restart(); }
   357	      return;
   358	    }
   359	    var d = null;
   360	    if (k === 'ArrowLeft') d = [0, -1]; else if (k === 'ArrowRight') d = [0, 1];
   361	    else if (k === 'ArrowUp') d = [-1, 0]; else if (k === 'ArrowDown') d = [1, 0];
   362	    if (d) {
   363	      e.preventDefault();
   364	      if (st.sel) attemptSwap(st.sel.r, st.sel.c, st.sel.r + d[0], st.sel.c + d[1]);
   365	      else { st.cur.r = FZ.clamp(st.cur.r + d[0], 0, ROWS - 1); st.cur.c = FZ.clamp(st.cur.c + d[1], 0, COLS - 1); }
   366	      return;
   367	    }
   368	    if (k === 'Enter' || k === ' ') {
   369	      e.preventDefault();
   370	      if (st.sel) { st.sel = null; }
   371	      else {
   372	        var cell = st.b.g[idx(st.cur.r, st.cur.c)];
   373	        if (FZ.swappable(cell)) { st.sel = { r: st.cur.r, c: st.cur.c }; FZ.audio.blip(); }
   374	        else { FZ.audio.deny(); st.denyT = 0.2; }
   375	      }
   376	    }
   377	  }
   378	
   379	  /* ---------------- overlays / HUD ---------------- */
   380	  function show(o) { o.classList.add('show'); }
   381	  function visible(o) { return o.classList.contains('show'); }
   382	  function hideOverlays() { el.endOv.classList.remove('show'); el.lvOv.classList.remove('show'); }
   383	
   384	  function syncHUD() {
   385	    if (!st) return;
   386	    var b = st.b;
   387	    el.lvName.textContent = st.endless ? 'ENDLESS FIZZ' : ('LEVEL ' + st.level + ' · ' + FZ.PATTERN_NAMES[st.cfg.bp % 7]);
   388	    el.moves.textContent = st.moves;
   389	    el.caps.textContent = st.endless ? String(b.capsOut) : (b.capsOut + '/' + st.cfg.caps);
   390	    el.seals.textContent = String(b.sealsLeft);
   620	    }
   621	
   622	    if (inFizz) {
   623	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(140,240,255,0.10)';
   624	      roundRect(x + pad, y + pad, t - pad * 2, t - pad * 2, 7); ctx.fill();
   625	    }
   626	  }
   627	
   628	  /* ---------------- update ---------------- */
   629	  function allSettled() {
   630	    var b = st.b;
   631	    for (var i = 0; i < b.g.length; i++) {
   632	      var cl = b.g[i];
   633	      if (!cl) continue;
   634	      if (Math.abs(cl.ry - cl.r) > 0.035 || Math.abs(cl.rx - cl.c) > 0.035) return false;
   635	    }
   636	    return true;
   637	  }
   638	
   639	  function update(dt, tm) {
   640	    if (!st) return;
   641	    var b = st.b, i;
   642	    var k = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 17);
   643	    for (i = 0; i < b.g.length; i++) {
   644	      var cl = b.g[i];
   645	      if (!cl) continue;
   646	      cl.rx += (cl.c - cl.rx) * k;
   647	      cl.ry += (cl.r - cl.ry) * k;
   648	      if (cl.born > 0) cl.born = Math.max(0, cl.born - dt * 5);
   649	      if (cl.dying) cl.dying += dt;
   650	    }
   651	
   652	    st.shake = Math.max(0, st.shake - dt * 40);
   653	    st.flash = Math.max(0, st.flash - dt * 2.2);
   654	    st.denyT = Math.max(0, st.denyT - dt);
   655	
   656	    /* particles */
   657	    for (i = parts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   658	      var p = parts[i];
   659	      p.l -= dt;
   660	      if (p.l <= 0) { parts.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   661	      p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vy += 420 * dt;
   662	    }
   663	    for (i = texts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   664	      texts[i].l -= dt; texts[i].y -= dt * 34;
   665	      if (texts[i].l <= 0) texts.splice(i, 1);
   666	    }
   667	
   668	    /* ambient fizz bubbles */
   669	    var g = geom();
   670	    if (bubbles.length < MAXB && Math.random() < dt * 55) {
   671	      var c = (Math.random() * COLS) | 0;
   672	      bubbles.push({ x: g.x + (c + 0.2 + Math.random() * 0.6) * g.t, y: g.y + g.h - Math.random() * 6, s: 1 + Math.random() * 3, l: 1.6, c: c });
   673	    }
   674	    for (i = bubbles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   675	      var bu = bubbles[i];
   676	      bu.y -= (26 + bu.s * 9) * dt;
   677	      bu.l -= dt * 0.5;
   678	      var lim = g.y + st.b.bnd[bu.c] * g.t;
   679	      if (bu.l <= 0 || bu.y < lim) bubbles.splice(i, 1);
   680	    }
   681	
   682	    /* phase machine */
   683	    if (st.over) return;
   684	    if (st.phase === 'idle') return;
   685	    st.timer -= dt;
   686	    if (st.phase === 'fall') {
   687	      if (allSettled() || st.timer <= 0) { snap(); step(); }
   688	    } else if (st.timer <= 0) {
   689	      step();
   690	    }
   691	  }
   692	
   693	  function snap() {
   694	    var b = st.b;
   695	    for (var i = 0; i < b.g.length; i++) { var cl = b.g[i]; if (cl) { cl.rx = cl.c; cl.ry = cl.r; } }
   696	  }
   697	
   698	  /* ---------------- loop ---------------- */
   699	  var last = 0, acc = 0;
   700	  function frame(ts) {
   701	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   702	    if (!last) last = ts;
   703	    var dt = (ts - last) / 1000;
   704	    last = ts;
   705	    if (dt > 0.05) dt = 0.05;          /* clamped delta */
   706	    if (dt < 0) dt = 0;
   707	    checkRotate();
   708	    if (!paused && !rotate) { acc += dt; update(dt, acc); }
   709	    render(acc);
   710	  }
   711	
   712	  /* ---------------- boot ---------------- */
   713	  function boot() {

   736

   743	    });
   744

   750	    });

   755	      var t = e.target;
   756	      while (t && t !== el.lvGrid && !t.getAttribute('data-lv')) t = t.parentNode;
   757	      if (!t || t === el.lvGrid) return;

   759	      if (!isFinite(n)) return;
   760	      hideOverlays(); startLevel(n, false);
   761	    });
   762	    $('endRetry').addEventListener('click', function () { restart(); });
   763	    $('endNext').addEventListener('click', function () {

   765	    });

   769	    checkRotate();
   770	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   771	  }
   772

   774	  else boot();
   775

    55	  function readSave() {
    56	    const blank = { shrineBits: 1, chestBits: 0, best: 0 };
    57	    try {
    58	      const raw = localStorage.getItem(SAVE_KEY);
    59	      if (typeof raw !== 'string' || raw.length > 2000) return blank;
    60	      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    61	      if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed)) return blank;
    62	      const shrineBits = Number.isInteger(parsed.shrineBits) ? (parsed.shrineBits & 15) | 1 : 1;
    63	      const chestBits = Number.isInteger(parsed.chestBits) ? (parsed.chestBits & 63) : 0;
    64	      const best = finite(parsed.best, 0, 0, 999999);
    65	      return { shrineBits, chestBits, best };
    66	    } catch (_) { return blank; }

## gridfall

MINOR/high-confidence gridfall/main.js:522,532 — `orientationchange` delays pause/overlay by 120ms, allowing simulation updates during rotation.

## scrapper-squad

MINOR/high-confidence scrapper-squad/js/game.js:571,579 — `pointercancel` uses pointer-up logic, so canceling a right-stick pointer can queue an unintended fire action.

MAJOR/high-confidence ridgeline-rumble/game.js:173,186 — The player is marked alive at 1 HP during respawn, and subsequent hits reset the five-second timer; sustained enemy fire can permanently prevent respawn.
